Journal: International Journal of Cancer  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-25  |  DOI: 10.1002/ijc.70849  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

中国18-45岁不同宫颈病变女性肛门生殖器部位HPV感染患病率及一致性

Prevalence and Concordance of HPV Infection at Anogenital Sites Among Chinese Women Aged 18–45 Years With Different Cervical Lesions
作者： Zhao‐feng Bi, Kai Chu, Zhong‐qiang Jin, Bin Zhang, Yan-Yun Hu, Fang-Hui Zhao, Wen‐Qian Li, Xin‐ye Gao, Kong‐xin Zhu, Guo‐hua Zhong
摘要中文翻译摘要 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 与多种癌症相关,但有关其 患病率 和肛门生殖器部位基因型分布的证据仍然有限。我们分析了 2020 年 9 月至 12 月在中国进行的九价 HPV 疫苗 III 期临床试验的基线数据。约 9327 名 18-45 岁的健康女性入组。收集宫颈、外阴和肛周拭子进行 HPV 基因分型。宫颈细胞学检查由病理学家进行评估。细胞学异常的女性接受阴道镜检查,如果有需要,则进行组织病理学 诊断 活检。在我们的研究中,大约 12.6% (1174/9327) 的参与者有宫颈细胞学异常,2.1% (194/9324) 被诊断为 CIN1+。在三个解剖部位中,HPV 患病率在外阴最高(22.43%),其次是肛周区域(17.65%)和子宫颈(14.15%)。多重 HPV 感染在非宫颈部位(外阴:33.17%;肛周:30.19%)比宫颈部位(24.94%)更常见。患有宫颈 HPV 感染的参与者更有可能并发外阴或肛周感染;HPV16在三部位感染中所占比例最高(47.26%)。此外,虽然整个试验人群中三个解剖部位的高危 HPV 类型的一致性中等,但细胞学异常的参与者的 kappa 值明显较高。本研究对 18-45 岁中国女性的 HPV 患病率 和多个肛门生殖部位的类型分布进行了全面评估,并作为评估 HPV 疫苗接种 的多部位影响的重要基线,并为未来的 宫颈癌 筛查和 监测 策略提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决 18-45 岁中国女性(尤其是患有宫颈病变的女性)中 HPV 患病率 和肛门生殖器部位基因型分布的有限证据。
设计与对象
九价HPV 疫苗的III期临床试验在中国进行,于2020年9月至12月期间招募了约9327名18-45岁的健康女性。
核心方法
对宫颈、外阴和肛周拭子进行 HPV 基因分型,并由病理学家对宫颈细胞学进行评估。细胞学异常的女性接受阴道镜检查和活检。
主要结果
HPV 患病率 在外阴最高(22.43%),其次是肛周区域(17.65%)和子宫颈(14.15%)。多重HPV感染在非宫颈部位更为常见,其中HPV16在三部位感染中所占比例最高(47.26%)。
解释与创新
该研究对中国女性多个肛门生殖部位的 HPV 患病率 和类型分布进行了全面评估,作为评估 HPV 疫苗接种 多部位影响的基线,并为未来的 宫颈癌 筛查和 监测 策略提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为中国多点HPV筛查项目的制定和改进宫颈癌预防策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到其观察性质和特定人群样本的限制,可能无法推广到其他人群。证据强度中等。

Prevalence and Concordance of HPV Infection at Anogenital Sites Among Chinese Women Aged 18–45 Years With Different Cervical Lesions

Authors: Zhao‐feng Bi, Kai Chu, Zhong‐qiang Jin, Bin Zhang, Yan-Yun Hu, Fang-Hui Zhao, Wen‐Qian Li, Xin‐ye Gao, Kong‐xin Zhu, Guo‐hua Zhong
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Human papillomavirus (HPV) is associated with multiple cancers, but evidence on its prevalence and genotype distribution across anogenital sites remains limited. We analyzed baseline data from a Phase III clinical trial of a nine‐valent HPV vaccine conducted in China, between September and December 2020. About 9327 healthy women aged 18–45 years were enrolled. Cervical, vulvar, and perianal swabs were collected for HPV genotyping. Cervical cytology was evaluated by pathologists. Women with abnormal cytology underwent colposcopy and, if indicated, biopsy for histopathological diagnosis. In our study, approximately 12.6% (1174/9327) of participants had abnormal cervical cytology, and 2.1% (194/9324) were diagnosed with CIN1+. Among the three anatomical sites, HPV prevalence was highest in the vulva (22.43%), followed by the perianal area (17.65%) and the cervix (14.15%). Multiple HPV infections were more common at noncervical sites (vulva: 33.17%; perianal: 30.19%) than at the cervix (24.94%). Participants with cervical HPV infection were more likely to have concurrent vulvar or perianal infection; HPV16 accounted for the highest proportion of three‐site infections (47.26%). Furthermore, while the consistency of high‐risk HPV types across the three anatomical sites was moderate in the overall trial population, the kappa values were markedly higher in participants with abnormal cytology. This study provides a comprehensive assessment of HPV prevalence and type distribution across multiple anogenital sites in Chinese women aged 18–45 years and serves as an essential baseline for evaluating the multi‐site impact of HPV vaccination and for informing future cervical cancer screening and surveillance strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limited evidence on HPV prevalence and genotype distribution across anogenital sites among Chinese women aged 18–45 years, particularly those with cervical lesions.
Design and population
A Phase III clinical trial of a nine-valent HPV vaccine was conducted in China, enrolling about 9327 healthy women aged 18–45 years between September and December 2020.
Core methods
HPV genotyping was performed on cervical, vulvar, and perianal swabs, and cervical cytology was evaluated by pathologists. Women with abnormal cytology underwent colposcopy and biopsy.
Main results
HPV prevalence was highest in the vulva (22.43%), followed by the perianal area (17.65%) and the cervix (14.15%). Multiple HPV infections were more common at noncervical sites, and HPV16 accounted for the highest proportion of three-site infections (47.26%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides a comprehensive assessment of HPV prevalence and type distribution across multiple anogenital sites in Chinese women, serving as a baseline for evaluating the multi-site impact of HPV vaccination and informing future cervical cancer screening and surveillance strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of multi-site HPV screening programs and improve cervical cancer prevention strategies in China.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational nature and the specific population sample, which may not be generalizable to other populations. The evidence strength is moderate.

香菇提取物 (AHCC®):通过临床证据增强 HPV 感染的清除

Shiitake Mushroom Extract (AHCC®): Enhancing Clearance of HPV Infection with Clinical Evidence
作者： Irene Stefanaki, Evdoxia Panou, Alexander Stratigos, Electra Nicolaidou
摘要中文翻译摘要 持续性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是多种肛门生殖器疾病的基础,包括生殖器疣、上皮内瘤变和 HPV 相关恶性肿瘤。尽管预防性 疫苗接种 非常有效,但它不能为已确定的感染提供治疗益处,这突出表明需要增强 宿主 抗病毒药物 免疫力的辅助策略。活性己糖相关化合物 (AHCC®) 是一种从香菇菌丝体中提取的标准化提取物,通过调节细胞因子信号传导以及激活自然杀伤细胞和 T 细胞反应,具有免疫调节和免疫增强作用。对持续 高危型HPV 感染的女性进行的试点临床研究和随后的随机临床研究表明,与安慰剂相比,AHCC® 的病毒清除率显着更高,同时免疫生物标志物正常化。在接受尖锐湿疣治疗的患者中,补充 AHCC® 与减少复发有关。尽管缺乏男性临床数据,但宫颈和阴茎 HPV 感染的共同免疫发病机制支持 AHCC® 在男性生殖器疾病(包括生殖器疣和阴茎上皮内瘤变)中的合理作用。因此,AHCC® 代表了一种治疗 HPV 相关皮肤病的有前途、安全的免疫调节辅助剂,值得在男性 队列研究 中进行进一步研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于预防性 疫苗接种 的局限性,该研究旨在解决在持续 HPV 感染中增强 宿主 抗病毒药物 免疫力的辅助策略的需求。
设计与对象
对持续 高危型HPV 感染的女性进行了一项随机临床研究,将 AHCC® 补充剂与安慰剂进行比较。
核心方法
通过调节细胞因子信号传导以及激活自然杀伤细胞和 T 细胞反应,评估了 AHCC® 的免疫调节和免疫增强作用。
主要结果
与安慰剂相比,AHCC® 补充剂可显着提高病毒清除率,同时免疫生物标志物正常化。
解释与创新
AHCC® 与减少尖锐湿疣复发有关,并且是治疗 HPV 相关皮肤病的一种有前途、安全的辅助药物,尽管缺乏男性临床数据。
科研与公卫意义
AHCC® 可以为治疗 HPV 相关皮肤病提供安全有效的辅助手段,支持对男性 队列研究 的进一步研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括缺乏男性临床数据以及需要对男性 队列研究 进行进一步研究,这限制了证据的强度。

Shiitake Mushroom Extract (AHCC®): Enhancing Clearance of HPV Infection with Clinical Evidence

Authors: Irene Stefanaki, Evdoxia Panou, Alexander Stratigos, Electra Nicolaidou
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Persistent human papillomavirus (HPV) infection underlies a wide spectrum of anogenital disease, including genital warts, intraepithelial neoplasia, and HPV-related malignancy. Although prophylactic vaccination is highly effective, it does not provide therapeutic benefit for established infection, highlighting the need for adjunctive strategies that enhance host antiviral immunity. Active Hexose Correlated Compound (AHCC®), a standardized extract derived from Lentinula edodes mycelia, has demonstrated immunomodulatory and immunoenhancing effects through regulation of cytokine signalling and activation of natural killer and T-cell responses. Pilot clinical studies and subsequent randomized clinical investigations in women with persistent high-risk HPV infection have shown significantly higher viral clearance rates with AHCC® compared with placebo, accompanied by normalization of immune biomarkers. In patients treated for condyloma acuminata, AHCC® supplementation has been associated with reduced recurrence. Although clinical data in men are lacking, the shared immunopathogenesis of cervical and penile HPV infection supports a plausible role for AHCC® in male genital disease, including genital warts and penile intraepithelial neoplasia. AHCC® therefore represents a promising, safe immunomodulatory adjunct in HPV-related dermatoses that warrants further investigation in male cohorts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for adjunctive strategies to enhance host antiviral immunity in persistent HPV infection, given the limitations of prophylactic vaccination.
Design and population
A randomized clinical investigation was conducted in women with persistent high-risk HPV infection, comparing AHCC® supplementation to placebo.
Core methods
AHCC® was evaluated for its immunomodulatory and immunoenhancing effects through regulation of cytokine signalling and activation of natural killer and T-cell responses.
Main results
AHCC® supplementation resulted in significantly higher viral clearance rates compared to placebo, accompanied by normalization of immune biomarkers.
Interpretation and novelty
AHCC® has been associated with reduced recurrence of condyloma acuminata and represents a promising, safe adjunct in HPV-related dermatoses, though clinical data in men are lacking.
Scientific and public-health significance
AHCC® may offer a safe and effective adjunct in managing HPV-related dermatoses, supporting further investigation in male cohorts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the lack of clinical data in men and the need for further investigation in male cohorts, which limits the strength of the evidence.

维生素 D 激素调节 HPV16 阳性 SiHa 宫颈癌 细胞的氧化应激和细胞凋亡

Vitamin D Hormones Modulate Oxidative Stress and Apoptosis in HPV16‐Positive SiHa Cervical Cancer Cells
作者： Hoi Ching Cherry Chan, Sachin Bhoora, Esther Zhou, Sumari Marais, Tahir S. Pillay, Rivak Punchoo
摘要中文翻译摘要 宫颈癌 是全球女性中第四大最常见的恶性肿瘤,对资源有限国家的女性影响尤为严重。治疗 结果仍然受到肿瘤耐药性和延迟 诊断 的限制,通常导致晚期疾病。本研究调查了维生素 D 诱导的氧化应激、氧化信号通路的扰动以及 SiHa 宫颈癌 细胞的细胞凋亡。用生理和超生理浓度的 25-羟基维生素 D 3 [25(OH)D 3 ] 和卡泊三醇(一种合成维生素 D 受体激动剂)处理细胞。在生理浓度下,治疗 均支持细胞存活。超生理学 25(OH)D 3 诱导适度的线粒体应激,与早期到中期的细胞凋亡变化一致。相反,超生理性卡泊三醇诱导明显的氧化应激和线粒体去极化,降低细胞抗氧化能力,并促进细胞凋亡的生化和形态学特征。这些效应伴随着 MAPK、AKT 和 NF-κB 信号通路内相对蛋白表达的显着下调,与晚期细胞凋亡的进展一致。总的来说,这些发现证明了维生素 D 相关化合物在 宫颈癌 细胞中具有剂量依赖性的促氧化和促凋亡作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究维生素 D 激素对 HPV16 阳性 SiHa 宫颈癌 细胞氧化应激和细胞凋亡的影响,鉴于当前 治疗 的局限性,解决对新治疗策略的需求。
设计与对象
该研究使用生理和超生理浓度的 25-羟基维生素 D3 和卡泊三醇处理 SiHa 宫颈癌 细胞,以检查对氧化应激和细胞凋亡的影响。
核心方法
用不同浓度的维生素 D 相关化合物处理细胞,以评估它们对氧化应激、细胞凋亡和信号通路的影响。
主要结果
在生理浓度下,治疗 均支持细胞存活。超生理性25(OH)D3诱导适度的线粒体应激,而超生理性卡泊三醇诱导明显的氧化应激、线粒体去极化、降低细胞抗氧化能力并促进细胞凋亡。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,维生素 D 相关化合物在 宫颈癌 细胞中具有剂量依赖性促氧化和促凋亡作用,为潜在的治疗策略提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为通过调节氧化应激和细胞凋亡途径来开发 HPV16 相关 宫颈癌 的靶向疗法提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用体外细胞培养物,可能无法完全代表体内条件,以及关注单一 HPV16 阳性细胞系,限制了普遍性。

Vitamin D Hormones Modulate Oxidative Stress and Apoptosis in HPV16‐Positive SiHa Cervical Cancer Cells

Authors: Hoi Ching Cherry Chan, Sachin Bhoora, Esther Zhou, Sumari Marais, Tahir S. Pillay, Rivak Punchoo
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Cervical cancer is the fourth most prevalent malignancy among women worldwide and disproportionately affects women in resource‐constrained countries. Treatment outcomes remain limited by tumour resistance and delayed diagnosis, often resulting in advanced disease. This study investigated vitamin D‐induced oxidative stress, perturbation of oxidative signalling pathways, and apoptosis in SiHa cervical cancer cells. Cells were treated with physiological and supraphysiological concentrations of 25‐hydroxyvitamin D 3 [25(OH)D 3 ] and calcipotriol, a synthetic vitamin D receptor agonist. At physiological concentrations, both treatments supported cell survival. Supraphysiological 25(OH)D 3 induced modest mitochondrial stress, consistent with early‐to‐intermediate apoptotic changes. In contrast, supraphysiological calcipotriol induced pronounced oxidative stress and mitochondrial depolarisation, reduced cellular antioxidant capacity, and promoted biochemical and morphological features of apoptosis. These effects were accompanied by significant downregulation of relative protein expression within the MAPK, AKT and NF‐κB signalling pathways, consistent with progression towards late apoptosis. Collectively, these findings demonstrate dose‐dependent pro‐oxidative and pro‐apoptotic effects of vitamin D‐related compounds in cervical cancer cells.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the effects of vitamin D hormones on oxidative stress and apoptosis in HPV16-positive SiHa cervical cancer cells, addressing the need for new therapeutic strategies given the limitations of current treatments.
Design and population
The study used SiHa cervical cancer cells treated with physiological and supraphysiological concentrations of 25-hydroxyvitamin D3 and calcipotriol to examine the effects on oxidative stress and apoptosis.
Core methods
Cells were treated with vitamin D-related compounds at different concentrations to assess their impact on oxidative stress, apoptosis, and signaling pathways.
Main results
At physiological concentrations, both treatments supported cell survival. Supraphysiological 25(OH)D3 induced modest mitochondrial stress, while supraphysiological calcipotriol induced pronounced oxidative stress, mitochondrial depolarization, reduced cellular antioxidant capacity, and promoted apoptosis.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate dose-dependent pro-oxidative and pro-apoptotic effects of vitamin D-related compounds in cervical cancer cells, providing new insights into potential therapeutic strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results could inform the development of targeted therapies for HPV16-associated cervical cancer by modulating oxidative stress and apoptosis pathways.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of in vitro cell cultures, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions, and the focus on a single HPV16-positive cell line, limiting generalizability.

纵向病毒载量轨迹可预测持续 人乳头瘤病毒 感染女性的宫颈病变风险:一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究

Longitudinal viral load trajectories predict cervical lesion risk in women with persistent human papillomavirus infection: A prospective cohort study
作者： Yi-Lu Zhou, Shuai Chen, Ying Zhang, Yu Jiang, Mo-Han Xiao, Xian-Zhen Xin, Zhong-Hua Shi, Li-Na Zhang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解纵向病毒载量轨迹在预测持续性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染女性宫颈病变风险中的作用,解决单一病毒载量测量可能无法捕获异质病毒动态的局限性。
设计与对象
一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究在 1502 名 HPV 阳性女性中进行,其中 624 名女性患有特定类型的持续感染,每隔大约 6 个月进行一次随访。
核心方法
潜在类别增长分析用于确定持续感染女性中的病毒载量轨迹,并使用多变量逻辑回归和 Firth 惩罚回归评估与宫颈病变的关联。
主要结果
Four viral load trajectories were identified: low stable (39.6%), moderate declining (18.9%), moderate increasing (29.8%), and persistently high (11.7%). Any-lesion prevalence was 10.9%, 17.8%, 74.2%, and 90.4%, respectively (p < .001). The persistently high group was strongly associated with cervical lesions, with fully adjusted odds ratios of 124.29 (95% CI, 47.30–326.60) compared to the low-stable group.
解释与创新
该研究得出的结论是,纵向 HPV 病毒载量动态确定了临床上不同的风险表型,其中中度增加和持续高的轨迹与宫颈病变密切相关,从而改善了基线测量之外的辨别力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,纵向病毒载量信息可以改善宫颈病变风险的预测,从而为 监测、诊断 和 治疗 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括在临床实施之前需要进行外部验证,以及依赖具有特定纳入标准的前瞻性 队列研究。

Longitudinal viral load trajectories predict cervical lesion risk in women with persistent human papillomavirus infection: A prospective cohort study

Authors: Yi-Lu Zhou, Shuai Chen, Ying Zhang, Yu Jiang, Mo-Han Xiao, Xian-Zhen Xin, Zhong-Hua Shi, Li-Na Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Persistent human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is central to cervical lesion development, but single viral‐load measurements may not capture heterogeneous viral dynamics. This study characterized longitudinal viral‐load trajectories among women with persistent infection and evaluated their associations with cervical lesions and incremental predictive value beyond baseline factors. Methods In this prospective cohort of 1502 HPV‐positive women followed at approximately 6‐month intervals, latent class growth analysis using actual follow‐up time was conducted among 624 women with type‐specific persistent infection. Associations between trajectory group and any cervical lesion (low‐grade squamous intraepithelial lesion or high‐grade squamous intraepithelial lesion [HSIL]) were assessed using multivariable logistic regression; HSIL was explored using Firth‐penalized regression. Discrimination, calibration, and decision‐curve analysis were used to assess incremental predictive performance. Results Of 1170 women with classifiable outcomes, 624 had persistent infection. Four trajectories were identified: low stable (39.6%), moderate declining (18.9%), moderate increasing (29.8%), and persistently high (11.7%). Any‐lesion prevalence was 10.9%, 17.8%, 74.2%, and 90.4%, respectively (p < .001). Compared with the low‐stable group, fully adjusted odds ratios (ORs) were 1.95 (95% CI, 1.02–3.74), 29.66 (95% CI, 16.75–52.51), and 124.29 (95% CI, 47.30–326.60) for the other groups, respectively. The persistently high group was also associated with HSIL (OR, 9.47; 95% CI, 3.18–33.09). The optimism‐corrected area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) increased from 0.713 for the base model to 0.888 for both the simple longitudinal and trajectory models and 0.899 for the joint model. Conclusions Longitudinal HPV viral‐load dynamics identified clinically distinct risk phenotypes. Moderate‐increasing and persistently high trajectories were strongly associated with cervical lesions, whereas longitudinal information improved discrimination beyond baseline measurements. External validation is required before clinical implementation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the role of longitudinal viral load trajectories in predicting cervical lesion risk among women with persistent human papillomavirus (HPV) infection, addressing the limitation that single viral load measurements may not capture heterogeneous viral dynamics.
Design and population
A prospective cohort study was conducted among 1502 HPV-positive women, with 624 having type-specific persistent infection, followed at approximately 6-month intervals.
Core methods
Latent class growth analysis was used to identify viral load trajectories among women with persistent infection, and associations with cervical lesions were assessed using multivariable logistic regression and Firth-penalized regression.
Main results
Four viral load trajectories were identified: low stable (39.6%), moderate declining (18.9%), moderate increasing (29.8%), and persistently high (11.7%). Any-lesion prevalence was 10.9%, 17.8%, 74.2%, and 90.4%, respectively (p < .001). The persistently high group was strongly associated with cervical lesions, with fully adjusted odds ratios of 124.29 (95% CI, 47.30–326.60) compared to the low-stable group.
Interpretation and novelty
The study concluded that longitudinal HPV viral load dynamics identified clinically distinct risk phenotypes, with moderate-increasing and persistently high trajectories strongly associated with cervical lesions, improving discrimination beyond baseline measurements.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that longitudinal viral load information can improve the prediction of cervical lesion risk, which could inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the need for external validation before clinical implementation, and the reliance on a prospective cohort with specific inclusion criteria.

1999 年至 2024 年英国的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染、疫苗接种 和宫颈筛查:来自全国性态度和生活方式重复横断面调查 (Natsal) 的结果。

Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection, vaccination, and cervical screening in Britain from 1999 to 2024: findings from the repeated cross-sectional National Surveys of Sexual Attitudes and Lifestyles (Natsal).
作者： Emily Trickett, Kate Soldan, Simon Beddows, Claudia Gascoyne, Kavita Panwar, Andrew Copas, Elise Paul, Jo Gibbs, Clare Tanton, Amy Hough
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在量化 HPV 疫苗接种 对 HPV-16/18 患病率 的影响以及英国对 宫颈癌 预防干预措施的采用,以满足公平获取这些干预措施的需要。
设计与对象
该研究使用了1999-2001年、2010-12年和2022-24年进行的三项全国性态度和生活方式调查(Natsal)的数据,这些调查是具有全国代表性的横断面人口调查,包含个人数据和生物样本的HPV检测。
核心方法
该研究分析了 Natsal-2、Natsal-3 和 Natsal-4 的数据,其中包括尿液生物样本,以及 Natsal-4 中的阴道拭子和尿液生物样本,以估计 HPV-16/18 感染的年龄调整 患病率 比率,并评估与 HPV 疫苗接种 和宫颈筛查相关的因素。
主要结果
研究发现,与 Natsal-2 相比,Natsal-4 中 18-29 岁女性的 HPV-16/18 患病率 感染率降低了 86%,Natsal-4 中的所有 HPV-16/18 感染均发生在未接种疫苗的女性中。在符合常规 疫苗接种 资格的女性中,23.0% 的女性报告未接种疫苗,而符合宫颈筛查资格的女性中有 5.3% 的女性从未参加过筛查。
解释与创新
该研究解释说,英国的 HPV 疫苗接种 计划显着减少了 HPV-16/18 患病率,但少数族裔、宗教团体和英国境外出生的人中不接受 疫苗接种 和筛查的比例仍然较高,这凸显了扩大预防干预措施范围的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,持续的 HPV 疫苗接种 规划可以实现世卫组织 宫颈癌 消除目标,但需要努力确保所有人群亚组的公平获取和吸收。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括依赖 疫苗接种 和筛选吸收的自我报告数据、非概率样本中的潜在偏差以及需要进一步研究以了解 疫苗接种 计划的长期影响。

Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection, vaccination, and cervical screening in Britain from 1999 to 2024: findings from the repeated cross-sectional National Surveys of Sexual Attitudes and Lifestyles (Natsal).

Authors: Emily Trickett, Kate Soldan, Simon Beddows, Claudia Gascoyne, Kavita Panwar, Andrew Copas, Elise Paul, Jo Gibbs, Clare Tanton, Amy Hough
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination and cervical screening have substantially reduced overall cervical cancer risk, but differences in uptake must be quantified to inform equitable access. This study aimed to provide population-level evidence of the impact of HPV vaccination on HPV types 16 and 18 (hereafter HPV-16/18) prevalence in England, Scotland, and Wales (hereafter Britain), and uptake of cervical cancer prevention interventions (ie, vaccination and screening), 15 years after the introduction of the vaccination programme in 2008. METHODS: We conducted the National Surveys of Sexual Attitudes and Lifestyles (Natsal), which are nationally representative, cross-sectional population surveys with individual-level data and HPV testing of biosamples. Data from Natsal-2 (1999-2001; before the vaccination programme was introduced, and included urine biosamples), Natsal-3 (2010-12; early in the vaccination programme, and included urine biosamples), and Natsal-4 (2022-24; after the vaccination programme was established, and included vaginal swab and urine biosamples) were analysed. Age-adjusted prevalence ratios (aPRs) for HPV-16/18 infection were estimated among women eligible for routine vaccination (born from Sept 1, 1995) compared with same-aged women before the vaccination programme was introduced. Combined Natsal-4 probability and non-probability data assessed factors associated with self-reported HPV vaccination and cervical screening. FINDINGS: Among 10 418 Natsal-4 women (aged 16-59 years), 1059 women in the probability sample group (aged 18-44 years) provided usable biosamples (Natsal-2 n=1849; Natsal-3 n=2407). HPV-16/18 prevalence was 86% lower (aPR 0·14, 95% CI 0·02-0·84) among those aged 18-29 years in Natsal-4 (1·6%, 95% CI 0·3-8·2) than in Natsal-2 (8·7%, 95% CI 6·7-11·3). All HPV-16/18 infections reported in Natsal-4 occurred in unvaccinated women. Of 733 routine vaccination-eligible women, 23·0% (95% CI 19·4-27·0) reported being unvaccinated. Among 3530 women (aged 25-59 years) eligible for cervical screening, 5·3% (95% CI 4·4-6·3) reported never attending screening. Non-uptake of vaccination and screening was higher among minority ethnic groups, religious groups, and those born outside the UK. Prevalence of high-risk, non-vaccine-preventable HPV (HPV 35, 39, 51, 56, and 59) infections was 4·9% (95% CI 3·7-6·4) in the total population, without any statistically significant differences between these subgroups. Women with higher-risk sexual behaviours were more likely to both be vaccinated and attend for screening. INTERPRETATION: Britain's HPV vaccination programme has driven a dramatic decline in HPV-16/18 prevalence and WHO cervical cancer elimination targets might be achievable overall if this is sustained. However, women from minority ethnic groups, religious groups, and those not born in the UK are less likely to be vaccinated and screened while remaining at risk. It is crucial to improve the reach of prevention interventions to more equitably reduce cervical cancer morbidity and mortality. FUNDING: Wellcome Trust, Economic and Social Research Council, and the National Institute for Health Research.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to quantify the impact of HPV vaccination on HPV-16/18 prevalence and uptake of cervical cancer prevention interventions in Britain, addressing the need to inform equitable access to these interventions.
Design and population
The study used data from three National Surveys of Sexual Attitudes and Lifestyles (Natsal) conducted in 1999-2001, 2010-12, and 2022-24, which are nationally representative, cross-sectional population surveys with individual-level data and HPV testing of biosamples.
Core methods
The study analysed data from Natsal-2, Natsal-3, and Natsal-4, which included urine biosamples and, in Natsal-4, vaginal swab and urine biosamples, to estimate age-adjusted prevalence ratios for HPV-16/18 infection and assess factors associated with HPV vaccination and cervical screening.
Main results
The study found that HPV-16/18 prevalence was 86% lower among women aged 18-29 in Natsal-4 compared to Natsal-2, with all HPV-16/18 infections in Natsal-4 occurring in unvaccinated women. Among routine vaccination-eligible women, 23.0% reported being unvaccinated, and 5.3% of women eligible for cervical screening reported never attending screening.
Interpretation and novelty
The study interpreted that Britain's HPV vaccination programme has significantly reduced HPV-16/18 prevalence, but non-uptake of vaccination and screening remains higher among minority ethnic groups, religious groups, and those born outside the UK, highlighting the need for improved reach of prevention interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that sustained HPV vaccination programmes could achieve WHO cervical cancer elimination targets, but efforts are needed to ensure equitable access and uptake among all population subgroups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the reliance on self-reported data for vaccination and screening uptake, potential biases in the non-probability sample, and the need for further research to understand the long-term impact of the vaccination programme.

宫颈癌风险降低在人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种时代的影响。

Cervical cancer risk reduction in the era of human papillomavirus vaccination.
作者： Sarah A Phillips, Priti Bandi, Jessica Star, Sarah M Temkin, Robert A Smith, Rebecca Landy
摘要中文翻译背景:在评估宫颈筛查对 宫颈癌 风险的人群水平影响时,需要考虑 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 状态,但 疫苗接种 状态很少显示筛查率,这限制了对 宫颈癌 人群水平保护的评估。方法:作者使用 2019 年全国健康访谈调查 (N = 4000) 中的 21-39 岁女性,报告了按年龄和自我报告的 HPV 疫苗接种 状态分层的最新宫颈筛查比例。假设 HPV 疫苗接种 和最新筛查状态导致的 宫颈癌 风险分别降低 85% 和 70%,作者探索了不同 HPV 疫苗接种 状态筛查覆盖率带来的人群水平 宫颈癌 风险降低情况。结果:共有 78.4% 的女性接受了最新的筛查,38.0% 的女性报告曾接受过 HPV 疫苗,15.3% 的女性未接种疫苗且未接受最新的筛查。在各个年龄组中,接受最新筛查的女性比例,接种疫苗的女性比未接种疫苗的女性高出 6.3%-19.1%。与不进行 疫苗接种 和筛查相比,疫苗接种 和筛查的 宫颈癌 风险相对降低范围为 36-39 岁女性的 62.1% 到 26-30 岁女性的 73.1%。尽管 21-25 岁女性的筛查率最低,但由于 疫苗接种 水平较高,她们的 宫颈癌 风险降低并不是最低的。结论:接种 HPV 疫苗的女性的宫颈筛查率较高,导致 宫颈癌 的人群水平风险高于独立考虑 疫苗接种 和筛查率的预期。将 疫苗接种 状态纳入国家 监测 数据集中将有助于识别和针对高风险社区内的干预措施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 HPV 疫苗接种 状态对宫颈筛查的影响和 宫颈癌 风险的影响,解决在评估人群层面针对 宫颈癌 的保护时考虑 疫苗接种 状态的需要。
设计与对象
该研究使用了 2019 年全国健康访谈调查的数据,涉及 4000 名 21-39 岁的女性,按年龄和 HPV 疫苗接种 状态对宫颈筛查的进行情况进行分层。
核心方法
作者分析了 21-39 岁女性自我报告的 HPV 疫苗接种 状态和宫颈筛查情况,假设 HPV 疫苗接种 和最新筛查使 宫颈癌 风险分别降低 85% 和 70%。
主要结果
在接受调查的女性中,78.4% 的女性及时进行了筛查,38.0% 的女性报告曾接受过 HPV 疫苗,15.3% 的女性未接种疫苗且未及时进行筛查。在各个年龄组中,与未接种疫苗的女性相比,接种疫苗的女性具有更高的筛查率,并且 宫颈癌 风险的相对降低因 疫苗接种 和筛查而异。
解释与创新
研究发现,尽管年轻女性的筛查率较低,但由于 疫苗接种 水平较高,她们的 宫颈癌 风险降低并不是最低的,这凸显了在国家 监测 数据中考虑 疫苗接种 状态的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将 疫苗接种 状态纳入国家 监测 数据集中可以帮助识别和针对高风险社区内的干预措施,从而提高人群对 宫颈癌 的保护。
局限与证据强度
该研究对自我报告数据的依赖以及缺乏 HPV 感染或 宫颈癌 发病率 的直接证据限制了证据的强度,应谨慎解释研究结果。

Cervical cancer risk reduction in the era of human papillomavirus vaccination.

Authors: Sarah A Phillips, Priti Bandi, Jessica Star, Sarah M Temkin, Robert A Smith, Rebecca Landy
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination status is important to consider when evaluating the population-level impact of cervical screening uptake on cervical cancer risk, yet screening rates are rarely presented by vaccination status, limiting assessments of population-level protection against cervical cancer. METHODS: Using females 21-39 years old from the 2019 National Health Interview Survey (N = 4000), the authors report the proportion up-to-date with cervical screening stratified by age and self-reported HPV vaccination status. Using assumptions of 85% and 70% reduction in cervical cancer risk from HPV vaccination and up-to-date screening status, respectively, the authors explore the population-level reduction in cervical cancer risk from varying screening coverage by HPV vaccination status. RESULTS: A total of 78.4% of females were up-to-date with screening, 38.0% reported ever receiving an HPV vaccine, and 15.3% were both unvaccinated and not up-to-date with screening. Across age groups, the proportion of females up-to-date with screening was 6.3%-19.1% higher for vaccinated versus unvaccinated females. The relative reduction in cervical cancer risk from vaccination and screening ranged from 62.1% for females 36-39 years old to 73.1% for females 26-30 years old, compared to the absence of vaccination and screening. Despite females 21-25 years old having the lowest screening uptake, their reduction in cervical cancer risk was not the lowest due to higher vaccination levels. CONCLUSIONS: Cervical screening rates are higher among females who were vaccinated against HPV, resulting in a higher population-level risk of cervical cancer than expected from considering vaccination and screening rates independently. Including vaccination status in national surveillance data sets would help identify and target interventions within communities at higher risk.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the impact of HPV vaccination status on cervical screening uptake and cervical cancer risk, addressing the need to consider vaccination status in assessing population-level protection against cervical cancer.
Design and population
The study used data from the 2019 National Health Interview Survey, involving 4000 females aged 21-39, to stratify cervical screening uptake by age and HPV vaccination status.
Core methods
The authors analyzed self-reported HPV vaccination status and cervical screening uptake among females aged 21-39, using assumptions of 85% and 70% reduction in cervical cancer risk from HPV vaccination and up-to-date screening, respectively.
Main results
Of the surveyed females, 78.4% were up-to-date with screening, 38.0% reported ever receiving an HPV vaccine, and 15.3% were both unvaccinated and not up-to-date with screening. Across age groups, vaccinated females had higher screening rates compared to unvaccinated females, and the relative reduction in cervical cancer risk from vaccination and screening varied by age group.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that despite lower screening rates among younger females, their cervical cancer risk reduction was not the lowest due to higher vaccination levels, highlighting the importance of considering vaccination status in national surveillance data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that including vaccination status in national surveillance data sets could help identify and target interventions within communities at higher risk, thereby improving population-level protection against cervical cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's reliance on self-reported data and the absence of direct evidence of HPV infection or cervical cancer incidence limit the strength of the evidence, and the findings should be interpreted cautiously.

RB1 和 PTPN14 肿瘤抑制因子的失活协同作用使 人乳头瘤病毒 E7 癌蛋白的寿命延长

Inactivation of the RB1 and PTPN14 tumor suppressors cooperatively enables the lifespan extension activity of the human papillomavirus E7 oncoprotein
作者： Pushkal Sinduvadi Ramesh, Angelo A. Nicolaci, Leah E. Graham, Joangela Nouel, Kevin Xu, Jennifer M. Binning, Karl Münger, Elizabeth A. White, Karl Munger
摘要中文翻译高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) E7 蛋白结合并灭活 宿主 细胞肿瘤抑制因子,对于原代人类角质形成细胞的永生化至关重要。来自高危和低危 HPV 基因型的 E7 蛋白直接与至少两种肿瘤抑制因子 RB1 和 PTPN14 结合,并使两者失活。我们之前对 高危型HPV E7 蛋白中的 突变 进行了表征,该蛋白选择性地消除了 E7 结合 RB1 或 PTPN14 的能力。在这里,我们利用与 RB1 或 PTPN14 结合缺陷的 E7 突变体建立了遗传互补系统。单独的突变体都不能延长原代角质形成细胞的寿命。当一起表达时,突变体可以像野生型 高危型HPV E7 一样延长角质形成细胞的寿命。高风险和低风险E7均降低PTPN14蛋白水平并降低角质形成细胞分化基因的表达,而只有高风险E7降低稳态RB1水平并诱导E2F依赖性基因。RB1 或 PTPN14 的缺失可以与低风险 HPV6 E7 配合,延长角质形成细胞的寿命。我们的研究结果推进了这样的模型:高危型HPV E7 的寿命延长活性需要至少两种肿瘤抑制因子的失活。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 高危型HPV E7 蛋白对 RB1 和 PTPN14 的协同失活,这对于原代人类角质形成细胞的永生化至关重要。该研究弥补了理解这些蛋白质如何实现 E7 寿命延长活性的空白。
设计与对象
该研究使用了一种基因互补系统,其中 E7 突变体无法与原代人角质形成细胞中表达的 RB1 或 PTPN14 结合。
核心方法
建立 E7 突变体并在角质形成细胞中表达,以评估其延长细胞寿命的能力。使用测定法测量 RB1 和 PTPN14 的失活以及对角质形成细胞分化基因的影响。
主要结果
单独的突变体都不能延长角质形成细胞的寿命,但当一起表达时,它们协同延长了原代角质形成细胞的寿命。高危型HPV E7降低RB1水平并诱导E2F依赖性基因,而低危HPV6 E7降低PTPN14蛋白水平和角质形成细胞分化基因表达。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,高危型HPV E7 的寿命延长活性需要 RB1 和 PTPN14 失活,从而推进了 E7 功能模型。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 HPV E7 作用机制的深入了解,并可以为通过针对这些途径预防 HPV 相关癌症的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到遗传互补系统的使用和对原代角质形成细胞的关注的限制,这可能无法完全代表体内条件。证据强度中等。

Inactivation of the RB1 and PTPN14 tumor suppressors cooperatively enables the lifespan extension activity of the human papillomavirus E7 oncoprotein

Authors: Pushkal Sinduvadi Ramesh, Angelo A. Nicolaci, Leah E. Graham, Joangela Nouel, Kevin Xu, Jennifer M. Binning, Karl Münger, Elizabeth A. White, Karl Munger
Original abstract (English)
High-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) E7 proteins bind and inactivate host cellular tumor suppressors and are essential for the immortalization of primary human keratinocytes. E7 proteins from high- and low-risk HPV genotypes bind directly to at least two tumor suppressors, RB1 and PTPN14, and inactivate both. We previously characterized mutations in high-risk HPV E7 proteins that selectively abrogate the ability of E7 to bind either RB1 or PTPN14. Here, we established a genetic complementation system using the E7 mutants defective for binding to RB1 or PTPN14. Neither mutant alone could extend the lifespan of primary keratinocytes. When expressed together, the mutants could, like wild-type high-risk HPV E7, extend keratinocyte lifespan. Both high- and low-risk E7 reduced PTPN14 protein levels and reduced expression of keratinocyte differentiation genes, whereas only high-risk E7 reduced steady-state RB1 levels and induced E2F-dependent genes. Depletion of either RB1 or PTPN14 could cooperate with low-risk HPV6 E7 to extend keratinocyte lifespan. Our findings advance the model that inactivation of at least two tumor suppressors is required for the lifespan extension activity of high-risk HPV E7.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the cooperative inactivation of RB1 and PTPN14 by high-risk HPV E7 proteins, which are essential for the immortalization of primary human keratinocytes. The research addressed the gap in understanding how these proteins enable the lifespan extension activity of E7.
Design and population
The study used a genetic complementation system with E7 mutants defective for binding to RB1 or PTPN14, expressed in primary human keratinocytes.
Core methods
E7 mutants were established and expressed in keratinocytes to assess their ability to extend cell lifespan. Assays were used to measure the inactivation of RB1 and PTPN14, and the effects on keratinocyte differentiation genes.
Main results
Neither mutant alone could extend keratinocyte lifespan, but when expressed together, they cooperatively extended the lifespan of primary keratinocytes. High-risk HPV E7 reduced RB1 levels and induced E2F-dependent genes, while low-risk HPV6 E7 reduced PTPN14 protein levels and keratinocyte differentiation gene expression.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that inactivation of both RB1 and PTPN14 is required for the lifespan extension activity of high-risk HPV E7, advancing the model of E7 function.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the mechanisms of HPV E7 action and could inform strategies for preventing HPV-associated cancers by targeting these pathways.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of a genetic complementation system and the focus on primary keratinocytes, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. The evidence strength is moderate.

30 例外阴鳞状细胞癌中的 TP53 测序和 高危型HPV 状态,具有罕见的 p16 和 p53 表达模式组合。

TP53 sequencing and high-risk HPV status in 30 vulvar squamous cell carcinomas with rare combinations of p16 and p53 expression patterns.
作者： Louise Baandrup, Malene G Madsen, Anja E Sørensen, Emma L Kaderly Rasmussen, Susanne K Kjær
摘要中文翻译大多数外阴鳞状细胞癌 (VSCC) 使用 p16 和 p53 免疫组织化学分类为所谓的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关肿瘤(p16 阳性)或 HPV 独立肿瘤(通常为 p16 阴性),具有野生型或突变型 p53 表达。然而,一个罕见的 VSCC 亚群表现出不寻常的 p16/p53 染色模式,这对它们的分类提出了挑战。在此,我们报告了 30 个此类肿瘤,据我们所知,这是迄今为止最大的系列。使用六模式框架评估 p53 免疫染色,包括四种突变模式(基底过度表达、副基底/弥漫性过度表达、细胞质和缺失)和两种野生型模式(分散和中上皮)。所有病例均接受了 TP53 新一代测序和 高危型HPV (hrHPV) DNA PCR 检测。最大的亚组由显示 p16 阳性和 p53 过度表达的肿瘤组成 (n=20)。其中,18 个携带 TP53 突变,而 7 个肿瘤中检测到 hrHPV DNA,其中包括两个 TP53 野生型肿瘤。6 个肿瘤显示 p16 阳性,但缺乏 p53 染色;所有患者均携带 TP53 突变,其中两人的 hrHPV DNA 也呈阳性。三个肿瘤显示 p16 阳性,仅限于细胞核,且 p53 过度表达;所有病例均携带 TP53 突变,其中 1 例为 hrHPV 阳性。最后一个病例是 p16 阴性,具有中上皮 p53 染色模式,已知与 HPV 相关肿瘤密切相关。该肿瘤为 TP53 野生型且 hrHPV33 阳性。我们的研究表明,并非所有 VSCC 都可以通过 p16 和 p53 免疫组织化学轻松分类,并证实仅 p16 阳性不足以定义 HPV 相关性。大多数肿瘤同时表现出 p16 阳性(其中三个仅核染色)和 p53 突变模式,分子分析主要支持 TP53 驱动的致癌作用,其中 93% (27/29) 检测到 TP53 突变。然而,在 10 例病例中也检测到了 hrHPV DNA,这表明生物学更为复杂。因此,这种罕见的 VSCC 子集可以根据其免疫表型和分子特征进行更好的分类,而不是强行归入当前的分类。需要进一步的预后相关性研究来阐明其生物学和临床意义。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
大多数外阴鳞状细胞癌 (VSCC) 使用 p16 和 p53 免疫组织化学分类为 HPV 相关或 HPV 独立肿瘤,但少数亚群表现出不寻常的 p16/p53 染色模式,这对它们的分类提出了挑战。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 30 种外阴鳞状细胞癌,其中最大的亚组显示 p16 阳性和 p53 过度表达 (n=20)。最后一个病例是 p16 阴性,具有中上皮 p53 染色模式,已知与 HPV 相关肿瘤密切相关。
核心方法
所有病例均接受了 TP53 新一代测序和 高危型HPV (hrHPV) DNA PCR 检测,以评估免疫表型和分子特征。
主要结果
在 20 个显示 p16 阳性和 p53 过表达的肿瘤中,18 个含有 TP53 突变,而 7 个肿瘤中检测到 hrHPV DNA,其中包括两个 TP53 野生型肿瘤。6 个肿瘤显示 p16 阳性,但缺乏 p53 染色;所有患者均携带 TP53 突变,其中两人的 hrHPV DNA 也呈阳性。三个肿瘤显示 p16 阳性,仅限于细胞核,且 p53 过度表达;所有病例均携带 TP53 突变,其中 1 例为 hrHPV 阳性。最后一个病例为 p16 阴性,具有中上皮 p53 染色模式、TP53 野生型和 hrHPV33 阳性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,罕见的 VSCC 子集可能会根据其免疫表型和分子特征进行更好的分类,而不是被迫进入当前的分类,因为分子分析主要支持 TP53 驱动的致癌作用,在 93% (27/29) 的病例中检测到 TP53 突变。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,目前基于 p16 和 p53 免疫组织化学的分类可能不足以适用于所有 VSCC,需要进行分子分析以更好地了解其生物学和临床意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量相对较小和缺乏预后相关数据的限制,但证据很充分,因为它代表了迄今为止此类肿瘤的最大系列,并包括详细的分子分析。

TP53 sequencing and high-risk HPV status in 30 vulvar squamous cell carcinomas with rare combinations of p16 and p53 expression patterns.

Authors: Louise Baandrup, Malene G Madsen, Anja E Sørensen, Emma L Kaderly Rasmussen, Susanne K Kjær
Original abstract (English)
Most vulvar squamous cell carcinomas (VSCCs) are classified using p16 and p53 immunohistochemistry into so-called human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated (p16-positive) or HPV-independent tumors (typically p16-negative) with wild-type or mutant p53 expression. However, a rare subset of VSCCs demonstrates unusual p16/p53 staining patterns, challenging their classification. Here, we report 30 such tumors, representing, to our knowledge, the largest series to date. p53 immunostaining was evaluated using the six-pattern framework, including four mutant patterns (basal overexpression, parabasal/diffuse overexpression, cytoplasmic, and absent) and two wild-type patterns (scattered and mid-epithelial). All cases underwent TP53 next-generation sequencing and high-risk HPV (hrHPV) DNA PCR testing. The largest subgroup consisted of tumors showing p16 positivity and p53 overexpression (n=20). Of these, 18 harbored TP53 mutations, while hrHPV DNA was detected in seven, including the two TP53 wild-type tumors. Six tumors showed p16 positivity with absent p53 staining; all harbored TP53 mutations, and two were also hrHPV DNA positive. Three tumors showed p16 positivity confined to the nuclei with p53 overexpression; all harbored TP53 mutations, and one was hrHPV-positive. The final case was p16-negative with a mid-epithelial p53 staining pattern, known to be strongly associated with HPV-associated tumors. This tumor was TP53 wild-type and hrHPV33-positive. Our study demonstrates that not all VSCCs are readily classified by p16 and p53 immunohistochemistry and confirms that p16 positivity alone is insufficient to define HPV-association. Most tumors showed concurrent p16 positivity (three with nuclear-only staining) and p53 mutant patterns, and molecular analysis supported predominantly TP53-driven carcinogenesis with TP53 mutations detected in 93% (27/29). However, hrHPV DNA was also detected in 10 cases, suggesting more complex biology. This rare subset of VSCCs may therefore be better categorized by their immunophenotypic and molecular profiles rather than forced into the current classification. Further studies with prognostic correlation are needed to clarify their biological and clinical significance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Most vulvar squamous cell carcinomas (VSCCs) are classified using p16 and p53 immunohistochemistry into HPV-associated or HPV-independent tumors, but a rare subset demonstrates unusual p16/p53 staining patterns, challenging their classification.
Design and population
The study analyzed 30 vulvar squamous cell carcinomas, with the largest subgroup showing p16 positivity and p53 overexpression (n=20). The final case was p16-negative with a mid-epithelial p53 staining pattern, known to be strongly associated with HPV-associated tumors.
Core methods
All cases underwent TP53 next-generation sequencing and high-risk HPV (hrHPV) DNA PCR testing to evaluate immunophenotypic and molecular profiles.
Main results
Of the 20 tumors showing p16 positivity and p53 overexpression, 18 harbored TP53 mutations, while hrHPV DNA was detected in seven, including the two TP53 wild-type tumors. Six tumors showed p16 positivity with absent p53 staining; all harbored TP53 mutations, and two were also hrHPV DNA positive. Three tumors showed p16 positivity confined to the nuclei with p53 overexpression; all harbored TP53 mutations, and one was hrHPV-positive. The final case was p16-negative with a mid-epithelial p53 staining pattern, TP53 wild-type, and hrHPV33-positive.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the rare subset of VSCCs may be better categorized by their immunophenotypic and molecular profiles rather than forced into the current classification, as molecular analysis supported predominantly TP53-driven carcinogenesis with TP53 mutations detected in 93% (27/29) of cases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the current classification based on p16 and p53 immunohistochemistry may not be sufficient for all VSCCs, and molecular analysis is needed to better understand their biological and clinical significance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its relatively small sample size and the lack of prognostic correlation data, but the evidence is strong as it represents the largest series of such tumors to date and includes detailed molecular analysis.

骨髓间充质干细胞通过激活miR-129诱导HPV相关宫颈癌上皮间质转化的分子机制研究。

Study on the molecular mechanism of bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells inducing epithelial-mesenchymal transition in HPV-associated cervical cancer through activation of miR-129.
作者： Nieying Ma, Kaijie Xu, Zhize Wang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨 miR-129 在 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 中的作用,解决骨髓间充质干细胞 (BMSC) 和 miR-129 在肿瘤进展中之间尚不清楚的关系。HPV 相关的 宫颈癌 仍然是全球女性癌症相关死亡的主要原因。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 77 个 宫颈癌 和 30 个正常宫颈组织样本,将 BMSC 与 SiHa 和 HeLa 细胞共培养,以检测 miR-129 表达及其对细胞增殖、侵袭和 EMT 标志物表达的影响。
核心方法
通过 qRT-PCR 测量 miR-129 表达,并分析与临床病理特征和总生存期的关联。通过ROC曲线评估诊断价值,并将BMSC与SiHa和HeLa细胞共培养以检查miR-129过表达或敲低效应。
主要结果
与正常对照相比,宫颈癌 组织中 miR-129 的表达显着较高。miR-129 高表达与肿瘤大小、FIGO 分期和淋巴结转移相关,并且与较短的总生存期相关。BMSC 共培养上调 miR-129 表达,促进增殖、集落形成和侵袭,而 miR-129 过表达降低 E-钙粘蛋白并增加 N-钙粘蛋白和 MRP1 表达。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,BMSCs/miR-129 轴可能有助于 宫颈癌 的进展,但还需要进一步的体内和机制研究。
科研与公卫意义
该研究将 miR-129 确定为 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 的潜在诊断和预后生物标志物,并强调了 BMSC 在调节 miR-129 表达和癌细胞表型中的作用。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到体外模型的使用和进一步体内验证的需要的限制。样本量相对较小,研究结果的普遍性可能有限。

Study on the molecular mechanism of bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells inducing epithelial-mesenchymal transition in HPV-associated cervical cancer through activation of miR-129.

Authors: Nieying Ma, Kaijie Xu, Zhize Wang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: HPV-associated cervical cancer remains a leading cause of cancer-related death in women worldwide. The role of bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells (BMSCs) and miR-129 in tumor progression remains unclear. OBJECTIVES: To investigate miR-129 expression and its clinical significance in HPV-associated cervical cancer and to explore whether BMSCs regulate malignant phenotypes through miR-129. METHODS: miR-129 expression was measured by qRT-PCR in 77 cervical cancer and 30 normal cervical tissues. Associations with clinicopathological features and overall survival (OS) were analyzed. Diagnostic value was assessed by ROC curve. Rat BMSCs were co-cultured with SiHa and HeLa cells. Following miR-129 overexpression or knockdown, cell proliferation (CCK-8, colony formation), invasion (Transwell) and expression of E-cadherin, N-cadherin and MRP1 (Western blot) were examined. RESULTS: miR-129 expression was significantly higher in cervical cancer tissues than in normal controls (3.24±0.56 vs. High miR-129 expression correlated with tumor size ≥3 cm (P=0.009), FIGO stage III-IV (P=0.0034) and lymph node metastasis (P=0.038). High expression was associated with shorter OS (median: 28.4 vs. 46.2 months; HR=2.944, 95%CI:1.484-5.842, P=0.002). The AUC was 0.788 (95%CI:0.693-0.884), with a cut-off of 1.85 (sensitivity:76.9%, specificity:70.4%). BMSC co-culture upregulated miR-129 expression (SiHa: 3.52±0.42 vs. miR-129 overexpression promoted proliferation, colony formation and invasion, while knockdown suppressed these phenotypes. Overexpression of miR-129 reduced E-cadherin (0.42±0.05 vs. 1.00±0.08, P<0.01) and increased N-cadherin (2.85±0.22 vs. 1.00±0.10, P<0.01) and MRP1 (2.46±0.18 vs. Knockdown of miR-129 partially reversed BMSC-induced changes in these proteins. CONCLUSION: miR-129 is significantly upregulated in HPV-associated cervical cancer and shows potential as a diagnostic and prognostic biomarker. BMSCs upregulate miR-129 in cervical cancer cells and miR-129 modulates proliferation, invasion, EMT marker expression and MRP1 expression. These findings suggest that the BMSCs/miR-129 axis may contribute to cervical cancer progression, but further in-vivo and mechanistic studies are needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of miR-129 in HPV-associated cervical cancer, addressing the unclear relationship between bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells (BMSCs) and miR-129 in tumor progression. HPV-associated cervical cancer remains a leading cause of cancer-related death in women worldwide.
Design and population
The study involved 77 cervical cancer and 30 normal cervical tissue samples, with BMSCs co-cultured with SiHa and HeLa cells to examine miR-129 expression and its effects on cell proliferation, invasion, and EMT marker expression.
Core methods
miR-129 expression was measured by qRT-PCR, and associations with clinicopathological features and overall survival were analyzed. Diagnostic value was assessed by ROC curve, and BMSCs were co-cultured with SiHa and HeLa cells to examine miR-129 overexpression or knockdown effects.
Main results
miR-129 expression was significantly higher in cervical cancer tissues compared to normal controls. High miR-129 expression correlated with tumor size, FIGO stage, and lymph node metastasis, and was associated with shorter overall survival. BMSC co-culture upregulated miR-129 expression, promoting proliferation, colony formation, and invasion, while miR-129 overexpression reduced E-cadherin and increased N-cadherin and MRP1 expression.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the BMSCs/miR-129 axis may contribute to cervical cancer progression, but further in-vivo and mechanistic studies are needed.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study identifies miR-129 as a potential diagnostic and prognostic biomarker for HPV-associated cervical cancer and highlights the role of BMSCs in modulating miR-129 expression and cancer cell phenotypes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of in vitro models and the need for further in-vivo validation. The sample size is relatively small, and the generalizability of the findings may be limited.

使用 cobas® 4800 系统在赞比亚西部省筛查 宫颈癌 的女性中检测高风险 人乳头瘤病毒:基于实验室的回顾性研究

Detection of High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Using the cobas® 4800 System among Women Screened for Cervical Cancer in Western Province, Zambia: A Retrospective Laboratory-Based Study
作者： Phiri Faliton, Michael Mokha, Mukubesa Matakala, Paul Simusika, David Kajoba Mumena, Faliton Phiri
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定赞比亚西部省接受 宫颈癌 筛查的女性中的 患病率 和高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 感染的分布,解决该地区各地区 HR-HPV 感染的有限证据。
设计与对象
使用 2025 年 5 月 9 日至 2026 年 6 月 29 日期间在赞比亚西部省七个地区接受 HR-HPV 检测的女性的 宫颈癌 筛查记录进行了一项基于实验室的回顾性横断面研究。
核心方法
HR-HPV检测采用cobas® 4800 HPV Test进行,数据提取自Kaoma PCR实验室的实验室信息系统。患病率 使用具有 95% 置信区间的比例进行总结,并使用统计检验评估不同年龄组和地区之间的差异。
主要结果
在 1,623 名女性中,HR-HPV 的总体 患病率 为 32.59% (95% CI 30.35–34.92)。患病率 因年龄而异,25 岁​​以下的女性中发病率最高,也因地区而异,恩基耶马最高,卡拉博最低。
解释与创新
研究发现,在西部省七个地区接受筛查的女性中,约有三分之一受到 HR-HPV 感染的影响,不同年龄和地区的差异很大。其他 HR-HPV 靶点的主导地位凸显了广泛 HR-HPV 检测在 宫颈癌 筛查中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
区级 监测 使用常规生成的实验室数据可以为监测和加强西部省 宫颈癌 预防服务提供有用的证据。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对常规收集数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差。样本量和地理覆盖范围足以满足研究目标,但由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Detection of High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Using the cobas® 4800 System among Women Screened for Cervical Cancer in Western Province, Zambia: A Retrospective Laboratory-Based Study

Authors: Phiri Faliton, Michael Mokha, Mukubesa Matakala, Paul Simusika, David Kajoba Mumena, Faliton Phiri
Original abstract (English)
Background: High-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection is the cause of cervical cancer, but its distribution varies across populations and geographical settings. Evidence on HR-HPV infection across districts of Western Province, Zambia, remains limited. This study determined the prevalence and distribution of HR-HPV infection and examined variation by age and district among women screened for cervical cancer. Methods: We conducted a retrospective laboratory-based cross-sectional study using routinely collected cervical cancer screening records from women tested for HR-HPV between 9 May 2025 and 29 June 2026. Data were extracted from the laboratory information system of the Kaoma PCR Laboratory, which serves seven districts in Western Province. HR-HPV testing was performed using the cobas® 4800 HPV Test. The primary outcome was detection of at least one HR-HPV target. HR-HPV prevalence and target distribution were summarized using proportions with 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Differences across age groups and districts were assessed using Pearson's chi-square test. Logistic regression was used to estimate crude and adjusted odds ratios (aORs) with 95% CIs for factors associated with HR-HPV positivity. Results: Among 1,623 women, the median age was 40 years (IQR 31–49; range 15–89). HR-HPV was detected in 529 women, giving an overall prevalence of 32.59% (95% CI 30.35–34.92). Prevalence was highest among women aged <25 years (46.58%) and decreased to 26.67% among those aged 45–54 years before increasing to 34.32% among those aged 55–64 years (χ2=26.96, p<0.001). HR-HPV prevalence ranged from 21.84% in Kalabo to 37.04% in Nkeyema (χ2=12.70, p=0.048). Among HR-HPV-positive women, 456 (86.20%) had other HR-HPV targets, 88 (16.64%) had HPV16, and 48 (9.07%) had HPV18/45. Compared with women aged <25 years, adjusted odds of HR-HPV positivity were lower among those aged 25–34 years (aOR 0.66, 95% CI 0.45–0.97), 35–44 years (0.43, 0.29–0.64), 45–54 years (0.38, 0.25–0.57), 55–64 years (0.55, 0.35–0.88), and ≥65 years (0.50, 0.26–0.97). Women from Kalabo had lower odds of HR-HPV positivity than those from Kaoma (aOR 0.55, 95% CI 0.37–0.83). Conclusions: HR-HPV infection affected approximately one-third of women screened across seven districts of Western Province, with substantial variation by age and district. The predominance of other HR-HPV targets highlights the importance of broad HR-HPV detection in cervical cancer screening. District-level surveillance using routinely generated laboratory data can provide useful evidence for monitoring and strengthening cervical cancer prevention services in Western Province.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to determine the prevalence and distribution of high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection among women screened for cervical cancer in Western Province, Zambia, addressing the limited evidence on HR-HPV infection across districts in the region.
Design and population
A retrospective laboratory-based cross-sectional study was conducted using cervical cancer screening records from women tested for HR-HPV between May 9, 2025, and June 29, 2026, in seven districts of Western Province, Zambia.
Core methods
HR-HPV testing was performed using the cobas® 4800 HPV Test, and data were extracted from the laboratory information system of the Kaoma PCR Laboratory. Prevalence was summarized using proportions with 95% confidence intervals, and differences across age groups and districts were assessed using statistical tests.
Main results
Among 1,623 women, the overall prevalence of HR-HPV was 32.59% (95% CI 30.35–34.92). Prevalence varied by age, with the highest rates among women aged <25 years, and by district, with the highest in Nkeyema and lowest in Kalabo.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that HR-HPV infection affected approximately one-third of women screened across seven districts of Western Province, with substantial variation by age and district. The predominance of other HR-HPV targets highlights the importance of broad HR-HPV detection in cervical cancer screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
District-level surveillance using routinely generated laboratory data can provide useful evidence for monitoring and strengthening cervical cancer prevention services in Western Province.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on routinely collected data, which may introduce bias. The sample size and geographical coverage are adequate for the study objectives, but the evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

在小鼠模型中口服表达 人乳头瘤病毒 16 E2 的乳杆菌诱导 免疫应答 的评价

Evaluation of Immune Responses Induced by Oral Administration of Human Papillomavirus 16 E2-Expressing Lactobacillus in a Mouse Model
作者： Mitsuru Toeda, Saki Kamata, Rie Maruyama, Shingo Naraki, Isamu Taiko, Kazunori Kanemaru, Rina Kawatake, Risa Yoshida, Nobuki Hayashi, Yuki Okuma
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:持续性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染会增加 宫颈癌 风险,但尚无既定的 治疗 能够根除持续性感染。我们开发了 LacE2N,一种口服 疫苗 制剂,源自表达 HPV16 E2 N 末端结构域的重组副干酪乳杆菌,并评估了鼠免疫原性和人宫颈标本中的 E2 转录。方法:通过 MHC I 类结合预测和 IFN-γ ELISpot 筛选来鉴定候选 E2 肽。通过菌落印迹和流式细胞术评估重组细菌 E2N 表达。口服免疫后,通过 ELISpot 评估脾细胞和肠上皮内淋巴细胞 (IEL) 反应。通过 LDH 释放来测量针对 TC-1/E2 与亲代 TC-1 细胞的差异细胞毒性。通过 RT-qPCR 评估宫颈 E2 转录本。结果:肽 8(氨基酸 71-85)被选为候选免疫原性区域。菌落印迹和流式细胞术支持细菌 E2N 表达。在一项探索性 IEL 比较(一只小鼠/组)中,5 mg LacE2N 的反应高于 PBS。LacE2N (1 mg) 比 Lac 对照产生更高的 IEL 和脾细胞 IFN-γ 反应。2 mg LacE2N 或 Lac 的脾脏反应在数值上高于 1 mg LacE2N 或 Lac。高剂量免疫增强了针对 TC-1/E2 的差异细胞毒性,效应子与靶标的比例为 5:1 和 10:1。在所有 68 份 HPV16 阳性宫颈标本中均检测到 E2 N 末端转录本,其中 7 份没有组织学可检测到的宫颈上皮内瘤变。结论:这些发现为进一步研究 E2 靶向口服 疫苗接种 针对持续 HPV16 感染的作用提供了临床前依据,特别是在已形成的癌前病变发生之前。需要进一步的研究来建立抗原特异性免疫机制和治疗功效。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估针对 HPV16 的口服 疫苗 制剂,解决缺乏针对持续 HPV 感染的既定 治疗 的问题,这会增加 宫颈癌 的风险。
设计与对象
该研究涉及小鼠模型,以评估表达 HPV16 E2 N 末端结构域的重组副干酪乳杆菌的免疫原性和 E2 转录。
核心方法
通过 MHC I 类结合预测和 IFN-γ ELISpot 筛选鉴定了候选 E2 肽。通过菌落印迹和流式细胞术证实细菌E2N表达。使用 ELISpot 在脾细胞和肠上皮内淋巴细胞中评估 免疫应答,并通过 LDH 释放测量差异细胞毒性。通过 RT-qPCR 评估 E2 转录本。
主要结果
选择肽8作为候选免疫原性区域。与 PBS 相比,5 mg LacE2N 观察到更高的 免疫应答,1 mg LacE2N 产生比 Lac 对照更高的响应。高剂量免疫增强了针对 TC-1/E2 细胞的差异细胞毒性。在所有 HPV16 阳性宫颈标本中均检测到 E2 N 末端转录本。
解释与创新
这些发现为进一步研究 E2 靶向口服 疫苗接种 对抗持续性 HPV16 感染提供了临床前依据,特别是在已形成的癌前病变发生之前。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明对 HPV16 感染和 宫颈癌 预防具有潜在的治疗益处,需要进一步研究以确定抗原特异性免疫机制和治疗效果。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于使用小鼠模型以及需要进一步的人体研究来证实研究结果而受到限制。由于该研究的探索性,证据强度中等。

Evaluation of Immune Responses Induced by Oral Administration of Human Papillomavirus 16 E2-Expressing Lactobacillus in a Mouse Model

Authors: Mitsuru Toeda, Saki Kamata, Rie Maruyama, Shingo Naraki, Isamu Taiko, Kazunori Kanemaru, Rina Kawatake, Risa Yoshida, Nobuki Hayashi, Yuki Okuma
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Persistent human papillomavirus (HPV) infection increases cervical cancer risk, yet no established treatment eradicates persistent infection. We developed LacE2N, an oral vaccine formulation derived from recombinant Lacticaseibacillus paracasei expressing the HPV16 E2 N-terminal domain, and evaluated murine immunogenicity and E2 transcription in human cervical specimens. Methods: Candidate E2 peptides were identified by MHC class I-binding prediction and IFN-γ ELISpot screening. Recombinant bacterial E2N expression was assessed by colony blotting and flow cytometry. Following oral immunization, splenocyte and intestinal intraepithelial lymphocyte (IEL) responses were evaluated by ELISpot. Differential cytotoxicity against TC-1/E2 versus parental TC-1 cells was measured by LDH release. Cervical E2 transcripts were assessed by RT-qPCR. Results: Peptide 8 (amino acids 71–85) was selected as a candidate immunogenic region. Colony blotting and flow cytometry supported bacterial E2N expression. In an exploratory IEL comparison (one mouse/group), responses were higher with 5 mg LacE2N than PBS. LacE2N (1 mg) yielded higher IEL and splenocyte IFN-γ responses than Lac controls. Splenic responses were numerically higher with 2 mg than 1 mg LacE2N or Lac. High-dose immunization enhanced differential cytotoxicity against TC-1/E2 at effector-to-target ratios of 5:1 and 10:1. E2 N-terminal transcripts were detected in all 68 HPV16-positive cervical specimens, including seven without histologically detectable cervical intraepithelial neoplasia. Conclusions: These findings provide a preclinical rationale for further investigation of E2-targeted oral vaccination against persistent HPV16 infection, particularly before the development of established precancerous lesions. Further studies are warranted to establish antigen-specific immune mechanisms and therapeutic efficacy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate an oral vaccine formulation for HPV16, addressing the lack of established treatments for persistent HPV infection, which increases cervical cancer risk.
Design and population
The study involved a mouse model to assess the immunogenicity and E2 transcription of a recombinant Lacticaseibacillus paracasei expressing the HPV16 E2 N-terminal domain.
Core methods
Candidate E2 peptides were identified through MHC class I-binding prediction and IFN-γ ELISpot screening. Bacterial E2N expression was confirmed by colony blotting and flow cytometry. Immune responses were evaluated in splenocytes and intestinal intraepithelial lymphocytes using ELISpot, and differential cytotoxicity was measured by LDH release. E2 transcripts were assessed by RT-qPCR.
Main results
Peptide 8 was selected as a candidate immunogenic region. Higher immune responses were observed with 5 mg LacE2N compared to PBS, and 1 mg LacE2N yielded higher responses than Lac controls. High-dose immunization enhanced differential cytotoxicity against TC-1/E2 cells. E2 N-terminal transcripts were detected in all HPV16-positive cervical specimens.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide a preclinical rationale for further investigation of E2-targeted oral vaccination against persistent HPV16 infection, particularly before the development of established precancerous lesions.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results suggest potential therapeutic benefits for HPV16 infection and cervical cancer prevention, warranting further studies to establish antigen-specific immune mechanisms and therapeutic efficacy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its use of a mouse model and the need for further human studies to confirm the findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the exploratory nature of the study.

社区参与的高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 自抽样用于性和性别少数人群 宫颈癌 筛查的可行性研究

A Community Engaged Feasibility Study of High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Self-Sampling for Cervical Cancer Screening in Sexual and Gender Minority People
作者： Mollie Aleshire, Jennifer S. Tinman, Ryan Combs, Rhiannon Huffines, Nqobile Nzama, Yasmine Bey, Tereasa Boose, Vickie Tencer, Shuying Sha
摘要中文翻译背景和目标:我们调查了肯塔基州性少数群体 (SGM) 家庭自我采样 宫颈癌 筛查 (CCS) 对高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 的可接受性和可行性。方法:我们利用虚拟访谈进行了一项描述性定性研究,以调查影响 CCS 的社会生态因素以及家庭 hrHPV 自我采样用于原发性 CCS 的可行性和可接受性。使用标准抽样和滚雪球抽样。使用混合归纳演绎主题方法和团队成员共识来分析数据,以确定显着主题。结果:19 名参与者在 2022-2023 年完成了 hrHPV 自我采样和定性访谈。确定的 6 个首要主题是 宫颈癌 筛查认知、既往负面医疗经历、hrHPV 自我采样的可取性、完成 hrHPV 自我采样、对 hrHPV 自我采样的担忧以及参与研究的原因。结论:家庭 hrHPV 自我采样是一种有前景的干预措施,可增加 SGM 人群的 CCS。应让个人放心,自我取样是主要 CCS 的有效方法,并且他们很可能能够收集产生准确结果的样本。为了最大限度地提高 SGM 个人完成 CCS 的可能性,医疗保健提供者和医疗保健环境必须在整个 CCS 过程中成为肯定的、具有文化能力和知情的合作伙伴。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查肯塔基州性少数群体 (SGM) 家庭自我采样 宫颈癌 筛查 (CCS) 对高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 的可接受性和可行性。
设计与对象
我们在 2022 年至 2023 年期间对 19 名参与者进行了虚拟访谈,进行了一项描述性定性研究,以探讨影响 宫颈癌 筛查的社会生态因素以及在 SGM 个体中进行 hrHPV 自我采样的可行性。
核心方法
使用标准和滚雪球抽样来招募参与者,并使用混合归纳演绎主题方法和团队成员共识来分析数据,以确定显着主题。
主要结果
十九名参与者完成了高危型HPV自我采样和定性访谈,并识别出六个主要主题,涉及对宫颈癌筛查的看法、先前负面的医疗经历、高危型HPV自我采样的吸引力、完成高危型HPV自我采样的过程、对高危型HPV自我采样的担忧以及参与研究的原因。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明,在家进行高危型HPV自我采样是一种有前景的干预措施,可提高SGM人群的宫颈癌筛查率,强调医疗提供者需要成为肯定和支持且具有文化能力的合作伙伴。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,高危型HPV自我采样可能是提高SGM个体宫颈癌筛查率的宝贵工具,可能有助于实现更公平的医疗保健获取和结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到定性设计和小样本量的限制,但鉴于其彻底的主题分析和团队成员共识,证据是强有力的。

A Community Engaged Feasibility Study of High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Self-Sampling for Cervical Cancer Screening in Sexual and Gender Minority People

Authors: Mollie Aleshire, Jennifer S. Tinman, Ryan Combs, Rhiannon Huffines, Nqobile Nzama, Yasmine Bey, Tereasa Boose, Vickie Tencer, Shuying Sha
Original abstract (English)
Background and Objectives: We investigated the acceptability and feasibility of at-home self-sampling cervical cancer screening (CCS) for high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) in sexual and gender minority (SGM) Kentuckians. Methods: We conducted a descriptive qualitative study using virtual interviews to investigate socioecological factors influencing CCS and the feasibility and acceptability of at-home hrHPV self-sampling for primary CCS. Criterion and snowball sampling were used. Data were analyzed using a hybrid inductive-deductive thematic approach and team member consensus to identify salient themes. Results: Nineteen participants completed hrHPV self-sampling and qualitative interviews in 2022–2023. The 6 overarching themes identified were cervical cancer screening perceptions, prior negative healthcare experiences, desirability of hrHPV self-sampling, completing hrHPV self-sampling, concerns about hrHPV self-sampling, and reasons for study participation. Conclusion: At-home hrHPV self-sampling is a promising intervention to increase CCS in SGM people. Individuals should be reassured that self-sampling is an effective method for primary CCS and that they are likely to be able to collect a sample that will yield accurate results. To maximize the possibility of CCS completion by SGM individuals, healthcare providers and healthcare environments must be affirming, culturally competent, and informed partners throughout the CCS process.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the acceptability and feasibility of at-home self-sampling cervical cancer screening (CCS) for high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) in sexual and gender minority (SGM) Kentuckians.
Design and population
A descriptive qualitative study was conducted using virtual interviews with 19 participants in 2022–2023 to explore socioecological factors influencing cervical cancer screening and the feasibility of hrHPV self-sampling in SGM individuals.
Core methods
Criterion and snowball sampling were used to recruit participants, and data were analyzed using a hybrid inductive-deductive thematic approach with team member consensus to identify salient themes.
Main results
Nineteen participants completed hrHPV self-sampling and qualitative interviews, and six overarching themes were identified related to cervical cancer screening perceptions, prior negative healthcare experiences, desirability of hrHPV self-sampling, completing hrHPV self-sampling, concerns about hrHPV self-sampling, and reasons for study participation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that at-home hrHPV self-sampling is a promising intervention to increase cervical cancer screening in SGM people, highlighting the need for healthcare providers to be affirming and culturally competent partners.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that hrHPV self-sampling could be a valuable tool for improving cervical cancer screening rates among SGM individuals, potentially contributing to more equitable healthcare access and outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its qualitative design and small sample size, but the evidence is strong given the thorough thematic analysis and team member consensus.

Erectile function and health-related quality of life in men with genital warts: a comparative cross-sectional study.

Authors: Mehmet Altan, Ekin Deniz Gumustas
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Genital warts are associated with psychosocial and sexual health concerns. However, comparative evidence regarding erectile function and health-related quality of life (HRQoL) in affected men remains limited. OBJECTIVE: To compare erectile function, the proportion with IIEF-5-defined erectile dysfunction (ED), and HRQoL between men with and without genital warts, and to examine associations between erectile function and HRQoL. METHODS: This comparative cross-sectional study included 65 men with clinically diagnosed genital warts and 65 control participants without genital warts. Erectile function and HRQoL were assessed using the IIEF-5 and 12-Item Short Form Health Survey (SF-12), respectively. Between-group comparisons used the Mann-Whitney U test. Age-category-adjusted prevalence ratios were estimated using modified Poisson regression. Correlations were assessed using Spearman's coefficient, with Benjamini-Hochberg false discovery rate correction applied separately to outcome comparisons and correlation tests. RESULTS: Median IIEF-5 scores were 21 in the genital warts group and 24 in controls (p < 0.001; Hodges-Lehmann location shift, - 2.0 points; 95% CI, - 3.0 to - 1.0). IIEF-5-defined ED occurred in 33/65 (50.8%) and 13/65 (20.0%) participants, respectively (age-category-adjusted prevalence ratio, 2.54; 95% CI, 1.53-4.19). The PCS-12 between-group difference was small (Hodges-Lehmann location shift, - 0.66 points; p = 0.023; FDR-adjusted q = 0.035), while MCS-12 scores did not differ significantly (p = 0.110). IIEF-5 correlated positively with PCS-12 in the overall sample (ρ = 0.39), genital warts group (ρ = 0.29), and controls (ρ = 0.44), but not with MCS-12. CONCLUSIONS: Men with genital warts had lower erectile function scores and a higher proportion with IIEF-5-defined ED than controls. The IIEF-5 score difference was modest, with uncertain clinical relevance, and HRQoL differences were limited. Because of the cross-sectional design and potential residual confounding, causality cannot be established. Prospective studies are warranted.
View research seven-element analysis

Design and population
Erectile function and HRQoL were assessed using the IIEF-5 and 12-Item Short Form Health Survey (SF-12), respectively.
Core methods
Age-category-adjusted prevalence ratios were estimated using modified Poisson regression.
Main results
IIEF-5-defined ED occurred in 33/65 (50.8%) and 13/65 (20.0%) participants, respectively (age-category-adjusted prevalence ratio, 2.54; 95% CI, 1.53-4.19).
Interpretation and novelty

Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on abstract only. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

口腔健康专业人员和 人乳头瘤病毒 预防:紧急行动呼吁

Oral Health Professionals and Human Papillomavirus Prevention: An Urgent Call to Action
作者： Lakshman Samaranayake, Saman Warnakulasuriya, Lakshman Perera Samaranayake
摘要中文翻译口腔健康专家
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究强调了口腔健康专业人员在预防 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染和相关疾病方面的关键作用,强调需要提高这一领域的认识和采取积极措施。
设计与对象
该研究是《国际牙科杂志》的社论,发表于 2026-12-01,重点关注口腔健康专业人员在 HPV 预防中的责任。
核心方法
该社论概述了将 HPV 预防策略纳入常规牙科护理的重要性,包括教育、筛查以及与其他医疗保健提供者的合作。
主要结果
该社论报道称,口腔健康专业人员有独特的机会通过早期检测和患者教育为 HPV 预防做出贡献,但没有提供具体的数字结果。
解释与创新
作者解释说,将 HPV 预防纳入牙科实践是减少 HPV 相关疾病 发病率 的一种新方法,特别是在以口腔健康专业人员为主要接触点的人群中。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,口腔健康专业人员可以在 HPV 监测 和预防中发挥关键作用,这对旨在减轻 HPV 相关癌症和感染负担的公共卫生战略具有重大影响。
局限与证据强度
作为一篇社论,该研究没有提供原始数据,其结论是基于现有文献和专家意见,这将证据的强度限制在中等。

通过新的数学模型对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 进行小波研究和分析

Wavelet-based study and analysis of human papillomavirus (HPV) through a new mathematical model
作者： Raghuramreddy Yeshwanth, Srinivasa Kumbinarasaiah
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染被认为是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,宫颈癌 是影响全球女性的最常见恶性肿瘤之一。本文提出了一个分区数学模型,描述了 HPV 的 传播 导致 宫颈癌。该模型将总人口分为六个部分:易感人群、暴露人群、感染人群、宫颈癌、康复人群和接种疫苗人群。制定的非线性微分方程系统捕获了这些类别之间的流行病学转变,包括感染、进展、恢复和疾病引起的 死亡率。为了获得高效且准确的近似解,采用卢卡斯小波方法(LuWM)。Lucas小波基函数具有正交性和紧支持性,可将系统转化为一组代数方程组。收敛分析证实了所提出方案的可靠性。通过数值模拟和图形说明证明了该方法的效率。各种技术的结果与所提出的 LuWM 进行了比较。使用图形讨论不同因变量相对于不同参数的变化。这些发现为 HPV 动态提供了宝贵的见解,并有助于设计 宫颈癌 预防的最佳控制策略。

Wavelet-based study and analysis of human papillomavirus (HPV) through a new mathematical model

Authors: Raghuramreddy Yeshwanth, Srinivasa Kumbinarasaiah
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is recognized as the leading cause of cervical cancer, one of the most prevalent malignancies affecting women worldwide. This article presents a compartmental mathematical model that describes the transmission of HPV leading to cervical cancer. The model divides the total population into six compartments: susceptible, exposed, infected, cervical cancer, recovered, and vaccinated. The formulated nonlinear system of differential equations captures the epidemiological transitions among these classes, including infection, progression, recovery, and disease-induced mortality. To obtain an efficient and accurate approximate solution, the Lucas wavelet method (LuWM) is employed. The Lucas wavelet basis functions, possessing orthogonality and compact support, are used to transform the system into a set of algebraic equations. Convergence analyses confirm the reliability of the proposed scheme. The method’s efficiency is demonstrated through numerical simulations and graphical illustrations. The results of various techniques are compared with the proposed LuWM. The variation of different dependent variables with respect to different parameters is discussed using figures. The findings provide valuable insight into HPV dynamics and can assist in designing optimal control strategies for cervical cancer prevention.

Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is recognized as the leading cause of cervical cancer, one of the most prevalent malignancies affecting women worldwide.

人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 覆盖范围对细胞病理学结果的影响:北方邦三级护理教学医院的横断面研究

Impact of Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Coverage on Cytopathological Findings: A Cross-sectional Study in a Tertiary Care Teaching Hospital of Uttar Pradesh
作者： Nidhi Gupta, Akrishti Gupta
摘要中文翻译背景:高危型 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 持续感染是 宫颈癌 发展的主要病因。尽管 HPV 疫苗 提供了强有力的保护,但全剂量和不完整/无 疫苗接种 对细胞学结果的影响存在差异。目的:评估和比较完成完整 HPV 的女性中 患病率 宫颈细胞学异常的情况疫苗 时间表以及部分或未接受 疫苗接种 的患者。材料和方法:这项以医院为基础的横断面研究于 2023 年 1 月至 2025 年 1 月在印度北方邦阿格拉的一家三级护理医院进行。总共纳入了 4,200 名年龄在 25-45 岁之间接受 宫颈癌 筛查的女性。参与者根据以下条件进行分类: HPV 疫苗接种 状态:全剂量(三剂量)与部分/无剂量。根据 Bethesda 2014 系统对巴氏涂片进行分类。评估人口统计学、生殖史和行为风险因素。多变量逻辑回归确定调整后的关联。结果:与部分/无剂量组相比,全剂量组中异常子宫颈抹片检查 (5.8%) 显着降低(14.7%) (p < 0.001)。完全接种疫苗的女性出现高级别病变 (HSIL+) 的几率降低[调整后的比值比 (aOR):0.23;95% CI: 0.15-0.36]。按年龄、胎次和初次性行为分层的亚组中,保护作用保持一致。结论:完成完整的 HPV 疫苗 时间表与显着降低的细胞学异常率相关。增强 疫苗 的依从性应成为 宫颈癌 预防的优先事项。

Impact of Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Coverage on Cytopathological Findings: A Cross-sectional Study in a Tertiary Care Teaching Hospital of Uttar Pradesh

Authors: Nidhi Gupta, Akrishti Gupta
Original abstract (English)
Background: Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) is the primary etiological factor in cervical cancer development.Although HPV vaccines provide strong protection, disparities exist between full-dose and incomplete/no vaccination in their impact on cytological outcomes.Objective: To evaluate and compare the prevalence of cervical cytological abnormalities among women who completed the full HPV vaccine schedule and those who received partial or no vaccination.Materials and methods: This hospital-based cross-sectional study was conducted from January 2023 to January 2025 in a tertiary care hospital in Agra, Uttar Pradesh, India.A total of 4,200 women aged 25-45 years undergoing cervical cancer screening were included.Participants were categorized based on HPV vaccination status: Full-dose (three-doses) vs partial/no-dose.Pap smears were classified per the Bethesda 2014 system.Demographics, reproductive history, and behavioral risk factors were assessed.Multivariable logistic regression determined adjusted associations.Results: Abnormal Pap smear prevalence was significantly lower in the full-dose group (5.8%) compared to the partial/no-dose group (14.7%) (p < 0.001).Fully vaccinated women had reduced odds of high-grade lesions (HSIL+) [adjusted odds ratio (aOR): 0.23; 95% CI: 0.15-0.36].The protective effect remained consistent across subgroups stratified by age, parity, and sexual debut.Conclusion: Completion of the full HPV vaccine schedule is associated with markedly lower rates of cytological abnormalities.Enhancing vaccine adherence should be a priority for cervical cancer prevention.

乳腺癌活检中人乳头瘤病毒和Epstein-Barr病毒的存在作为潜在风险因素

Presence of Human Papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr Virus in Breast Cancer Biopsies as Potential Risk Factors
作者： Marla Ladera, Andreína Fernándes, Marco López, Adriana Pesci-Feltri, Ávila Maira, María Correnti, Andreina Fernandes, Maira Ávila
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乳腺癌是委内瑞拉女性人群中癌症相关死亡的主要原因。许多促进其发生的危险因素已被报道;然而,人乳头瘤病毒和Epstein-Barr病毒在过去几十年中被关联为这种恶性肿瘤发展的潜在危险因素。
设计与对象
从在加拉加斯大学医院接受手术的乳腺癌患者和乳腺良性病理患者中收集新鲜活检样本。
核心方法
使用INNO-LIPA ® HPV Genotyping Extra商业试剂盒(Innogenetics)检测人乳头瘤病毒,使用Epstein-Barr病毒BMLF1商业试剂盒(Maxim Biotech,Inc.)检测Epstein-Barr病毒基因组。
主要结果
63.6和13.6%的乳腺癌病例分别对人乳头瘤病毒和Epstein-Barr病毒DNA呈阳性,而良性病理样本中每种病毒的阳性率均为4.5%;42.90%的乳腺癌样本存在低和高致癌风险的人乳头瘤病毒基因型混合感染。
解释与创新
我们可以认为人乳头瘤病毒和Epstein-Barr病毒是乳腺癌的重要危险因素;然而,需要进行研究来阐明这些病毒在疾病发展中的作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来关于 人乳头瘤病毒 在乳腺癌发展中的作用的研究提供信息,并强调需要进一步研究以阐明其对该疾病的潜在贡献。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其观察性质和缺乏详细的统计分析而受到限制,这可能会影响证据的强度。

Presence of Human Papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr Virus in Breast Cancer Biopsies as Potential Risk Factors

Authors: Marla Ladera, Andreína Fernándes, Marco López, Adriana Pesci-Feltri, Ávila Maira, María Correnti, Andreina Fernandes, Maira Ávila
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Breast cancer represents the leading cause of cancer-related death in the Venezuelan female population.Many risk factors favoring its appearance have been reported; however, human papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr virus have been associated in the past few decades as potential risk factors in the development of this malignancy.Objective: To detect the presence of human papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr virus in breast cancer biopsies in order to establish a possible link between infection with these viral agents and the development of this pathology.Methods: Fresh biopsies were collected from patients with breast cancer and patients with breast benign pathology attending the Hospital Universitario de Caracas for surgery.Human papillomavirus detection was made using the INNO-LIPA ® HPV Genotyping Extra commercial kit (Innogenetics), and Epstein-Barr virus genome was detected with Epstein-Barr Virus BMLF1 commercial kit (Maxim Biotech, Inc.).Results: 63.6 and 13.6% of breast cancer cases were positive for human papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr virus DNA, respectively, whereas the benign pathology samples had 4.5% positivity for each one of the viruses; 42.90% of breast cancer samples had mixed infection with low and high oncogenic risk human papillomavirus genotypes.Conclusion: We can suggest that human papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr virus are important risk factors for breast cancer; however, studies allowing for the role of these viruses in the development of the disease to be elucidated are required.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Breast cancer represents the leading cause of cancer-related death in the Venezuelan female population. Many risk factors favoring its appearance have been reported; however, human papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr virus have been associated in the past few decades as potential risk factors in the development of this malignancy.
Design and population
Fresh biopsies were collected from patients with breast cancer and patients with breast benign pathology attending the Hospital Universitario de Caracas for surgery.
Core methods
Human papillomavirus detection was made using the INNO-LIPA ® HPV Genotyping Extra commercial kit (Innogenetics), and Epstein-Barr virus genome was detected with Epstein-Barr Virus BMLF1 commercial kit (Maxim Biotech, Inc.).
Main results
63.6 and 13.6% of breast cancer cases were positive for human papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr virus DNA, respectively, whereas the benign pathology samples had 4.5% positivity for each one of the viruses; 42.90% of breast cancer samples had mixed infection with low and high oncogenic risk human papillomavirus genotypes.
Interpretation and novelty
We can suggest that human papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr virus are important risk factors for breast cancer; however, studies allowing for the role of these viruses in the development of the disease to be elucidated are required.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future studies on the role of human papillomavirus in breast cancer development and highlight the need for further research to clarify its potential contribution to the disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational nature and the lack of detailed statistical analysis, which may affect the strength of the evidence.
