Human Papillomavirus DNA Detection in Laryngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma: Prevalence and Exploratory Survival Associations in a Retrospective Cohort from the Mexican Bajío Region
作者： Pablo Romero-Morelos, Francisco Arath Ruiz-Velázquez, Ana Lilia González-Yebra, Marcela Pérez Villaseñor, Luis Jonathan Bueno-Rosario, Beatríz González-Yebra, Ana Lilia González Yebra
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是口咽癌中公认的病因和预后因素,但其在喉鳞状细胞癌(LSCC)中的作用仍存在争议。目的:评估 LSCC HPV DNA 的 患病率 并分析其与临床病理特征和生存的关系。方法:我们对 2007 年 6 月至 2022 年 8 月期间在墨西哥巴希奥地区一家转诊医院诊断的连续 47 名经组织学确诊为 LSCC 的患者进行了回顾性 队列研究 研究。使用GP5+/GP6+引物通过共有PCR检测HPV DNA;未评估 HPV 基因分型和病毒转录活性标记。使用Kaplan-Meier法和Cox回归分析总生存期(OS)和局部无病生存期(局部DFS)。结果:大多数患者为男性(95.7%),平均年龄为62.5±11.2岁;74.5% 为吸烟者,61.7% 为饮酒者,63.8% (30/47) 被诊断为 IV 期。在 6 个肿瘤中检测到 HPV DNA(12.8%;95% CI 4.8–25.7)。声门肿瘤 (28.6%) 和非角化肿瘤 (22.2%) 以及 I-II 期疾病的阳性率较高(III-IV 期为 33.3% vs. 7.5%;p = 0.120)。HPV 阳性患者接受手术的频率较低(33.3% vs. 中位随访时间为 48.7 个月,2 年和 5 年 OS 率分别为 52.7% 和 32.1%。HPV 状态与 OS 无关(6 名 HPV 阳性患者中有 4 例死亡;HR 1.42;95% CI 0.48–4.15;调整后 HR 1.07;95% CI 0.32–3.61;p = 0.918)、局部 DFS(HR 1.02)或局部复发(16.7% vs. 结论:我们的结果表明,队列研究 中大约八分之一的 LSCC 含有 HPV DNA;早期疾病的阳性率较高。尽管统计功效有限(最小可检测 HR ≈),但未检测到预后关联。4.6),HPV 状态仅基于 DNA 检测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 LSCC 中 HPV DNA 的 患病率 并分析其与临床病理特征和生存的关系。HPV 是口咽癌中公认的病因和预后因素,但其在 LSCC 中的作用仍存在争议。
设计与对象
对 2007 年 6 月至 2022 年 8 月期间在墨西哥巴希奥地区一家转诊医院诊断的连续 47 名经组织学证实患有 LSCC 的患者进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究。
核心方法
使用 GP5+/GP6+ 引物通过共有 PCR 检测 HPV DNA。使用Kaplan-Meier法和Cox回归分析总生存期(OS)和局部无病生存期(局部DFS)。
主要结果
在 6 个肿瘤中检测到 HPV DNA(12.8%;95% CI 4.8–25.7)。声门肿瘤 (28.6%) 和非角化肿瘤 (22.2%) 以及 I-II 期疾病的阳性率较高(III-IV 期为 33.3% vs. 7.5%;p = 0.120)。HPV 阳性患者接受手术的频率较低(33.3% vs. 中位随访时间为 48.7 个月,2 年和 5 年 OS 率分别为 52.7% 和 32.1%。HPV 状态与 OS 无关(HR 1.42;95% CI 0.48–4.15;调整后 HR 1.07;95% CI 0.32–3.61;p = 0.918)或局部 DFS(HR 1.02)。
解释与创新
结果表明,队列研究 中大约八分之一的 LSCC 含有 HPV DNA;早期疾病的阳性率较高。尽管统计功效有限(最小可检测 HR ≈ 4.6)并且 HPV 状态仅基于 DNA 检测,但未检测到与预后相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果警告说,仅 HPV DNA 检测可能不足以用于 LSCC 的预后目的。他们认为 HPV 阳性在早期疾病中更为常见,可能对 监测 和 治疗 策略产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计、样本量小以及缺乏 HPV 基因分型和病毒转录活性标记而受到限制。统计功效有限,应谨慎解释结果。

Human Papillomavirus DNA Detection in Laryngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma: Prevalence and Exploratory Survival Associations in a Retrospective Cohort from the Mexican Bajío Region

Authors: Pablo Romero-Morelos, Francisco Arath Ruiz-Velázquez, Ana Lilia González-Yebra, Marcela Pérez Villaseñor, Luis Jonathan Bueno-Rosario, Beatríz González-Yebra, Ana Lilia González Yebra
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a well-established etiological and prognostic factor in oropharyngeal cancer, but its role in laryngeal squamous cell carcinoma (LSCC) remains controversial. Objective: To estimate the prevalence of HPV DNA in LSCC and to analyze its association with clinicopathological characteristics and survival. Methods: We conducted a retrospective cohort study of 47 consecutive patients with histologically confirmed LSCC diagnosed between June 2007 and August 2022 at a referral hospital in the Bajío region of Mexico. HPV DNA was detected by consensus PCR using GP5+/GP6+ primers; HPV genotyping and markers of viral transcriptional activity were not assessed. Overall survival (OS) and locoregional disease-free survival (locoregional DFS) were analyzed using the Kaplan–Meier method and Cox regression. Results: Most patients were men (95.7%), with a mean age of 62.5 ± 11.2 years; 74.5% were smokers, 61.7% consumed alcohol, and 63.8% (30/47) were diagnosed at stage IV. HPV DNA was detected in 6 tumors (12.8%; 95% CI 4.8–25.7). Positivity was numerically higher in glottic (28.6%) and non-keratinizing (22.2%) tumors and in stage I–II disease (33.3% vs. 7.5% in stage III–IV; p = 0.120). HPV-positive patients underwent surgery less frequently (33.3% vs. With a median follow-up of 48.7 months, the 2- and 5-year OS rates were 52.7% and 32.1%, respectively. HPV status was not associated with OS (4 deaths among the 6 HPV-positive patients; HR 1.42; 95% CI 0.48–4.15; adjusted HR 1.07; 95% CI 0.32–3.61; p = 0.918), locoregional DFS (HR 1.02), or locoregional recurrence (16.7% vs. Conclusions: Our results suggest that approximately one in eight LSCCs in this cohort harbored HPV DNA; positivity was numerically higher in earlier-stage disease. No prognostic association was detected, although statistical power was limited (minimum detectable HR ≈ 4.6) and HPV status was based on DNA detection only.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to estimate the prevalence of HPV DNA in LSCC and to analyze its association with clinicopathological characteristics and survival. HPV is a well-established etiological and prognostic factor in oropharyngeal cancer, but its role in LSCC remains controversial.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study of 47 consecutive patients with histologically confirmed LSCC diagnosed between June 2007 and August 2022 at a referral hospital in the Bajío region of Mexico was conducted.
Core methods
HPV DNA was detected by consensus PCR using GP5+/GP6+ primers. Overall survival (OS) and locoregional disease-free survival (locoregional DFS) were analyzed using the Kaplan–Meier method and Cox regression.
Main results
HPV DNA was detected in 6 tumors (12.8%; 95% CI 4.8–25.7). Positivity was numerically higher in glottic (28.6%) and non-keratinizing (22.2%) tumors and in stage I–II disease (33.3% vs. 7.5% in stage III–IV; p = 0.120). HPV-positive patients underwent surgery less frequently (33.3% vs. With a median follow-up of 48.7 months, the 2- and 5-year OS rates were 52.7% and 32.1%, respectively. HPV status was not associated with OS (HR 1.42; 95% CI 0.48–4.15; adjusted HR 1.07; 95% CI 0.32–3.61; p = 0.918) or locoregional DFS (HR 1.02).
Interpretation and novelty
The results suggest that approximately one in eight LSCCs in this cohort harbored HPV DNA; positivity was numerically higher in earlier-stage disease. No prognostic association was detected, although statistical power was limited (minimum detectable HR ≈ 4.6) and HPV status was based on DNA detection only.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution that HPV DNA detection alone may not be sufficient for prognostic purposes in LSCC. They suggest that HPV positivity is more common in earlier-stage disease and may have implications for surveillance and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design, small sample size, and lack of HPV genotyping and markers of viral transcriptional activity. The statistical power was limited, and the results should be interpreted with caution.

非 16/18 高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 基因型多重性与持续 HPV 阳性的宫颈上皮内瘤变 2+ 检测的关联

Association of non-16/18 high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) genotype multiplicity with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 2+ detection in persistent HPV positivity
作者： Cem Yağmur Özdemir, Cem Yagmur Ozdemir
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
旨在调查因持续非 16/18 高危型HPV 阳性而接受阴道镜活检的女性中多个非 16/18 高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 基因型阳性与组织学证实的宫颈上皮内瘤变 2+ (CIN2+) 之间的关联。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究评估了 238 名 18-65 岁的女性,她们在至少两次间隔 12 个月或更长时间的连续测试中 高危型HPV 呈阳性后,在单一中心接受了阴道镜活检。HPV16 阳性和 HPV18 阳性女性被排除在外。
核心方法
HPV 多样性是根据最近阴道镜检查前测试报告的基因型类别来定义的。事后简约逻辑回归模型包括多重性和高风险细胞学。主要结果是组织学证实的 CIN2+。
主要结果
Of the 238 women, 159 had single-category positivity, and 79 had multiple-category positivity; 25 women had CIN2+. CIN2+ was detected in 14/79 women (17.7%) in the multiple HPV group and 11/159 women (6.9%) in the single HPV group. The unadjusted odds ratio (OR) was 2.90, 95% confidence interval (CI) 1.25–6.72, P = 0.014. After adjustment for high-risk cytology, the estimate was attenuated and no longer statistically significant; adjusted OR 2.37, 95% CI 0.96–5.82, P = 0.061. The HPV31-containing multiple-infection subgroup finding was exploratory and not statistically significant.
解释与创新
在这个选定的具有重复非 16/18 高危型HPV 阳性的 队列研究 中,多个报告的基因型类别阳性与较高的未调整 CIN2+ 检测相关,但在对高风险细胞学进行有限调整后,估计值有所减弱。HPV31 亚组结果是初步的且是假设性的。这些发现并未将多重性确定为独立或因果风险因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,非 16/18 高危型HPV 基因型的多样性可能是持续非 16/18 高危型HPV 阳性女性 CIN2+ 风险增加的标志,但需要进一步的研究来证实这种关联并了解其临床意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和患有 CIN2+ 的女性人数较少而受到限制。高危细胞学的调整是有限的,HPV31亚组的发现是探索性的。证据强度中等。

Association of non-16/18 high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) genotype multiplicity with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 2+ detection in persistent HPV positivity

Authors: Cem Yağmur Özdemir, Cem Yagmur Ozdemir
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: To investigate the association between multiple non-16/18 high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) genotype positivity and histologically confirmed cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 2+ (CIN2+) among women undergoing colposcopic biopsy because of persistent non-16/18 high-risk HPV positivity. Material and Methods: This retrospective study evaluated 238 women aged 18–65 years who underwent colposcopic biopsy at a single center after high-risk HPV positivity on at least two consecutive tests performed 12 months or more apart. HPV16-positive and HPV18-positive women were excluded. Multiplicity was defined from the genotype categories reported on the most recent pre-colposcopy test. A post hoc parsimonious logistic regression model included multiplicity and high-risk cytology. The primary outcome was histologically confirmed CIN2+. Results: Of the 238 women, 159 had single-category positivity, and 79 had multiple-category positivity; 25 women had CIN2+. CIN2+ was detected in 14/79 women (17.7%) in the multiple HPV group and 11/159 women (6.9%) in the single HPV group. The unadjusted odds ratio (OR) was 2.90, 95% confidence interval (CI) 1.25–6.72, P = 0.014. After adjustment for high-risk cytology, the estimate was attenuated and no longer statistically significant; adjusted OR 2.37, 95% CI 0.96–5.82, P = 0.061. The HPV31-containing multiple-infection subgroup finding was exploratory and not statistically significant. Conclusion: In this selected cohort with repeated non-16/18 high-risk HPV positivity, multiple reported genotype-category positivity was associated with higher unadjusted CIN2+ detection, but the estimate was attenuated after limited adjustment for high-risk cytology. The HPV31 subgroup result was preliminary and hypothesis-generating. These findings do not establish multiplicity as an independent or causal risk factor.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To investigate the association between multiple non-16/18 high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) genotype positivity and histologically confirmed cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 2+ (CIN2+) among women undergoing colposcopic biopsy because of persistent non-16/18 high-risk HPV positivity.
Design and population
This retrospective study evaluated 238 women aged 18–65 years who underwent colposcopic biopsy at a single center after high-risk HPV positivity on at least two consecutive tests performed 12 months or more apart. HPV16-positive and HPV18-positive women were excluded.
Core methods
HPV multiplicity was defined from the genotype categories reported on the most recent pre-colposcopy test. A post hoc parsimonious logistic regression model included multiplicity and high-risk cytology. The primary outcome was histologically confirmed CIN2+.
Main results
Of the 238 women, 159 had single-category positivity, and 79 had multiple-category positivity; 25 women had CIN2+. CIN2+ was detected in 14/79 women (17.7%) in the multiple HPV group and 11/159 women (6.9%) in the single HPV group. The unadjusted odds ratio (OR) was 2.90, 95% confidence interval (CI) 1.25–6.72, P = 0.014. After adjustment for high-risk cytology, the estimate was attenuated and no longer statistically significant; adjusted OR 2.37, 95% CI 0.96–5.82, P = 0.061. The HPV31-containing multiple-infection subgroup finding was exploratory and not statistically significant.
Interpretation and novelty
In this selected cohort with repeated non-16/18 high-risk HPV positivity, multiple reported genotype-category positivity was associated with higher unadjusted CIN2+ detection, but the estimate was attenuated after limited adjustment for high-risk cytology. The HPV31 subgroup result was preliminary and hypothesis-generating. These findings do not establish multiplicity as an independent or causal risk factor.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that multiplicity of non-16/18 high-risk HPV genotypes may be a marker for increased risk of CIN2+ in women with persistent non-16/18 high-risk HPV positivity, but further research is needed to confirm this association and understand its clinical implications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the small number of women with CIN2+. The adjustment for high-risk cytology was limited, and the HPV31 subgroup finding was exploratory. The evidence strength is moderate.

Homoeopathic Management of Vulvar Genital Warts Treated with Thuja Occidentalis: A Case Report

Homoeopathic Management of Vulvar Genital Warts Treated with Thuja Occidentalis: A Case Report

Authors: Sajitha RB, Binaya BV, Manikanda Perumal K
Original abstract (English)
: Vulvar genital warts, also known as condylomata acuminata, are benign epithelial proliferations of the external genitalia caused by Human Papillomavirus (HPV) infection, predominantly involving HPV types 6 and 11. A 40years old female patient presented with complaints of warts over the vulvar region for a duration of six months. On clinical examination, clustered, irregular, exophytic lesions with a rough, papillomatous surface were observed over the vulvar region. The lesions exhibited variable yellowish-white and greyish pigmentation with irregular projections, giving them a cauliflower-like appearance. Homoeopathic medicine Thuja occidentalis 200 was selected on the basis of Homoeopathic case taking and symptoms totality.

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于持续性 高危型HPV 感染在 宫颈癌 和其他上皮恶性肿瘤中的重要作用,该研究解决了了解 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 进入和细胞内运输的分子机制的需要,以确定阻断 HPV 感染的新靶点。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了涉及 HPV 进入的关键 宿主 因子、膜平台和细胞内运输过程,涵盖各种实验系统和细胞模型。
核心方法
该综述结合文献综述和实验数据,总结了 HPV 进入所涉及的关键 宿主 因素、膜平台、非典型内吞途径、内体分选事件、逆行运输途径和核进入过程。
主要结果
该综述详细介绍了 HPV 进入的多步骤贩运网络,包括 BM/ECM 相关和细胞表面附着因子的初始附着、衣壳构象启动、L2 N 末端暴露和弗林蛋白酶介导的 L2 裂解,然后是进入平台组织和细胞内贩运事件。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调了关键 宿主 因子和细胞内运输过程作为 HPV 感染早期干预目标的潜在价值,强调了 HPV 进入的复杂性和进一步研究的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过确定新的干预靶点,为 HPV 相关疾病(特别是 宫颈癌)的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于对现有文献和实验数据的回顾,这可能无法捕捉不同背景下 HPV 进入的所有细微差别。由于缺乏原始实验数据,证据强度中等。

Question and background
The study addresses the need to understand the molecular mechanisms of human papillomavirus (HPV) entry and intracellular trafficking to identify novel targets for blocking HPV infection, given the significant role of persistent high-risk HPV infection in cervical cancer and other epithelial malignancies.
Design and population
The study reviewed the key host factors, membrane platforms, and intracellular trafficking processes involved in HPV entry, spanning various experimental systems and cell models.
Core methods
The review summarized the key host factors, membrane platforms, atypical endocytic routes, endosomal sorting events, retrograde trafficking pathways, and nuclear entry processes involved in HPV entry, using a combination of literature review and experimental data.
Main results
The review detailed the multistep trafficking network of HPV entry, including initial attachment by BM/ECM-associated and cell-surface attachment factors, capsid conformational priming, L2 N-terminal exposure, and furin-mediated L2 cleavage, followed by entry platform organization and intracellular trafficking events.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the potential value of key host factors and intracellular trafficking processes as early intervention targets against HPV infection, emphasizing the complexity of HPV entry and the need for further research.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for HPV-associated diseases, particularly cervical cancer, by identifying novel targets for intervention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a review of existing literature and experimental data, which may not capture all nuances of HPV entry in different contexts. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of original experimental data.

表面健康的多哥女性中与 HPV 感染和宫颈发育不良相关的阴道微生物组改变

Vaginal microbiome alterations associated with HPV infection and cervical dysplasia in apparently healthy Togolese women
作者： Eya H. Kamassa, Gnatoulma Katawa, Komi Nambou, Righteous Kwaku Agoha, Enoch Matey Langmer, Adwoa Ayewa Ayimadu, Bingo Kignomon M’bortche, Charles Ochieng’ Olwal, Essimanam Rosalie Awesso, Motomdéwa Monique Kadanga
摘要中文翻译背景 持续高危人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染是宫颈癌的主要原因;然而,在撒哈拉以南非洲地区,阴道微生物群失调在 HPV 阳性和宫颈发育不良中的作用仍然知之甚少。本研究调查了多哥表面健康女性中与 HR-HPV 感染和宫颈发育不良相关的宫颈阴道微生物组特征。方法 在多哥洛美进行了一项基于社区的横断面 宫颈癌 筛查研究。使用 Nugent 评分评估阴道生态失调,使用 Bethesda 2014 系统进行宫颈细胞学检查,并使用 Anyplex II HPV HR 检测试剂盒进行 HR-HPV 基因分型。通过 Illumina MiSeq 对 16S rRNA 基因的 V3-V4 区域进行测序来进行阴道微生物组分析。生物信息学包括用于处理的 DADA2、用于多样性分析的 R 中的 phyloseq 和 vegan、用于差异丰度的 DESeq2 以及用于功能预测的带有 MetaCyc 的 PICRUSt2。结果 338名女性中,HR-HPV 患病率占31.33%,宫颈发育不良占10.06%。HPV16 是最常见的基因型,其次是 HPV51 和 HPV58。HR-HPV 感染和发育异常与显着较高的 Nugent 评分相关(p = 0.001 和 p = 0.008)。Alpha 和 Beta 多样性分析显示,HPV 或不典型增生状态没有显着的全球微生物组重组。然而,成分变化表明乳酸菌减少,厌氧和机会性菌群富集,包括阴道阿托波菌、血链球菌、普雷沃氏菌、厌氧消化链球菌和粪消化链球菌。群落状态类型分析揭示了从乳酸菌主导的 CST 到菌群失调 CST IV 的转变。功能预测显示受影响组中蔗糖降解 II、分支酸生物合成 II 和厌氧途径的富集。未观察到 HPV 病毒载量与生态失调评分之间存在相关性。结论 多哥妇女的 HR-HPV 感染和宫颈发育不良与阴道微生物群失调有关,其特征是乳酸菌耗竭和厌氧、潜在促炎类群富集。这些发现表明微生物组对 HPV 阳性和宫颈病变有贡献,并支持将其纳入撒哈拉以南非洲的预防策略。这是第一项以西非社区为基础的研究,结合了盲法宫颈筛查、Nugent 评分和基于测序的微生物组分析。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查多哥表面健康女性的阴道微生物群失调在 HPV 阳性和宫颈发育不良中的作用,解决撒哈拉以南非洲地区特征不明的关系。
设计与对象
在多哥洛美对 338 名女性进行了一项横断面、基于社区的 宫颈癌 筛查研究。
核心方法
该研究结合了盲法宫颈筛查、Nugent 评分和基于测序的微生物组分析来评估阴道菌群失调和 HPV 感染。
主要结果
338名女性中,HR-HPV 患病率为31.33%,宫颈发育不良为10.06%。HPV16 是最常见的基因型,其次是 HPV51 和 HPV58。HR-HPV 感染和发育异常与显着较高的 Nugent 评分相关(p = 0.001 和 p = 0.008)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明微生物组对 HPV 阳性和宫颈病变有贡献,支持将其纳入撒哈拉以南非洲的预防策略。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调了阴道微生物群失调在 HPV 感染和宫颈发育不良中的重要性,这可以为多哥和其他撒哈拉以南非洲国家未来的 监测 和预防工作提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,未建立因果关系,并且缺乏纵向数据。样本量适中,并且该研究是在单一地点进行的,限制了普遍性。

Vaginal microbiome alterations associated with HPV infection and cervical dysplasia in apparently healthy Togolese women

Authors: Eya H. Kamassa, Gnatoulma Katawa, Komi Nambou, Righteous Kwaku Agoha, Enoch Matey Langmer, Adwoa Ayewa Ayimadu, Bingo Kignomon M’bortche, Charles Ochieng’ Olwal, Essimanam Rosalie Awesso, Motomdéwa Monique Kadanga
Original abstract (English)
Background Persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection is the main cause of cervical cancer; however, the role of vaginal microbiome dysbiosis in HPV positivity and cervical dysplasia remains poorly characterized in sub-Saharan Africa. This study examined cervicovaginal microbiome profiles associated with HR-HPV infection and cervical dysplasia among apparently healthy women in Togo. Methods A cross-sectional, community-based cervical cancer screening study was conducted in Lomé, Togo. Vaginal dysbiosis was assessed using Nugent scoring, cervical cytology using the Bethesda 2014 system, and HR-HPV genotyping using the Anyplex II HPV HR Detection Kit. Vaginal microbiome profiling was performed by Illumina MiSeq sequencing of the V3-V4 region of the 16S rRNA gene. Bioinformatics included DADA2 for processing, phyloseq and vegan in R for diversity analyses, DESeq2 for differential abundance, and PICRUSt2 with MetaCyc for functional prediction. Results Among 338 women, HR-HPV prevalence was 31.33% and cervical dysplasia 10.06%. HPV16 was the most frequent genotype, followed by HPV51 and HPV58. HR-HPV infection and dysplasia were associated with significantly higher Nugent scores (p = 0.001 and p = 0.008). Alpha and beta diversity analyses showed no significant global microbiome restructuring by HPV or dysplasia status. However, compositional shifts showed depletion of Lactobacillus and enrichment of anaerobic and opportunistic taxa, including Atopobium vaginae, Sneathia sanguinegens, Prevotella spp., Peptostreptococcus anaerobius, and Peptoniphilus faecalis. Community state type analysis revealed a shift from Lactobacillus-dominated CSTs to dysbiotic CST IV. Functional prediction showed enrichment of sucrose degradation II, chorismate biosynthesis II, and anaerobic pathways in affected groups. No correlation was observed between HPV viral load and dysbiosis score. Conclusion HR-HPV infection and cervical dysplasia in Togolese women are linked to vaginal microbiome dysbiosis characterized by Lactobacillus depletion and enrichment of anaerobic, potentially pro-inflammatory taxa. These findings suggest a microbiome contribution to HPV positivity and cervical lesions and support its integration into prevention strategies in sub-Saharan Africa. This is the first West African community-based study combining blinded cervical screening, Nugent scoring, and sequencing-based microbiome profiling.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of vaginal microbiome dysbiosis in HPV positivity and cervical dysplasia among apparently healthy women in Togo, addressing the poorly characterized relationship in sub-Saharan Africa.
Design and population
A cross-sectional, community-based cervical cancer screening study was conducted in Lomé, Togo, among 338 women.
Core methods
The study used a combination of blinded cervical screening, Nugent scoring, and sequencing-based microbiome profiling to assess vaginal dysbiosis and HPV infection.
Main results
Among 338 women, HR-HPV prevalence was 31.33%, and cervical dysplasia was 10.06%. HPV16 was the most frequent genotype, followed by HPV51 and HPV58. HR-HPV infection and dysplasia were associated with significantly higher Nugent scores (p = 0.001 and p = 0.008).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest a microbiome contribution to HPV positivity and cervical lesions, supporting its integration into prevention strategies in sub-Saharan Africa.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study highlights the importance of vaginal microbiome dysbiosis in HPV infection and cervical dysplasia, which could inform future surveillance and prevention efforts in Togo and other sub-Saharan African countries.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which does not establish causality, and the lack of longitudinal data. The sample size is moderate, and the study was conducted in a single location, limiting generalizability.

通过 cobas® 4800 系统对秘鲁利马女性进行人乳头瘤病毒检测

Human Papilloma Virus detection through the cobas® 4800 system in women from Lima, Peru
作者： Javier Manrique-Hinojosa, Gustavo Sarria-Bardales, Ma. del Carmen Núñez-Teran, Abelardo Arias, Pamela Mora, Yasser Sullcahuamán-Allende, Ysis Roa-Meggo, Guisselle Pineda-Rodríguez
摘要中文翻译目的:使用 Cobas 4800 检测确定 2012 年至 2013 年间利马女性高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 感染的 患病率。方法:该研究纳入了 5324 名 30 岁或以上的女性,其宫颈样本接受了 Cobas 4800 分子检测。阳性样本通过液基细胞学分析。结果: HR-HPV 感染的 患病率 为 15.2%。基因型 16 占 16.2%,基因型 18 占 3. 1%,基因型 31、33、35、39、45、51、52、56、58、59、66 和 68 占 71.2%.62.8% 的样本经鉴定无细胞学变化,LSIL 为 19.8%,ASCUS 为 14%,HSIL 为 2%,ASC-H 为 0.7%。结论:本研究中 HR-HPV 的 患病率 仍然显着。在简单感染中,基因型 16 和 18 的频率突出,而在合并感染中,其他 12 种 HPV-RA 基因型组占主导地位。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在使用 Cobas 4800 测试确定 2012 年至 2013 年间利马女性高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 感染的 患病率。
设计与对象
该研究纳入了 5324 名 30 岁或以上的女性,她们的宫颈样本接受了 Cobas 4800 分子检测。
核心方法
使用 Cobas 4800 分子检测对宫颈样本进行检测,并通过液基细胞学分析阳性样本。
主要结果
高危型HPV感染的患病率为15.2%,其中16型占16.2%,31、33、35、39、45、51、52、56、58、59、66和68型合计占71.2%。62.8%的样本未发现细胞学改变,19.8%为低度鳞状上皮内病变(LSIL),14%为非典型鳞状上皮细胞(ASCUS),2%为高度鳞状上皮内病变(HSIL),0.7%为ASC-H。
解释与创新
本研究中 HR-HPV 的 患病率 仍然很重要,简单感染显示基因型 16 和 18 的频率较高,而合并感染则以其他 12 种 HPV-RA 基因型组为主。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,HR-HPV 仍然是利马的一个重大公共卫生问题,特定基因型很普遍。
局限与证据强度
研究设计的局限性包括样本量和缺乏随机性,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。

Human Papilloma Virus detection through the cobas® 4800 system in women from Lima, Peru

Authors: Javier Manrique-Hinojosa, Gustavo Sarria-Bardales, Ma. del Carmen Núñez-Teran, Abelardo Arias, Pamela Mora, Yasser Sullcahuamán-Allende, Ysis Roa-Meggo, Guisselle Pineda-Rodríguez
Original abstract (English)
Aim: Determine the prevalence of high-risk Human Papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection in women from Lima between 2012 and 2013 using the Cobas 4800 test.Method: The study included 5324 women aged 30 or over, whose cervical samples underwent the Cobas 4800 molecular test.Positive samples were analyzed through liquid-based cytology.Results: The prevalence of HR-HPV infection was 15.2%.Genotype 16 was found in 16.2%, genotype 18 in 3. 1% and genotypes 31, 33, 35, 39, 45, 51, 52, 56, 58, 59, 66, and 68 in 71.2%.62.8% of samples were identified without cytological changes, 19.8% with LSIL, 14% with ASCUS, 2% with HSIL, and 0.7% with ASC-H.Conclusion: The prevalence of HR-HPV in this study continues to be significant.In simple infections, the frequency of genotypes 16 and 18 stood out, and in coinfections, the group of the other twelve HPV-RA genotypes predominated.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to determine the prevalence of high-risk Human Papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection in women from Lima between 2012 and 2013 using the Cobas 4800 test.
Design and population
The study included 5324 women aged 30 or over, whose cervical samples underwent the Cobas 4800 molecular test.
Core methods
Cervical samples were tested using the Cobas 4800 molecular test, and positive samples were analyzed through liquid-based cytology.
Main results
The prevalence of HR-HPV infection was 15.2%, with genotype 16 found in 16.2% and genotypes 31, 33, 35, 39, 45, 51, 52, 56, 58, 59, 66, and 68 in 71.2%. 62.8% of samples were identified without cytological changes, 19.8% with LSIL, 14% with ASCUS, 2% with HSIL, and 0.7% with ASC-H.
Interpretation and novelty
The prevalence of HR-HPV in this study continues to be significant, with simple infections showing a high frequency of genotypes 16 and 18, and coinfections predominated by the group of other twelve HPV-RA genotypes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that HR-HPV remains a significant public health concern in Lima, with specific genotypes being prevalent.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design limitations include the sample size and the lack of randomization, which may affect the generalizability of the results.

前瞻性 队列研究 中的基线红细胞叶酸和低度宫颈上皮内瘤变的结果

Baseline erythrocyte folate and outcomes of low grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia in a prospective cohort
作者： Ding L, Li Z, Qiao X, Hu N, Jia A, Liu J, Zheng X, Li H, Zhao K, Zhang M
摘要中文翻译这些发现并不能确立叶酸补充的因果关系、与HR-HPV的相互作用或治疗益处。研究得到了山西医科大学伦理委员会的批准(编号:Ten)。因样本不足而排除了10例,最终有2,287名女性符合诊断类别;其中564名患有CIN1的女性参与了本研究。TCT,薄层细胞学检查;NILM,未发现上皮内病变或恶性病变;ASC-US+,不典型鳞状上皮细胞意义未明或更严重;HPV,人乳头瘤病毒;NC,正常宫颈;CIN1,宫颈上皮内瘤变1级;CIN2/3,宫颈上皮内瘤变2级或3级;SCC,鳞状细胞癌。研究流程图显示,从基线时的39,971例中,分为NILM(37,219例)和ASC-US+(2,752例)。排除和随访过程导致NC(1,503例)、CIN1(564例)、CIN2/3(218例)和SCC(2例)的亚组被纳入本研究。2.2 数据和样本收集经过培训的研究人员使用标准化问卷进行面对面访谈,收集人口统计学特征、生活方式因素、个人卫生习惯、月经和性史以及肿瘤家族史。目的:探讨基线红细胞叶酸与CIN1逆转、持续和进展之间的关联。方法:本社区基于人群的前瞻性队列研究研究包括564名中国CIN1患者,随访评估在6、12和24个月进行。基线红细胞叶酸通过化学发光免疫分析测定。组织学结果分为逆转、持续或进展至CIN2或更严重(CIN2+);持续和进展形成一个复合不良结果。分析包括...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过前瞻性 队列研究 调查基线红细胞叶酸水平与低度宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN1) 结局之间的关联。该研究解决了有关叶酸对 CIN1 进展影响的知识差距,并为了解 宫颈癌 预防中的营养因素提供了理论基础。
设计与对象
一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究于 2020 年至 2024 年进行,招募了 1,503 名无宫颈上皮内瘤变 (NC) 的女性、564 名患有 CIN1 的女性、218 名患有 CIN2/3 的女性和 2 名患有鳞状细胞癌 (SCC) 的女性。通过面对面访谈收集数据,并使用化学发光免疫测定法测量红细胞叶酸水平。
核心方法
使用化学发光免疫测定法测量红细胞叶酸水平。Cox 回归、限制三次样条和受试者工作特征 (ROC) 曲线用于统计分析。该研究调整了潜在的混杂因素,例如 HR-HPV 感染和肿瘤家族史。
主要结果
较低的基线红细胞叶酸与不良 CIN1 结局相关,调整后的风险比 (aHR) 为 2.42 (95% CI: 1.62–3.61)。在 6 个月或 12 个月时未检测到显着的非线性,但在 24 个月时观察到 L 形关联(非线性 P = 0.005)。
解释与创新
研究发现,调整混杂因素后,较低的红细胞叶酸基线与不良 CIN1 结局相关。这种关联是新颖的,并为了解叶酸在 CIN1 进展中的作用提供了见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,维持足够的叶酸水平可能有益于预防 CIN1 的不良后果。这可以为 监测 和 宫颈癌 的预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括事件时间假设和残留混杂因素,这限制了结果的解释。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Baseline erythrocyte folate and outcomes of low grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia in a prospective cohort

Authors: Ding L, Li Z, Qiao X, Hu N, Jia A, Liu J, Zheng X, Li H, Zhao K, Zhang M
Original abstract (English)

View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the association between baseline erythrocyte folate levels and outcomes of low-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN1) in a prospective cohort. The research addressed the knowledge gap regarding the impact of folate on CIN1 progression and provided a rationale for understanding nutritional factors in cervical cancer prevention.
Design and population
A prospective cohort study was conducted from 2020 to 2024, enrolling 1,503 women with no cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (NC), 564 with CIN1, 218 with CIN2/3, and 2 with squamous cell carcinoma (SCC). Data were collected through face-to-face interviews and erythrocyte folate levels were measured using chemiluminescence immunoassay.
Core methods
Erythrocyte folate levels were measured using a chemiluminescence immunoassay. Cox regression, restricted cubic splines, and receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curves were used for statistical analyses. The study adjusted for potential confounders such as HR-HPV infection and family history of tumors.
Main results
Lower baseline erythrocyte folate was associated with adverse CIN1 outcomes, with an adjusted hazard ratio (aHR) of 2.42 (95% CI: 1.62–3.61). No significant nonlinearity was detected at 6 or 12 months, but an L-shaped association was observed at 24 months (P for nonlinearity = 0.005).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that lower baseline erythrocyte folate was associated with adverse CIN1 outcomes after adjustment for confounders. This association was novel and provided insights into the role of folate in CIN1 progression.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that maintaining adequate folate levels may be beneficial in preventing adverse outcomes of CIN1. This could inform surveillance and prevention strategies for cervical cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include event-time assumptions and residual confounding, which limit the interpretation of the results. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

尼日利亚中北部拉菲亚联邦大学教学医院的婚姻状况与 Hpv 16/18 癌蛋白阳性和宫颈发育不良之间的关系

Association of marital status with Hpv 16/18 oncoprotein positivity and cervical dysplasia at the Federal University Teaching Hospital, Lafia, North-Central Nigeria
作者： Peter Stephen Akpulu, Paul Agabi Odeh, Dauda Stephen Makpa, Lawal Mudashiru, Peter Gbenga Oderinde, Mairiga Abdullahi Alhaji
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨尼日利亚拉菲亚女性婚姻状况与 HPV 16/18 癌蛋白阳性和宫颈发育不良之间的关系,解决文献中不一致的发现。
设计与对象
在尼日利亚拉菲亚,对 75 名年龄≥18 岁的女性进行了一项探索性横断面研究,使用醋酸目视检查、巴氏涂片细胞学检查和 HPV 16/18 快速检测。
核心方法
使用快速检测对参与者进行 HPV 16/18 筛查,并使用目视检查和巴氏涂片细胞学诊断宫颈发育不良。婚姻状况分为已婚和未婚两类。
主要结果
Cervical dysplasia was found in 8 out of 75 women (10.7%), and HPV 16/18 positivity was detected in 9 out of 75 women (12.0%). Marital status was not significantly associated with either outcome, but HPV 16/18 positivity was associated with cervical dysplasia (OR = 6.10, 95% CI: 1.16 to 32.05, p = 0.050).
解释与创新
该研究发现婚姻状况与 HPV 16/18 癌蛋白阳性或宫颈发育不良之间没有显着关联,但强调了 HPV 16/18 阳性与宫颈发育不良之间的关联,表明 HPV 16/18 在 宫颈癌 风险中可能发挥作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,婚姻状况可能不是宫颈不典型增生或 HPV 16/18 癌蛋白阳性的重要危险因素,但它们强调了 HPV 16/18 阳性在 宫颈癌 风险分层中的重要性,强调需要进行更大规模、更有力的研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究的样本量较小,并且将婚姻状况分为已婚和未婚两类,可能限制了其检测显着关联的能力。需要更大规模的研究来证实这些发现。

Association of marital status with Hpv 16/18 oncoprotein positivity and cervical dysplasia at the Federal University Teaching Hospital, Lafia, North-Central Nigeria

Authors: Peter Stephen Akpulu, Paul Agabi Odeh, Dauda Stephen Makpa, Lawal Mudashiru, Peter Gbenga Oderinde, Mairiga Abdullahi Alhaji
Original abstract (English)
This exploratory cross-sectional study examined the association between marital status and HPV 16/18 E6/E7 oncoprotein positivity and cervical dysplasia among women screened in Lafia, Nigeria. The study found no statistically significant association between marital status and either outcome, whereas HPV 16/18 positivity was associated with cervical dysplasia. Abstract Persistent oncogenic human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is the major cause of cervical dysplasia and cervical carcinoma, although its association with marital status remains inconsistent. A minimum sample size of 52 was calculated using Cochran's formula, based on an HPV 16/18 prevalence of 3.5%. However, 75 women aged ≥18 years were consecutively recruited and screened using visual inspection with acetic acid (VIA), Pap smear cytology, and an HPV 16/18 rapid test. Marital status was recorded in four categories but dichotomised as ever-married versus never-married because of the sparse outcome events. Cervical dysplasia was diagnosed in 8/75 women (10.7%), while HPV 16/18 positivity was detected in 9/75 women (12.0%). Marital status was not significantly associated with either outcome (cervical dysplasia: OR = 1.69, 95% CI: 0.19 to 14.92; HPV 16/18 positivity: OR = 0.78, 95% CI: 0.14 to 4.22; p = 1.00 and p = 0.67, respectively). HPV 16/18 positivity was associated with cervical dysplasia (37.5% vs. 9.0%; OR = 6.10, 95% CI: 1.16 to 32.05; p = 0.050). Larger, adequately powered studies are needed to establish whether marital status has any role in cervical cancer risk stratification. Keywords Copyright © 2026 Author(s) retain the copyright of this article. This article is published under the terms of the Creative Commons Attribution License 4.0.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the association between marital status and HPV 16/18 oncoprotein positivity and cervical dysplasia among women in Lafia, Nigeria, addressing the inconsistent findings in the literature.
Design and population
An exploratory cross-sectional study was conducted among 75 women aged ≥18 years in Lafia, Nigeria, using visual inspection with acetic acid, Pap smear cytology, and an HPV 16/18 rapid test.
Core methods
Participants were screened for HPV 16/18 using a rapid test, and cervical dysplasia was diagnosed using visual inspection and Pap smear cytology. Marital status was dichotomised into ever-married and never-married.
Main results
Cervical dysplasia was found in 8 out of 75 women (10.7%), and HPV 16/18 positivity was detected in 9 out of 75 women (12.0%). Marital status was not significantly associated with either outcome, but HPV 16/18 positivity was associated with cervical dysplasia (OR = 6.10, 95% CI: 1.16 to 32.05, p = 0.050).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found no significant association between marital status and HPV 16/18 oncoprotein positivity or cervical dysplasia, but it highlighted the association between HPV 16/18 positivity and cervical dysplasia, suggesting a potential role for HPV 16/18 in cervical cancer risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that marital status may not be a significant risk factor for cervical dysplasia or HPV 16/18 oncoprotein positivity, but they underscore the importance of HPV 16/18 positivity in cervical cancer risk stratification, highlighting the need for larger, adequately powered studies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's small sample size and the dichotomisation of marital status into ever-married and never-married may have limited its power to detect significant associations. Larger studies are needed to confirm the findings.

队列研究 简介:韩国国家 人乳头瘤病毒 免疫计划 队列研究

Cohort profile: the National Human Papillomavirus Immunization Program Cohort in Korea
作者： Jinhee Lee, Hee-Yeon Kang, Hyunju Lee, Kyung-Jin Min, Jeeyeon Shin, Boyeong Ryu, Eunjin Hyun, Hoyeon Jang, Log Young Kim, Jaiyong Kim
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在建立 队列研究 来评估韩国国家 HPV 疫苗接种 计划的长期有效性,解决随着疫苗接种人群成熟而监测 HPV 相关疾病 发病率 的需要。
设计与对象
韩国 HPV NIP 队列研究 是通过将 KDCA 免疫登记信息系统与 NHIS-NHID 连接而建立的,其中包括 2003 年至 2012 年间出生的 2,251,563 名女孩,以评估 疫苗 的有效性并支持未来的政策决策。
核心方法
疫苗接种 状态按 疫苗 类型和接受的剂量次数进行分类,并使用 ICD-10 代码从健康保险索赔中识别出与 HPV 相关的疾病事件。
主要结果
队列研究 提供了全国性的、以人群为基础的资源,用于纵向监测 HPV 相关疾病 发病率 和评估 疫苗 的有效性。
解释与创新
队列研究 将支持针对广泛的 HPV 相关结果评估 疫苗 的有效性,并为未来的 HPV 疫苗接种 政策提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
队列研究 将有助于评估国家 HPV 疫苗接种 计划的长期有效性,并为未来有关 HPV 疫苗接种 的政策决策提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因疾病识别对索赔数据的依赖以及可能存在未测量的混杂因素而受到限制,但它为评估 疫苗 的有效性提供了宝贵的资源。

Cohort profile: the National Human Papillomavirus Immunization Program Cohort in Korea

Authors: Jinhee Lee, Hee-Yeon Kang, Hyunju Lee, Kyung-Jin Min, Jeeyeon Shin, Boyeong Ryu, Eunjin Hyun, Hoyeon Jang, Log Young Kim, Jaiyong Kim

View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to establish a cohort to evaluate the long-term effectiveness of the national HPV vaccination program in Korea, addressing the need to monitor the incidence of HPV-related diseases as the vaccinated population matures.
Design and population
The Korean HPV NIP cohort was established by linking the KDCA Immunization Registry Information System with the NHIS-NHID, including 2,251,563 girls born between 2003 and 2012, to evaluate vaccine effectiveness and support future policy decisions.
Core methods
Vaccination status was classified by vaccine type and number of doses received, and incident HPV-related diseases were identified from health insurance claims using ICD-10 codes.
Main results
The cohort provides a nationwide, population-based resource for longitudinal monitoring of HPV-related disease incidence and evaluation of vaccine effectiveness.
Interpretation and novelty
The cohort will support assessments of vaccine effectiveness against a broad spectrum of HPV-related outcomes and inform future HPV vaccination policy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The cohort will help in evaluating the long-term effectiveness of the national HPV vaccination program and inform future policy decisions regarding HPV vaccination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the reliance on claims data for disease identification and the potential for unmeasured confounders, but it provides a valuable resource for evaluating vaccine effectiveness.

B 常规重要学术文献 · 研究B routine priorityAcademic literature · Research

患病率 和与尼日利亚索科托州索科托选定医院就诊的女性人乳头瘤病毒感染相关的危险因素

Prevalence and Risk Factors Associated with Human Papilloma Virus Infection among Women Attending Selected Hospitals in Sokoto, Sokoto State, Nigeria
作者： S Gambo
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是几乎所有 宫颈癌 的主要原因。尽管 宫颈癌 在很大程度上是可以预防的,但它仍然是发展中国家女性妇科癌症相关死亡的主要原因。这项研究的目的是确定尼日利亚索科托州索科托选定医院就诊的妇女中与人乳头瘤病毒感染相关的 患病率 和危险因素。这项研究使用问卷调查来获取社会人口统计和危险因素的数据,并对 208 名女性的高阴道拭子样本进行分析。使用 ELISA 分析高阴道拭子样本的人乳头瘤病毒抗原。本研究记录了 6.23% 的人乳头瘤病毒感染 患病率。在这项研究中,婚姻状况、吸烟、同居妻子的数量、安全套的使用和第一次性行为的年龄被确定为人乳头状瘤病毒感染的重要危险因素。这项研究发现尼日利亚索科托的人乳头瘤病毒感染负担较低。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定尼日利亚索科托州索科托选定医院的女性中与人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染相关的 患病率 和危险因素。这是必要的,因为 HPV 感染几乎是所有 宫颈癌 的主要原因,尽管 宫颈癌 在很大程度上是可以预防的,但 宫颈癌 仍然是发展中国家女性妇科癌症相关死亡的主要原因。
设计与对象
该研究旨在评估尼日利亚索科托州索科托选定医院的 208 名女性的 HPV 感染情况。该设计涉及使用问卷收集社会人口统计和危险因素数据,以及使用 ELISA 对高阴道拭子样本进行分析。
核心方法
使用 ELISA 分析大量阴道拭子样本中的 HPV 抗原。该研究没有报告任何额外的实验室或统计程序。
主要结果
该研究记录的女性 HPV 感染 患病率 为 6.23%。婚姻状况、吸烟、同居妻子的数量、安全套的使用和第一次性行为的年龄被确定为 HPV 感染的重要危险因素。
解释与创新
作者解释了研究结果,指出尼日利亚索科托的 HPV 感染负担较低,并确定了具体的风险因素。该研究的新颖性在于它确定了当地人群中的这些危险因素。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为尼日利亚索科托的 监测 和 HPV 感染及相关 宫颈癌 的预防策略提供信息。他们强调了解决已确定的风险因素以减轻 HPV 感染负担的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用单一诊断方法(ELISA)和小样本量,这可能会限制研究结果的普遍性。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Prevalence and Risk Factors Associated with Human Papilloma Virus Infection among Women Attending Selected Hospitals in Sokoto, Sokoto State, Nigeria

Authors: S Gambo
Original abstract (English)
Human Papilloma Virus (HPV) infection is the primary cause of virtually all cervical cancers. Cervical cancer despite being largely preventable is still the leading cause of gynaecological cancer related death among females in developing countries. This study was carried out to determine the prevalence and risk factors associated with Human Papilloma Virus infection among women attending selected hospitals in Sokoto, Sokoto State, Nigeria. This study involves the use of questionnaire to obtain data on socio-demographic and risk factors as well as the analysis of high vaginal swab obtained from 208 women. The high vaginal swab samples were analysed for Human Papilloma Virus antigen using ELISA. Prevalence of 6.23% of Human papilloma virus infection was recorded in this study. Marital status, cigarette smoking, number of Co-wives, use of condom and age at first sex were identified as significant risk factors for Human papilloma virus infection in this study. This study identified a low burden of Human papilloma virus infection in Sokoto, Nigeria.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to determine the prevalence and risk factors associated with Human Papilloma Virus (HPV) infection among women attending selected hospitals in Sokoto, Sokoto State, Nigeria. This was necessary because HPV infection is the primary cause of virtually all cervical cancers, and despite being largely preventable, cervical cancer remains the leading cause of gynaecological cancer-related death among females in developing countries.
Design and population
The study was conducted to assess HPV infection among 208 women attending selected hospitals in Sokoto, Sokoto State, Nigeria. The design involved the use of questionnaires to gather socio-demographic and risk factor data, as well as the analysis of high vaginal swab samples using ELISA.
Core methods
High vaginal swab samples were analyzed for HPV antigen using ELISA. The study did not report any additional laboratory or statistical procedures.
Main results
The study recorded a prevalence of 6.23% for HPV infection among the women. Marital status, cigarette smoking, number of co-wives, use of condoms, and age at first sex were identified as significant risk factors for HPV infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings, noting a low burden of HPV infection in Sokoto, Nigeria, and identified specific risk factors. The novelty of the study lies in its identification of these risk factors in the local population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and prevention strategies for HPV infection and associated cervical cancer in Sokoto, Nigeria. They highlight the importance of addressing identified risk factors to reduce the burden of HPV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of a single diagnostic method (ELISA) and the small sample size, which may limit the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

未来 40 年减轻赞比亚 宫颈癌 的负担:加强筛查工作的影响

Reducing the burden of cervical cancer in Zambia in the next 40 years: impact of enhanced screening efforts
作者： Marta Galanti, Teresa K. Yamana, Susan Michaels-Strasser, Maria Teresa Timoney, Bridget Lumbwe, Anthony Mutiti, Paul Kamfwa, Tafadzwa Dzinamarira, Jeffrey L. Shaman
摘要中文翻译背景:由于 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 患病率 高、疫苗接种 和筛查方面的差距以及人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV) 的交叉流行,赞比亚是世界上 宫颈癌 负担最重的国家之一。方法:在这项研究中,我们根据 ICAP 哥伦比亚和赞比亚卫生部设计的社区外展计划,评估了与 疫苗接种 努力一起扩大 HPV 筛查的潜在影响。该计划针对赞比亚的农村社区,提倡自我采集样本、现场 HPV 检测以及符合条件的当天 治疗,并将严重病例转介至中心地点。为了估计扩大筛查的影响,我们开发了一个基于个体的模型,通过年龄结构的性伴侣以及随后从感染到宫颈病变和 宫颈癌 的进展来捕获 HPV 传播。该模型根据赞比亚的人口特征、2022 HPV 患病率、2020 宫颈癌 发病率 进行了校准。我们比较了 5 种 40 年情景:无干预,仅 疫苗接种(9-14 岁女孩);疫苗接种 加上基于醋酸 (VIA) 筛查的目视检查,针对感染艾滋病毒的女性,以及将筛查范围扩大到所有女性的方案(每年约 200,000 次测试与约 400,000 次测试,每年增加 5%)。结果为 宫颈癌 发病率、干预方案的减少百分比以及避免的累积病例。通过使用引导置信区间 (CI) 进行重复随机模拟来量化不确定性。敏感性分析检查了替代的 HIV 发病率 轨迹并降低了筛查的可及性。结果:到 2060 年,与目前的比率相比,单独使用 HPV 疫苗接种 可使 宫颈癌 发病率 降低 47%(95% CI:39%-55%),但在前 20 年效果有限。增加人群筛查可加速影响:每年约 400,000 次检测,发病率 到 20 年下降 64%(95% CI:56%-70%),到 40 年下降 97%(95% CI:96%-98%),到 2040 年避免约 35,000 例病例,到 2060 年避免约 183,000 例病例,并达到世界卫生组织的目标到 2060 年消除阈值。探索性分析表明,为男孩接种疫苗具有额外的长期益处,特别是当 疫苗接种 覆盖率不理想时。结论:在赞比亚,短期内单独使用 疫苗接种 并不能显着减少 宫颈癌 负担,而将 疫苗接种 与基于社区的 HPV 筛查/治疗 结合起来可以在二十年内预防数以万计的癌症病例,并使消除指日可待。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了赞比亚因高 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 患病率、疫苗接种 和筛查方面的差距以及艾滋病毒流行而造成的高负担。
设计与对象
该研究利用 ICAP Columbia 和赞比亚卫生部设计的社区外展计划,评估了在赞比亚农村社区扩大 HPV 筛查以及 疫苗接种 工作的潜在影响。
核心方法
开发了一个基于个体的模型来估计 HPV 筛查和 疫苗接种 的影响,并根据赞比亚的人口概况和 2022 HPV 患病率 进行校准,结果包括 宫颈癌 发病率 和通过随机模拟和敏感性分析避免的病例。
主要结果
到 2060 年,与目前的比率相比,仅 HPV 疫苗接种 就可将 发病率 降低 47%(95% CI:39%-55%),但增加人群筛查会加速影响,到 20 年将 发病率 降低 64%(95% CI:56%-70%),到 20 年降低 97%(95% CI:95% CI: 96%-98%)到40年,在二十年内避免了数以万计的病例。
解释与创新
该研究表明,将 疫苗接种 与基于社区的 HPV 筛查相结合,可以在二十年内预防数以万计的癌症病例,并使赞比亚的消除目标触手可及。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为减少赞比亚 宫颈癌 负担的策略提供信息,特别是通过将 HPV 筛查与农村社区的 疫苗接种 工作相结合。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括对结果模型的依赖、需要进一步探索替代的 HIV 发病率 轨迹以及降低筛查可及性,对结果的可信度中等。

Reducing the burden of cervical cancer in Zambia in the next 40 years: impact of enhanced screening efforts

Authors: Marta Galanti, Teresa K. Yamana, Susan Michaels-Strasser, Maria Teresa Timoney, Bridget Lumbwe, Anthony Mutiti, Paul Kamfwa, Tafadzwa Dzinamarira, Jeffrey L. Shaman
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Zambia bears one of the world's highest cervical cancer burdens, driven by high human papillomavirus (HPV) prevalence, gaps in vaccination and screening, and the intersecting human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) epidemic. METHODS: In this study we assess the potential impact of scaling up HPV screening alongside vaccination efforts, based on a community outreach plan designed by ICAP Columbia and Zambia's Ministry of Health. The plan targets Zambia's rural communities and promotes self-collection of samples, on-site HPV testing, and same-day treatment when eligible, with referral to hub sites for severe cases. To estimate the expanded screening's impact, we developed an individual-based model capturing HPV transmission through age-structured sexual partnership, and subsequent progression from infection to cervical lesions and cervical cancer. The model was calibrated to Zambia's demographic profile, 2022 HPV prevalence, 2020 cervical cancer incidence. We compared five 40-year scenarios: no intervention, vaccination only (girls 9-14 years); vaccination plus visual inspection with acetic acid (VIA)-based screening targeted towards women living with HIV, and scenarios extending screening to all women ( ~ 200,000 vs ~400,000 tests/year with 5% annual increase). Outcomes were cervical cancer incidence, percent reduction across intervention scenarios, and cumulative cases averted. Uncertainty was quantified via repeated stochastic simulations with bootstrap confidence intervals (CIs). Sensitivity analyses examined alternative HIV incidence trajectories and reduced screening accessibility. RESULTS: By 2060, HPV vaccination alone reduces cervical cancer incidence by 47% (95% CI: 39%-55%) compared to current rates but has limited effect in the first 20 years. Adding population screening accelerates impact: under ~400,000 tests/year, incidence falls 64% (95% CI: 56%-70%) by year 20 and 97% (95% CI: 96%-98%) by year 40, averting ~35,000 cases by 2040 and ~183,000 by 2060 and reaching the World Health Organization elimination threshold by 2060. Exploratory analyses suggest additional long-term benefit from vaccinating boys, especially when vaccination coverage is suboptimal. CONCLUSIONS: In Zambia, vaccination alone will not meaningfully reduce cervical cancer burden in the near term, and pairing vaccination with a community-based HPV-screening/treatment could prevent tens of thousands of cancer cases within two decades and place elimination within reach.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the high burden of cervical cancer in Zambia, driven by high human papillomavirus (HPV) prevalence, gaps in vaccination and screening, and the HIV epidemic.
Design and population
The study assesses the potential impact of scaling up HPV screening alongside vaccination efforts in Zambia's rural communities, using a community outreach plan designed by ICAP Columbia and Zambia's Ministry of Health.
Core methods
An individual-based model was developed to estimate the impact of HPV screening and vaccination, calibrated to Zambia's demographic profile and 2022 HPV prevalence, with outcomes including cervical cancer incidence and cases averted through stochastic simulations and sensitivity analyses.
Main results
By 2060, HPV vaccination alone reduces cervical cancer incidence by 47% (95% CI: 39%-55%) compared to current rates, but adding population screening accelerates impact, reducing incidence by 64% (95% CI: 56%-70%) by year 20 and 97% (95% CI: 96%-98%) by year 40, averting tens of thousands of cases within two decades.
Interpretation and novelty
The study suggests that pairing vaccination with community-based HPV screening could prevent tens of thousands of cancer cases within two decades and place elimination within reach in Zambia.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could inform strategies to reduce cervical cancer burden in Zambia, particularly through the integration of HPV screening and vaccination efforts in rural communities.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the reliance on a model for outcomes and the need for further exploration of alternative HIV incidence trajectories and reduced screening accessibility, with moderate confidence in the results.

9 价 HPV 疫苗 在中国 16-32 岁女性中的免疫原性和急性反应原性:一项回顾性 队列研究 研究

Immunogenicity and acute reactogenicity of the 9-valent HPV vaccine in Chinese females aged 16–32 years: a retrospective cohort study
作者： Guang-Hui Song, Shao-Han Zou, Si-Tian Wei, 贝贝 张, Bei-Bei Zhang
摘要中文翻译背景:九价 人乳头瘤病毒 (9vHPV) 疫苗 可针对 HPV 相关疾病提供广泛的保护。然而,其多维安全性——整合急性血流动力学变化、注射部位疼痛、局部炎症反应和血管迷走反应——仍未完全表征。还缺乏经过验证的个性化风险预测工具。方法:这项回顾性 队列研究 研究分析了 103 名 16-32 岁女性的常规收集数据,这些女性在 2023 年 1 月至 2024 年 1 月期间完成了三剂 9vHPV 疫苗接种 计划(0-2-6 个月)。参与者被分为三个年龄组:16-20(n = 36)、21-26(n = 33) 和 27-32 岁 (n = 34)。在第三剂给药后一个月,通过所有九种 HPV 类型的几何平均滴度 (GMT) 和血清阳性率 (SPR) 评估免疫原性。安全性结果包括急性血流动力学变化(心率、血压)、注射部位疼痛(视觉模拟量表)、局部炎症反应、免疫后全身不良事件(AEFI)和血管迷走神经反应。应用了多维相关分析、K 均值聚类、时间扫描统计和基于探索性逻辑回归的风险预测模型。结果:所有九种 HPV 类型均达到 SPR ≥98.1%(范围 98.1-100%),GMT 范围为 2832 至 16,875 mIU/mL。对于多种类型,16-20 岁组的 GMT 显着高于 27-32 岁组(例如 HPV16:+61.1%,P = 0.010)。83.5% 的参与者出现急性血流动力学变化,其中心率升高,其中 98.1% 在 30 分钟内恢复正常。注射部位疼痛在 疫苗接种 后立即达到峰值 (VAS 3.8 ± 2.1),主要为轻度至中度 (87.4%)。心率升高与疼痛强度显着相关(Spearman ρ = 0.706,P 85 bpm 是血管迷走神经反应的独立预测因子(OR = 3.20,P = 0.008),而较年轻的年龄和较低的 BMI 与血管迷走神经敏感表型显着相关。结论:在这项初步回顾性 队列研究 中,9vHPV 疫苗 在 16-32 岁年龄范围内诱导强大的免疫原性,没有与 疫苗 相关的严重不良事件。急性血流动力学变化和血管迷走反应是短暂的,大多是轻微的,并且可能受到程序和自主因素的影响,而不是单独的 疫苗 成分的影响。本研究提供了 9vHPV 疫苗接种 后急性血流动力学、疼痛和局部炎症反应之间联系的初步综合证据,并引入了内部验证的探索性风险分层工具。在临床实施之前,需要在未选择的真实世界 队列研究 中进行外部验证。

还缺乏经过验证的个性化风险预测工具。

主要结果
对于多种类型,16-20 岁组的 GMT 显着高于 27-32 岁组(例如 HPV16:+61.1%,P = 0.010)。

在这项初步回顾性 队列研究 中,9vHPV 疫苗 在 16-32 岁年龄范围内诱导了强大的免疫原性,没有出现与 疫苗 相关的严重不良事件。

Immunogenicity and acute reactogenicity of the 9-valent HPV vaccine in Chinese females aged 16–32 years: a retrospective cohort study

Authors: Guang-Hui Song, Shao-Han Zou, Si-Tian Wei, 贝贝 张, Bei-Bei Zhang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The nine-valent human papillomavirus (9vHPV) vaccine offers broad protection against HPV-related diseases. However, its multidimensional safety profile-integrating acute hemodynamic changes, injection-site pain, local inflammatory reactions, and vasovagal responses-remains incompletely characterized. Validated tools for individualised risk prediction are also lacking. METHODS: This retrospective cohort study analysed routinely collected data from 103 females aged 16-32 years who completed the three-dose 9vHPV vaccination schedule (0-2-6 months) between January 2023 and January 2024. Participants were stratified into three age groups: 16-20 (n = 36), 21-26 (n = 33), and 27-32 years (n = 34). Immunogenicity was assessed by geometric mean titers (GMT) and seropositive rates (SPR) for all nine HPV types one month after dose three. Safety outcomes included acute hemodynamic changes (heart rate, blood pressure), injection-site pain (Visual Analog Scale), local inflammatory reactions, systemic adverse events following immunisation (AEFI), and vasovagal reactions. Multidimensional correlation analyses, K-means clustering, temporal scan statistics, and an exploratory logistic regression-based risk prediction models were applied. RESULTS: All nine HPV types achieved SPR ≥98.1% (range 98.1-100%), with GMT ranging from 2832 to 16,875 mIU/mL. The 16-20 years group showed significantly higher GMTs than the 27-32 years group for multiple types (e.g., HPV16: +61.1%, P = 0.010). Acute hemodynamic changes included heart rate elevation in 83.5% of participants, with 98.1% normalising within 30 min. Injection-site pain peaked immediately post-vaccination (VAS 3.8 ± 2.1) and was predominantly mild to moderate (87.4%). Heart rate elevation correlated significantly with pain intensity (Spearman ρ = 0.706, P 85 bpm was an independent predictor of vasovagal reactions (OR = 3.20, P = 0.008), while younger age and lower BMI were significantly associated with the vasovagal-sensitive phenotype. CONCLUSIONS: In this preliminary retrospective cohort, the 9vHPV vaccine induced robust immunogenicity across the 16-32 years age range with no vaccine-related serious adverse events. Acute hemodynamic changes and vasovagal reactions were transient and mostly mild, and were likely influenced by procedural and autonomic factors rather than vaccine components alone. This study provides preliminary integrated evidence linking acute hemodynamic, pain, and local inflammatory responses after 9vHPV vaccination and introduces an internally validated, exploratory risk stratification tool. External validation in unselected real-world cohorts is required before clinical implementation.

南非夸祖鲁纳塔尔省青春期前女孩从二价 HPV 疫苗接种 转换为非二价 HPV 疫苗接种 的健康经济影响和成本阈值:考虑艾滋病毒流行的数学模型分析。

Health Economic Impact and Cost Threshold for Switching From Bivalent to Nonavalent HPV Vaccination for Preadolescent Girls in KwaZulu Natal, South Africa: A Mathematical Modeling Analysis Accounting for the HIV Epidemic.
作者： Christine L Hathaway, Jacinda Tran, Cari van Schalkwyk, Louise Kuhn, Darcy W Rao, Ruanne V Barnabas, Monisha Sharma
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒 二价 (2vHPV) 和非价 (9vHPV) 疫苗 分别可预防导致 宫颈癌 的 70% 和 90% 的 HPV 类型。南非指南建议为 9-14 岁的女孩接种 1 剂 2vHPV 疫苗。将建议改为 9vHPV 可以避免更多的 宫颈癌 负担,但 9vHPV 在南非的成本效益和成本阈值尚不清楚。方法:使用参数化于南非夸祖鲁-纳塔尔省的经过验证的区室 HPV 和 HIV 传播 模型,我们模拟了 100 年来 2vHPV 和 9vHPV 实施的场景,其中 2 剂(72% 覆盖率)和 1 剂(80% 和 90% 覆盖率)具有终生或减弱的单剂 疫苗 功效。我们评估了 宫颈癌 病例和避免的死亡。我们还使用支付意愿阈值从 500 美元/避免的伤残调整生命年 (DALY) 到 3015 美元/避免的 DALY(2023 美元)估计 9vHPV 的最大成本/剂量具有成本效益。我们还评估了 9vHPV 按当前成本(110 美元/剂)与 2vHPV(9.23 美元/剂)相比的增量成本效益比。结果:单剂 9vHPV 覆盖率达到 80%,与 2vHPV 相比,可避免多 7.9% 的 宫颈癌 病例和多 6.4% 的死亡(终身疗效)。具有成本效益的 9vHPV 最高价格(终身疗效,80% 覆盖率)范围从 15 美元(500 美元/DALY 避免阈值)到 45 美元/剂量(3015 美元/DALY 避免阈值)。按照目前的市场成本,覆盖率达 90% 的单剂 9vHPV 超过了常用的成本效益阈值,增量成本效益比为 10,480 美元/避免 DALY(终身功效)和 8806 美元/避免 DALY(功效减弱)。结论:与 2vHPV 相比,实施 9vHPV 可以更大程度地减少 宫颈癌 负担,但需要降低 疫苗 成本才能使 9vHPV 在夸祖鲁-纳塔尔省具有成本效益。

我们还使用支付意愿阈值从 500 美元/避免的伤残调整生命年 (DALY) 到 3015 美元/避免的 DALY(2023 美元)估计 9vHPV 的最大成本/剂量具有成本效益。

Health Economic Impact and Cost Threshold for Switching From Bivalent to Nonavalent HPV Vaccination for Preadolescent Girls in KwaZulu Natal, South Africa: A Mathematical Modeling Analysis Accounting for the HIV Epidemic.

Authors: Christine L Hathaway, Jacinda Tran, Cari van Schalkwyk, Louise Kuhn, Darcy W Rao, Ruanne V Barnabas, Monisha Sharma
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Human papillomavirus bivalent (2vHPV) and nonavalent (9vHPV) vaccines protect against HPV types causing 70% and 90% of cervical cancers, respectively. South African guidelines recommend vaccinating girls ages 9-14 with 1 dose of 2vHPV. Switching recommendations to 9vHPV could avert more cervical cancer burden, but the cost-effectiveness and cost-threshold of 9vHPV in South Africa is unknown. METHODS: Using a validated compartmental HPV and HIV transmission model parameterized to KwaZulu-Natal, South Africa, we simulated scenarios of 2vHPV and 9vHPV implementation over 100 years with 2 doses (72% coverage) and 1 dose (80% and 90% coverage) with lifelong or waning single-dose vaccine efficacy. We evaluated cervical cancer cases and deaths averted. We also estimated the maximum cost/dose for 9vHPV to be cost-effective using willingness-to-pay thresholds from $500/disability-adjusted life-year (DALY) averted to $3015/DALY averted (2023 USD). We also assessed the incremental cost-effectiveness ratios of 9vHPV at current costs ($110/dose) compared with 2vHPV ($9.23/dose). RESULTS: Single-dose 9vHPV at 80% coverage could avert 7.9% more cervical cancer cases and 6.4% more deaths than 2vHPV (lifelong efficacy). The maximum 9vHPV price to be cost-effective (lifelong efficacy, 80% coverage) ranged from $15 ($500/DALY averted threshold) to $45/dose ($3015/DALY averted threshold). At current market costs, single-dose 9vHPV at 90% coverage exceeds commonly used cost-effective thresholds with incremental cost-effectiveness ratios of $10,480/DALY averted (lifelong efficacy) and $8806/DALY averted (waning efficacy). CONCLUSIONS: Implementing 9vHPV can result in a greater reduction in cervical cancer burden than 2vHPV, but vaccine costs need to be reduced for 9vHPV to be cost-effective in KwaZulu-Natal.

确定孟加拉国提高 HPV 疫苗 意识的挑战和机遇:定性研究

Determining the Challenges and Opportunities for Increasing HPV Vaccine Awareness in Bangladesh: A Qualitative Study
作者： Shabnam Mostari, Aysharza Biswas Puja, Barnil Das Nakshi, Adiba Momtahin, Mohammad Rakibul Hasan, Mohammad Raihanul Hoque, Nusrat Parvin Mohona, Nafiz Farhan, Afela Jabin, Nick P Townsend
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景和目标 宫颈癌 仍然是低收入和中等收入国家的一个主要公共卫生问题,其中包括孟加拉国,这些国家对 宫颈癌 和 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 的认识有限。为了减轻疾病负担,孟加拉国于 2023 年针对 10-14 岁女孩启动了免费 HPV 疫苗接种 计划。然而,由于覆盖范围有限、意识低下和文化阻力,覆盖率仍然较低。本研究探讨了影响孟加拉国 HPV 疫苗 认知和使用的挑战和机遇。方法 完成了总共 25 名关键知情人访谈 (KII) 和深度访谈 (IDI),其中 9 名 KII 和 16 名 IDI,其中包括一名扩大免疫规划 (EPI) 项目经理 (n = 2);一名民间外科医生(n = 1);社区卫生工作者(n = 3);宗教领袖(n = 3);父母(n = 4);教师(n = 6);以及来自达卡、吉大港和考克斯巴扎尔地区的学生 (n = 6)。所有访谈均逐字记录,并使用反思性主题分析(Braun & Clarke)进行分析,以确定与 疫苗 认知和接受的障碍和促进因素相关的关键主题。结果 我们确定了 4 个主要主题和 12 个子主题。调查结果显示,青春期女孩对 宫颈癌 症状、预防以及 HPV 疫苗接种 的重要性严重缺乏了解。社区卫生工作者、家长和教师(尤其是农村地区)的负面看法进一步阻碍了 疫苗 的采用。宗教和文化问题是接受的主要障碍。参与者建议,组织教师家长会、校本健康教育课程和宣传活动有助于提高 疫苗 的接受度和理解。结论 不同利益相关群体对 HPV 疫苗接种 的了解和看法各不相同,影响了 疫苗 的整体接受度。量身定制的、基于社区的教育干预措施对于消除误解并提高青少年和主要社区影响者的认识至关重要。加强多部门合作可以提高 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率并支持孟加拉国的 宫颈癌 预防工作。

问题与背景
调查结果显示,青春期女孩对 宫颈癌 症状、预防以及 HPV 疫苗接种 的重要性严重缺乏了解。
设计与对象
参与者建议,组织教师家长会、校本健康教育课程和宣传活动有助于提高 疫苗 的接受度和理解。
核心方法
方法 完成了总共 25 名关键知情人访谈 (KII) 和深度访谈 (IDI),其中 9 名 KII 和 16 名 IDI,其中包括一名扩大免疫规划 (EPI) 项目经理 (n = 2);一名民间外科医生(n = 1);社区卫生工作者(n = 3);宗教领袖(n = 3);父母(n = 4);教师(n = 6);以及来自达卡、吉大港和考克斯巴扎尔地区的学生 (n = 6)。
主要结果

解释与创新
结论 不同利益相关群体对 HPV 疫苗接种 的了解和看法各不相同,影响了 疫苗 的整体接受度。
科研与公卫意义
加强多部门合作可以提高 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率并支持孟加拉国的 宫颈癌 预防工作。
局限与证据强度
量身定制的、基于社区的教育干预措施对于消除误解并提高青少年和主要社区影响者的认识至关重要。

Determining the Challenges and Opportunities for Increasing HPV Vaccine Awareness in Bangladesh: A Qualitative Study

Authors: Shabnam Mostari, Aysharza Biswas Puja, Barnil Das Nakshi, Adiba Momtahin, Mohammad Rakibul Hasan, Mohammad Raihanul Hoque, Nusrat Parvin Mohona, Nafiz Farhan, Afela Jabin, Nick P Townsend
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background and Aims Cervical cancer remains a major public health issue in low‐ and middle‐income countries, including Bangladesh, where awareness of cervical cancer and the human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine is limited. To reduce the disease burden, Bangladesh launched a free HPV vaccination program for girls ages 10–14 in 2023. However, coverage remains low due to limited reach, low awareness, and cultural resistance. This study examined the challenges and opportunities that influence HPV vaccine awareness and uptake in Bangladesh. Methods A total of 25 Key Informant Interviews (KIIs) and In‐Depth Interviews (IDIs) were completed, comprising 9 KIIs and 16 IDIs, including an Expanded Program on Immunization (EPI) program manager (n = 2); a Civil Surgeon (n = 1); community health workers (n = 3); religious leaders (n = 3); parents (n = 4); teachers (n = 6); and students (n = 6) from Dhaka, Chittagong, and Cox's Bazar districts. All interviews were transcribed verbatim and analyzed using reflexive thematic analysis (Braun & Clarke) to identify key themes related to barriers and facilitators of vaccine awareness and acceptance. Results We identified four major themes and 12 sub‐themes. Findings revealed a significant lack of understanding among adolescent girls about cervical cancer symptoms, prevention, and the importance of HPV vaccination. Negative perceptions among community health workers, parents, and teachers, particularly in rural areas, further hindered vaccine uptake. Religious and cultural concerns were major barriers to acceptance. Participants suggested that organizing teacher–parent meetings, school‐based health education sessions, and awareness campaigns could help increase vaccine acceptance and understanding. Conclusions Knowledge and perceptions about HPV vaccination varied across stakeholder groups, influencing overall vaccine acceptance. Tailored, community‐based educational interventions are crucial to address misconceptions and enhance awareness among adolescents and key community influencers. Strengthening multi‐sectoral collaboration could increase HPV vaccination coverage and support cervical cancer prevention efforts in Bangladesh.

计算发现 Lissachatina fulica 粘液中潜在的 HPV E6 和 E7 结合剂用于口腔鳞状细胞癌治疗

Computational discovery of potential HPV E6 and E7 binders from Lissachatina fulica slime for oral squamous cell carcinoma therapeutics
作者： K. T. Shanid, M. Anju, Ravindra Kumar
摘要中文翻译背景:高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关口腔鳞状细胞癌 (OSCC) 主要由病毒癌蛋白 E6 和 E7 驱动,它们通过肿瘤抑制因子 p53 和视网膜母细胞瘤蛋白的失活促进恶性进展。这项研究调查了来自 Lissachatina fulica 粘液的 26 种生物活性代谢物,作为计算预测的 HPV16 E6 和 E7 蛋白的结合物。在已确定的代谢物中,与两种参考化合物姜黄烯和美法仑一起进一步评估了药物相似性、药代动力学和毒性特征。对 19 种满足筛选标准的化合物进行针对 HPV16 E6 和 E7 蛋白的分子对接。通过分子动力学模拟进一步评估排名最高的复合物。苯酚、2,4-双(1,1-二甲基乙基)-与HPV16 E6形成最稳定的复合物,而(16R,17S)-10,10,16,17-四甲基-6-苯基-和calophyllolide与HPV16 E7表现出最大的稳定性,突出显示L. fulica粘液作为候选分子的推定来源,用于针对HPV相关OSCC的进一步实验研究。方法:使用 SwissADME 和 ProTox-3.0 对从 L. fulica 粘液中鉴定出的 26 种代谢物进行药物相似性、药代动力学和毒性特性筛选。使用 AutoDock Vina 以姜黄烯和美法仑作为参考化合物,将满足筛选标准的 19 种化合物与 HPV16 蛋白进行对接。使用带有 CHARMM27 力场的 GROMACS 软件包对排名最高的蛋白质-配体复合物进行 500 ns 扩展分子动力学模拟。轨迹分析包括均方根偏差 (RMSD)、均方根波动 (RMSF)、回转半径 (Rg)、溶剂可及表面积 (SASA) 和氢键分析。

Computational discovery of potential HPV E6 and E7 binders from Lissachatina fulica slime for oral squamous cell carcinoma therapeutics

Authors: K. T. Shanid, M. Anju, Ravindra Kumar
Original abstract (English)
CONTEXT: High-risk human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated oral squamous cell carcinoma (OSCC) is primarily driven by the viral oncoproteins E6 and E7 which promote malignant progression through inactivation of the tumour suppressors p53 and retinoblastoma protein. This study investigated 26 bioactive metabolites from the slime of Lissachatina fulica as computationally predicted binders of HPV16 E6 and E7 proteins. Among identified metabolites, the drug-likeness, pharmacokinetic, toxicity profiles were further evaluated along with two reference compounds Curcumene and Melphalan. Nineteen compounds satisfied the screening criteria were subjected to molecular docking against HPV16 E6 and E7 proteins. The highest-ranking complexes were further evaluated by molecular dynamics simulations. Phenol, 2,4-bis(1,1-dimethylethyl)- formed the most stable complex with HPV16 E6 whereas (16R,17S)-10,10,16,17-tetramethyl-6-phenyl- and calophyllolide exhibited the greatest stability with HPV16 E7 highlighting L. fulica slime as a putative source of candidate molecules for further experimental investigation against HPV-associated OSCC. METHODS: Twenty-six metabolites identified from L. fulica slime were screened for drug-likeness, pharmacokinetic and toxicity properties using SwissADME and ProTox-3.0. Nineteen compounds satisfying the screening criteria were docked against HPV16 proteins using AutoDock Vina with Curcumene and Melphalan as reference compounds. The highest-ranked protein-ligand complexes were subjected to 500 ns extended molecular dynamics simulations using the GROMACS package with the CHARMM27 force field. Trajectory analyses included root mean square deviation (RMSD), root mean square fluctuation (RMSF), radius of gyration (Rg), solvent-accessible surface area (SASA), and hydrogen-bond analysis.

临床检测来源与医院宫颈 HPV 基因分型中观察到的 HPV 负担相关:一项针对 36,362 名女性的回顾性研究

Clinical testing source is associated with observed HPV burden in hospital-based cervical HPV genotyping: a retrospective study of 36,362 women
作者： Jian-min Ren, Xiao-Yao Zhang, Zheng-Yun Liu, Miao-Fang Ye, Li-hong Pei, Wei-ping Jiang, Xiao-peng Liu, Qian-wen Yang, Cai-lian Fang, Wei-Bo Mao
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基于医院的宫颈 HPV 基因分型数据集包括通过不同临床途径进行测试的女性,因此观察到的 HPV 负担可能因来源人群而异。我们量化了特定来源的 HPV 负荷并评估了细胞学相关的背景。
设计与对象
我们对 36,362 名女性进行了一项单中心回顾性观察研究,每名女性进行了一次保留指数 HPV 检测。所有患者均接受统一的PCR-反向斑点杂交23型宫颈HPV基因分型,并按临床检测来源分类为体检参加者或妇科门诊患者。
核心方法
主要结果是任何 HPV 阳性、12 型致癌 HPV 阳性和多基因型感染。源组 患病率 比率根据年龄组和测试日历季度进行调整。细胞学在预先指定的±3天窗口内进行关联。
主要结果
The cohort included 9,969 health examination attendees and 26,393 gynecology outpatients; cytology was linked for 34,049 women (93.6%). Compared with health examination attendees, gynecology outpatients had higher any HPV positivity (28.7% vs 17.2%; adjusted PR, 1.61; 95% CI, 1.54-1.69), 12-type carcinogenic HPV positivity (19.4% vs 10.7%; adjusted PR, 1.75; 95% CI, 1.65-1.86), and multiple-genotype infection (9.2% vs 3.9%; adjusted PR, 2.23; 95% CI, 2.01-2.48).
解释与创新
临床检测来源与统一医院实验室工作流程中观察到的 HPV 负担存在显着差异相关,这凸显了在解释基于医院的 HPV 估计值时临床环境和来源人群的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果告诫不要在不考虑临床环境和来源人群的情况下解释基于医院的 HPV 估计值,这可能会影响观察到的 HPV 负担并为更准确的 监测 和 诊断 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
在排除 HPV/STI 相关临床标签子集以及限制具有相关细胞学的女性后,来源组对比有所减弱,但仍保持相同方向。由于回顾性设计和潜在的残留混杂因素,证据是中等的。

Clinical testing source is associated with observed HPV burden in hospital-based cervical HPV genotyping: a retrospective study of 36,362 women

Authors: Jian-min Ren, Xiao-Yao Zhang, Zheng-Yun Liu, Miao-Fang Ye, Li-hong Pei, Wei-ping Jiang, Xiao-peng Liu, Qian-wen Yang, Cai-lian Fang, Wei-Bo Mao
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Hospital-based cervical HPV genotyping datasets include women tested through different clinical pathways, so observed HPV burden may vary by source population. We quantified source-specific HPV burden and evaluated cytology-linked context. METHODS: We conducted a single-center retrospective observational study of 36,362 women with one retained index HPV test per woman. All underwent uniform 23-type cervical HPV genotyping by PCR-reverse dot blot and were classified as health examination attendees or gynecology outpatients by clinical testing source. Primary outcomes were any HPV positivity, 12-type carcinogenic HPV positivity, and multiple-genotype infection. Source-group prevalence ratios were adjusted for age group and calendar quarter of testing. Cytology was linked within a prespecified ±3-day window. RESULTS: The cohort included 9,969 health examination attendees and 26,393 gynecology outpatients; cytology was linked for 34,049 women (93.6%). Compared with health examination attendees, gynecology outpatients had higher any HPV positivity (28.7% vs 17.2%; adjusted PR, 1.61; 95% CI, 1.54-1.69), 12-type carcinogenic HPV positivity (19.4% vs 10.7%; adjusted PR, 1.75; 95% CI, 1.65-1.86), and multiple-genotype infection (9.2% vs 3.9%; adjusted PR, 2.23; 95% CI, 2.01-2.48). HPV16/18 and HPV52/58 positivity also varied across cytology strata. Source-group contrasts were attenuated but remained in the same direction after exclusion of an HPV/STI-related clinical-label subset and after restriction to women with linked cytology. CONCLUSIONS: Clinical testing source was associated with substantial differences in observed HPV burden within a uniform hospital laboratory workflow, highlighting the importance of clinical setting and source population when hospital-based HPV estimates are interpreted.

Question and background
Hospital-based cervical HPV genotyping datasets include women tested through different clinical pathways, so observed HPV burden may vary by source population. We quantified source-specific HPV burden and evaluated cytology-linked context.
Design and population
We conducted a single-center retrospective observational study of 36,362 women with one retained index HPV test per woman. All underwent uniform 23-type cervical HPV genotyping by PCR-reverse dot blot and were classified as health examination attendees or gynecology outpatients by clinical testing source.
Core methods
Primary outcomes were any HPV positivity, 12-type carcinogenic HPV positivity, and multiple-genotype infection. Source-group prevalence ratios were adjusted for age group and calendar quarter of testing. Cytology was linked within a prespecified ±3-day window.
Main results
The cohort included 9,969 health examination attendees and 26,393 gynecology outpatients; cytology was linked for 34,049 women (93.6%). Compared with health examination attendees, gynecology outpatients had higher any HPV positivity (28.7% vs 17.2%; adjusted PR, 1.61; 95% CI, 1.54-1.69), 12-type carcinogenic HPV positivity (19.4% vs 10.7%; adjusted PR, 1.75; 95% CI, 1.65-1.86), and multiple-genotype infection (9.2% vs 3.9%; adjusted PR, 2.23; 95% CI, 2.01-2.48).
Interpretation and novelty
Clinical testing source was associated with substantial differences in observed HPV burden within a uniform hospital laboratory workflow, highlighting the importance of clinical setting and source population when hospital-based HPV estimates are interpreted.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution against interpreting hospital-based HPV estimates without considering the clinical setting and source population, which may influence observed HPV burden and inform more accurate surveillance and diagnosis strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
Source-group contrasts were attenuated but remained in the same direction after exclusion of an HPV/STI-related clinical-label subset and after restriction to women with linked cytology. The evidence is moderate due to the retrospective design and potential for residual confounding.

人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 低收入和中等收入国家感染艾滋病毒的少女和年轻妇女的覆盖率:系统评价和荟萃分析

Human papillomavirus vaccination coverage among adolescent girls and young women living with HIV in low- and middle-income countries: A systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Ifeyinwa Lilian Ezenwosu, John Olajide Olawepo, Jane Nnedinma Ohaekweiro, Peter Ezeogo Ihere, Aneke Emeka John, Justus Uchenna Onu, Oluchukwu Perpetual Okeke, Olajide Odunayo Sobande, Emeka John Aneke
摘要中文翻译简介:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 引起的癌症对低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC) 感染艾滋病毒 (AGYWLHIV) 的少女和年轻女性影响尤为严重,但该亚群中的 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率仍然不理想。这项系统回顾和荟萃分析估计了中低收入国家 AGYWLHIV 中 HPV 疫苗接种 的汇总 患病率 覆盖率。方法:在 PubMed、Scopus、护理和联合健康文献累积索引 (CINAHL) 和 Web of Science 中对 2014 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间发表的观察性研究进行系统检索。使用随机效应荟萃分析来估计 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率的汇总 患病率。按年龄组和 疫苗 类型进行亚组分析,同时对 HPV 疫苗接种 递送平台进行描述性总结。还进行了发表偏倚和敏感性分析,并使用 I2 统计量评估了统计异质性。结果:荟萃分析共纳入 8 项研究,涉及 1,059 例 AGYWLHIV。根据随机效应模型,中低收入国家 AGYWLHIV 中的汇总 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率为 28.6%(95% CI:20.3%-38.7%),各研究之间存在显着异质性(I2 = 87.0%,p < 0.0001)。按年龄进行的探索性亚组分析得出,9-14 岁参与者(1 项研究;n = 31)中 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率为 90.3%(95% CI:74.2%-98.0%),15-26 岁参与者中 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率为 19.2%(95% CI:11.0%-31.4%)(4 项研究;n = 250)。探索性描述性研究结果表明,HPV 疫苗接种 是通过卫生设施、学校、社区外展以及这些方法的组合来传播的。然而,分母的差异限制了交付平台之间的直接比较。结论:来自中低收入国家研究的现有证据表明 AGYWLHIV 中 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率较低。将 HPV 疫苗接种 纳入常规 HIV 护理可能是一种潜在有用的策略,可改善 AGYWLHIV 患者的获取机会并减少错失的 疫苗接种 机会,并且应与强有力的政治承诺和多部门合作支持的更广泛努力一起进行进一步评估。普洛斯彼罗编号:CRD420251068971。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
在低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC),人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 引起的癌症对感染艾滋病毒 (AGYWLHIV) 的少女和年轻女性的影响尤为严重,但该亚群中的 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率仍然不理想。
证据基础与方法
这项系统回顾和荟萃分析估计了中低收入国家 AGYWLHIV 中 HPV 疫苗接种 的汇总 患病率 覆盖率。
共识与结论
荟萃分析共纳入 8 项研究,涉及 1,059 例 AGYWLHIV。
争议与缺口
还进行了发表偏倚和敏感性分析,并使用 I2 统计量评估了统计异质性。
科研与实践启示
按年龄组和 疫苗 类型进行亚组分析,同时对 HPV 疫苗接种 递送平台进行描述性总结。

Human papillomavirus vaccination coverage among adolescent girls and young women living with HIV in low- and middle-income countries: A systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Ifeyinwa Lilian Ezenwosu, John Olajide Olawepo, Jane Nnedinma Ohaekweiro, Peter Ezeogo Ihere, Aneke Emeka John, Justus Uchenna Onu, Oluchukwu Perpetual Okeke, Olajide Odunayo Sobande, Emeka John Aneke
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Human Papilloma Virus (HPV)-attributable cancers disproportionately affect adolescent girls and young women living with HIV (AGYWLHIV) in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs), yet HPV vaccination coverage in this subpopulation remains suboptimal. This systematic review and meta-analysis estimated the pooled prevalence of HPV vaccination coverage among AGYWLHIV in LMICs. METHODS: A systematic search was conducted in PubMed, Scopus, Cumulative Index to Nursing and Allied Health Literature (CINAHL), and Web of Science for observational studies published between January 2014 and December 2025. A random-effects meta-analysis was used to estimate the pooled prevalence of HPV vaccination coverage. Subgroup analyses by age group and vaccine type were conducted, while HPV vaccination delivery platforms were summarised descriptively. Publication bias and sensitivity analyses were also performed, and statistical heterogeneity was evaluated using the I2 statistic. RESULTS: A total of 8 studies involving 1,059 AGYWLHIV were included in the meta-analysis. The pooled HPV vaccination coverage among AGYWLHIV in LMICs was 28.6% (95% CI: 20.3%-38.7%) based on a random-effects model, with substantial heterogeneity across studies (I2 = 87.0%, p < 0.0001). Exploratory subgroup analysis by age yielded HPV vaccination coverage of 90.3% (95% CI: 74.2%-98.0%) among participants aged 9-14 years (1 study; n = 31) and 19.2% (95% CI: 11.0%-31.4%) among those aged 15-26 years (4 studies; n = 250). Exploratory descriptive findings indicated that HPV vaccination was delivered through health facilities, schools, community outreach, and combinations of these approaches. However, differences in denominators limited direct comparisons between delivery platforms. CONCLUSION: The available evidence from studies conducted in LMICs indicates low HPV vaccination coverage among AGYWLHIV. Integrating HPV vaccination into routine HIV care may represent a potentially useful strategy to improve access and reduce missed vaccination opportunities among AGYWLHIV and should be further evaluated alongside broader efforts supported by strong political commitment and multisectoral collaboration. PROSPERO Number: CRD420251068971.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Human Papilloma Virus (HPV)-attributable cancers disproportionately affect adolescent girls and young women living with HIV (AGYWLHIV) in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs), yet HPV vaccination coverage in this subpopulation remains suboptimal.
Evidence base and method
This systematic review and meta-analysis estimated the pooled prevalence of HPV vaccination coverage among AGYWLHIV in LMICs.
Consensus and conclusions
A total of 8 studies involving 1,059 AGYWLHIV were included in the meta-analysis.
Controversies and gaps
Publication bias and sensitivity analyses were also performed, and statistical heterogeneity was evaluated using the I2 statistic.
Research and practice implications
Subgroup analyses by age group and vaccine type were conducted, while HPV vaccination delivery platforms were summarised descriptively.

县医院耳鼻喉科患者人乳头瘤病毒知识及接受度

Human Papillomavirus Knowledge and Vaccine Receptiveness of County Hospital Otolaryngology Patients
作者： Sophia Chen, Lauran K. Evans, Matthew Ern Lin, Salmaan Sayeed, Maie A. St. John, Peter S. Han, Dinesh Khatri Chhetri
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 本研究评估县医院耳鼻喉科诊所患者的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 知识和 疫苗 接受度。方法 在 2023 年 9 月至 2026 年 7 月期间向成年患者提供调查。调查以英语和西班牙语进行,评估了 HPV 的认知度、与宫颈癌和口咽癌相关的知识以及接受 HPV 疫苗接种 的意愿。还收集了人口统计数据,包括性别、年龄、种族/民族、疫苗接种 状态和 HPV 相关疾病。卡方和费舍尔精确检验比较了男性和女性之间的差异。结果 201 名患者完成了调查问卷,其中 86 名患者获得了人口统计数据。大多数患者是讲西班牙语的女性、西班牙裔,且未接种 HPV 疫苗。52.2% (105/201) 听说过 HPV,而 25.1% (50/199) 知道 HPV 可能导致口咽癌。近五分之三的人没有听说过 HPV 疫苗 (60.2%,121/201),但如果提供的话,33.0% (64/194) 愿意接受。与男性相比,从统计数据来看,女性更有可能听说过 HPV、了解其与宫颈癌和口咽癌的关联,并相信 HPV 疫苗 的功效。如果提供 疫苗,则接受 疫苗 的意愿不存在性别差异。结论 该县耳鼻咽喉科门诊人群HPV健康素养和疫苗接种率较低。尽管如此,如果提供的话,这些患者非常愿意接受 疫苗。患者教育和耳鼻喉科就诊时的 HPV 疫苗接种 护理点代表了一种高度可行、可扩展的干预措施,可以绕过初级保健障碍并减少 HPV 相关疾病。证据水平 不适用

证据基础与方法
方法 对 2023 年 9 月至 2026 年 7 月期间的成年患者进行调查。
共识与结论
与男性相比,从统计数据来看,女性更有可能听说过 HPV、了解其与宫颈癌和口咽癌的关联,并相信 HPV 疫苗 的功效。

科研与实践启示
患者教育和耳鼻喉科就诊时的 HPV 疫苗接种 护理点代表了一种高度可行、可扩展的干预措施,可以绕过初级保健障碍并减少 HPV 相关疾病。

Human Papillomavirus Knowledge and Vaccine Receptiveness of County Hospital Otolaryngology Patients

Authors: Sophia Chen, Lauran K. Evans, Matthew Ern Lin, Salmaan Sayeed, Maie A. St. John, Peter S. Han, Dinesh Khatri Chhetri
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Objective This study evaluates human papillomavirus (HPV) knowledge and vaccine receptiveness among patients at an otolaryngology clinic in a county hospital setting. Methods Surveys were provided to adult patients between September 2023 and July 2026. Surveys were offered in English and Spanish and assessed awareness of HPV, knowledge of association with cervical and oropharyngeal cancers, and willingness to undergo HPV vaccination. Demographic data including sex, age, race/ethnicity, vaccination status, and HPV‐related disease were also collected. Chi‐squared and Fisher's exact test compared differences between males and females. Results Two hundred and one patients completed the questionnaire with demographic data available in 86. Most were Spanish‐speaking, female, Hispanic, and not vaccinated against HPV. 52.2% (105/201) had heard of HPV while 25.1% (50/199) knew HPV could cause oropharyngeal cancer. Almost three‐fifths had not heard of the HPV vaccine (60.2%, 121/201), but if offered, 33.0% (64/194) were open to receiving it. Compared with males, females were statistically more likely to have heard of HPV, understand its association with cervical and oropharyngeal cancer, and believe in the HPV vaccine's efficacy. There were no statistical differences by sex in willingness to receive the vaccine, if offered. Conclusion There is low HPV health literacy and vaccination rates among this county otolaryngology clinic population. Despite this, these patients are highly receptive to receiving the vaccine if offered. Patient education and point‐of‐care HPV vaccination at otolaryngology visits represent a highly feasible, scalable intervention to bypass primary care barriers and reduce HPV‐related disease. Level of Evidence N/A
View review five-element analysis

Evidence base and method
Methods Surveys were provided to adult patients between September 2023 and July 2026.
Consensus and conclusions
Compared with males, females were statistically more likely to have heard of HPV, understand its association with cervical and oropharyngeal cancer, and believe in the HPV vaccine's efficacy.
Controversies and gaps
Despite this, these patients are highly receptive to receiving the vaccine if offered.
Research and practice implications
Patient education and point‐of‐care HPV vaccination at otolaryngology visits represent a highly feasible, scalable intervention to bypass primary care barriers and reduce HPV‐related disease.

人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 和 治疗 用于皮肤疾病:无法预防。

Human papillomavirus vaccination as treatment for cutaneous diseases: beyond prevention.
作者： Junhua Qi, Gang Nie
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 会引起从寻常疣到皮肤鳞状细胞癌等皮肤疾病。尽管 HPV 疫苗 被许可用于预防肛门生殖器感染,但它们越来越多地用于治疗已确定的 HPV 相关皮肤病变。这篇叙述性综述评估了 HPV 疫苗接种 对顽固性疣、非黑色素瘤皮肤癌和免疫功能低下人群的治疗作用。唯一的大型随机对照试验显示对肛门生殖器疣没有任何益处,所有积极证据都来自小样本和异质设计的低质量研究。发表偏见是可能的。主要挑战包括缺乏对致癌 β-HPV 亚型的覆盖以及治疗的标签外性质。未来的方向需要针对皮肤 HPV 类型的下一代 疫苗 和精心设计的试验。在等待可靠的数据之前,治疗性 HPV 疫苗接种 应保持严格的研究性,并且仅应在临床研究背景下或在明确的共同决策后提供,而不是作为常规临床选择。
