主要挑战包括缺乏对致癌 β-HPV 亚型的覆盖以及治疗的标签外性质。
证据基础与方法
这篇叙述性综述评估了 HPV 疫苗接种 对顽固性疣、非黑色素瘤皮肤癌和免疫功能低下人群的治疗作用。
共识与结论
唯一的大型随机对照试验显示对肛门生殖器疣没有任何益处,所有积极证据都来自小样本和异质设计的低质量研究。
争议与缺口
未来的方向需要针对皮肤 HPV 类型的下一代 疫苗 和精心设计的试验。
科研与实践启示
在等待可靠的数据之前,治疗性 HPV 疫苗接种 应保持严格的研究性,并且仅应在临床研究背景下或在明确的共同决策后提供,而不是作为常规临床选择。

Human papillomavirus vaccination as treatment for cutaneous diseases: beyond prevention.

Authors: Junhua Qi, Gang Nie
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) causes cutaneous diseases ranging from common warts to cutaneous squamous cell carcinoma. Although HPV vaccines were licensed to prevent anogenital infections, they are increasingly used off-label to treat established HPV-associated cutaneous lesions. This narrative review evaluates therapeutic HPV vaccination for recalcitrant warts, nonmelanoma skin cancer and immunocompromised populations. The only large randomized controlled trial showed no benefit for anogenital warts, and all positive evidence derives from low-quality studies with small sample sizes and heterogeneous designs. Publication bias is likely. Key challenges include lack of coverage for oncogenic β-HPV subtypes and the off-label nature of therapy. Future directions require next-generation vaccines targeting cutaneous HPV types and well-designed trials. Pending robust data, therapeutic HPV vaccination should remain strictly investigational and should be offered only within the context of clinical research or after explicit shared decision making, rather than as a routine clinical option.
View review five-element analysis

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

中国郊区医院门诊人群 HPV 感染流行病学特征和 疫苗接种 覆盖率:7 年真实世界回顾性分析(2018-2024)

Epidemiological Characteristics of HPV Infection and Vaccination Coverage in Outpatient Population of a Suburban Chinese Hospital: A 7-Year Real-World Retrospective Analysis (2018–2024)
作者： Yu-Fei Feng, Xiao-Min Chen, Nan Jiang, Cui-Lan Zhang, Min Gong, Jie Wang, Shu Chen, Hong-Xiu Zhang, Er-Cui Feng
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗可以有效预防宫颈癌;然而,HPV 疫苗 在中国的实施比许多国家落后了近十年。农村地区的 宫颈癌 筛查率特别低,疫苗 的使用率甚至更有限。随着 疫苗接种 在以成年女性为主的人群中覆盖率的增加,HPV 的 患病率 和基因型分布预计会发生变化。本研究旨在为资源有限的中国郊区人口中 疫苗接种 覆盖率及其与 HPV 感染模式的关系提供真实世界的证据。方法:本回顾性研究分析了2018年至2024年中国郊区县医院门诊HPV检测数据。通过国家免疫信息系统核实疫苗接种历史,并辅以现场问卷调查。对 21 种 HPV 类型进行了 HPV 基因分型。进行年龄分层分析(≤45 岁和≥46 岁)以评估基因型特异性趋势,并计算优势比(OR)和 95% 置信区间(CI)以评估 疫苗 的保护作用。结果:在19,057名年龄≤45岁的合格门诊女性中,HPV 疫苗接种率从2018年的0.34%上升至2024年的57.47%(χ2 = 4986.046,p < 0.001),全程疫苗接种率为93.38%。九价疫苗型HPV阳性率从2018年的10.95%下降到2024年的6.72%(相对下降38.7%,95% CI 18.5–53.8),非疫苗型HPV阳性率从13.38%下降到6.21%(相对下降) 53.6%,95% CI 40.1–64.1);非 疫苗 类型的大幅下降表明,测试人群的时间变化也促成了这些趋势。在年龄≤45岁的女性中,疫苗覆盖型(HPV-16、-33、-45、-52、-58)和非疫苗型(HPV-39、-53、-73、-51、-56、-59、-68)呈现显着下降趋势(p < 0.05)。与未接种疫苗的女性相比,接种疫苗的女性 HPV-6、-11、-16、-33、-52 和 -45 的阳性率显着降低(OR 范围:0.333–0.838),HPV-18 和非 疫苗 型 HPV-73、-39 和 -59 的降低幅度较小,值得进一步研究。未接种疫苗的女性中,主要的高危类型是HPV-52(3.36%)、HPV-16(2.32%)和HPV-58(1.90%)。结论:从 2018 年到 2024 年,门诊女性中 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率大幅增加,同时 疫苗 和非 疫苗 型的阳性率下降;由于非疫苗型积极性也大幅下降,这些生态趋势不能单独归因于疫苗接种。调整年龄和日历年份后,疫苗接种 仍然与 疫苗 覆盖的基因型的较低阳性率相关,而对于非 疫苗 类型则没有观察到相关性;因此,个体非 疫苗 基因型的研究结果并未提供交叉保护的证据。目前的 疫苗 仍未发现包括 HPV-53、-39、-51、-66、-68 和 -56 在内的高 患病率 基因型,这凸显了对解决当地流行基因型的下一代更高价 疫苗 的需求。

范围与问题
农村地区的 宫颈癌 筛查率特别低,疫苗 的使用率甚至更有限。
证据基础与方法
这项回顾性研究分析了2018年至2024年中国郊区县医院门诊HPV检测数据。
共识与结论
九价疫苗型HPV阳性率从2018年的10.95%下降到2024年的6.72%(相对下降38.7%,95% CI 18.5–53.8),非疫苗型HPV阳性率从13.38%下降到6.21%(相对下降) 53.6%,95% CI 40.1–64.1);非 疫苗 类型的大幅下降表明,测试人群的时间变化也促成了这些趋势。
争议与缺口
本研究旨在为资源有限的中国郊区人口中 疫苗接种 覆盖率及其与 HPV 感染模式的关系提供真实世界的证据。
科研与实践启示
调整年龄和日历年份后,疫苗接种 仍然与 疫苗 覆盖的基因型的较低阳性率相关,而对于非 疫苗 类型则没有观察到相关性;因此,个体非 疫苗 基因型的研究结果并未提供交叉保护的证据。

Epidemiological Characteristics of HPV Infection and Vaccination Coverage in Outpatient Population of a Suburban Chinese Hospital: A 7-Year Real-World Retrospective Analysis (2018–2024)

Authors: Yu-Fei Feng, Xiao-Min Chen, Nan Jiang, Cui-Lan Zhang, Min Gong, Jie Wang, Shu Chen, Hong-Xiu Zhang, Er-Cui Feng
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines can effectively prevent cervical cancer; however, HPV vaccine implementation in China lags nearly a decade behind many countries. Rural areas face particularly low cervical cancer screening rates and even more limited vaccine uptake. As vaccination coverage increases among the predominantly adult female population, the prevalence and genotype distribution of HPV are expected to shift. This study aimed to provide real-world evidence on vaccination coverage and its relationship with HPV infection patterns in a resource-limited suburban Chinese population. Methods: This retrospective study analyzed outpatient HPV testing data from a suburban county hospital in China from 2018 to 2024. Vaccination histories were verified through the national immunization information system, supplemented by on-site questionnaire surveys. HPV genotyping was performed for 21 HPV types. Age-stratified analyses (≤45 and ≥46 years) were conducted to evaluate genotype-specific trends, and odds ratios (ORs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs) were calculated to assess vaccine protective effects. Results: Among 19,057 eligible outpatient females aged ≤45 years, the HPV vaccination rate increased from 0.34% in 2018 to 57.47% in 2024 (χ2 = 4986.046, p < 0.001), with a full-course vaccination rate of 93.38%. Nine-valent vaccine-type HPV positivity decreased from 10.95% in 2018 to 6.72% in 2024 (relative reduction 38.7%, 95% CI 18.5–53.8), and non-vaccine-type positivity decreased from 13.38% to 6.21% (relative reduction 53.6%, 95% CI 40.1–64.1); the larger decline in non-vaccine types suggests that temporal changes in the tested population also contribute to these trends. Among females aged ≤45 years, vaccine-covered types (HPV-16, -33, -45, -52, -58) and non-vaccine types (HPV-39, -53, -73, -51, -56, -59, -68) showed significant declining trends (p < 0.05). Vaccinated females had significantly lower positivity rates for HPV-6, -11, -16, -33, -52, and -45 compared with unvaccinated females (OR range: 0.333–0.838), with smaller reductions for HPV-18 and for the non-vaccine types HPV-73, -39, and -59 that warrant further investigation. The predominant high-risk types among unvaccinated females were HPV-52 (3.36%), HPV-16 (2.32%), and HPV-58 (1.90%). Conclusions: HPV vaccination coverage among outpatient females increased substantially from 2018 to 2024, accompanied by declines in positivity for vaccine and non-vaccine types; because non-vaccine-type positivity also fell substantially, these ecological trends cannot be attributed to vaccination alone. After adjustment for age and calendar year, vaccination remained associated with lower positivity for vaccine-covered genotypes, whereas no association was observed for non-vaccine types; the findings for individual non-vaccine genotypes therefore do not provide evidence of cross-protection. High-prevalence genotypes including HPV-53, -39, -51, -66, -68, and -56 remain uncovered by current vaccines, highlighting the need for next-generation higher-valency vaccines that address locally prevalent genotypes.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Rural areas face particularly low cervical cancer screening rates and even more limited vaccine uptake.
Evidence base and method
This retrospective study analyzed outpatient HPV testing data from a suburban county hospital in China from 2018 to 2024.
Consensus and conclusions
Nine-valent vaccine-type HPV positivity decreased from 10.95% in 2018 to 6.72% in 2024 (relative reduction 38.7%, 95% CI 18.5–53.8), and non-vaccine-type positivity decreased from 13.38% to 6.21% (relative reduction 53.6%, 95% CI 40.1–64.1); the larger decline in non-vaccine types suggests that temporal changes in the tested population also contribute to these trends.
Controversies and gaps
This study aimed to provide real-world evidence on vaccination coverage and its relationship with HPV infection patterns in a resource-limited suburban Chinese population.
Research and practice implications
After adjustment for age and calendar year, vaccination remained associated with lower positivity for vaccine-covered genotypes, whereas no association was observed for non-vaccine types; the findings for individual non-vaccine genotypes therefore do not provide evidence of cross-protection.

人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 在世界卫生组织东地中海区域的成本效益:系统评价

The Cost-Effectiveness of Human Papillomavirus Vaccination in the WHO Eastern Mediterranean Region: A Systematic Review
作者： Mohammad Veisi, Ehsan Kakavandi, Abouzar Nazari
摘要中文翻译本系统评价评估了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 在世界卫生组织 (WHO) 东地中海地区国家的成本效益。该综述旨在确定和综合已发表的 HPV 疫苗接种 经济评估,包括单独实施或与 宫颈癌 筛查结合实施的 疫苗接种 策略。它审查了报告的成本、健康结果、增量成本效益比以及有关成本效益或成本节约的结论。该审查还描述了经济成果如何根据国家背景和 疫苗接种 计划特征而变化,包括 疫苗 价格、疫苗 效价、剂量数量、目标人群和 疫苗接种 覆盖范围。研究结果旨在概述现有的经济证据,并找出需要进一步针对具体国家进行研究的差距。本次登记具有追溯效力。提交本注册前已完成审核;因此,这并不构成预期的预注册。

The Cost-Effectiveness of Human Papillomavirus Vaccination in the WHO Eastern Mediterranean Region: A Systematic Review

Authors: Mohammad Veisi, Ehsan Kakavandi, Abouzar Nazari
Original abstract (English)
This systematic review evaluates the cost-effectiveness of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination in countries of the World Health Organization (WHO) Eastern Mediterranean Region. The review aims to identify and synthesize published economic evaluations of HPV vaccination, including vaccination strategies implemented alone or in combination with cervical cancer screening. It examines reported costs, health outcomes, incremental cost-effectiveness ratios, and conclusions regarding cost-effectiveness or cost-saving. The review also describes how economic outcomes vary according to country context and vaccination programme characteristics, including vaccine price, vaccine valency, number of doses, target population, and vaccination coverage. The findings are intended to provide an overview of the available economic evidence and identify gaps requiring further country-specific research. This registration is retrospective. The review had been completed before submission of this registration; therefore, it does not constitute a prospective preregistration.

The findings are intended to provide an overview of the available economic evidence and identify gaps requiring further country-specific research.

中国男性人乳头瘤病毒流行病学研究的Meta分析

Meta-analysis of Epidemiological Studies on Human Papillomavirus Among Men in China
作者： Zikereya Saimi, Ayipairi Aireti, MiriGuli Musa, Kadeliya Muhetaer, Abuduwupuer Haibier, Guzalinuer Abulizi, Remila Rezhake
摘要中文翻译本研究是一项系统回顾和荟萃分析,旨在综合评估中国男性中人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染的流行病学特征。目前,国内HPV流行病学研究大多集中在女性,而对男性HPV感染的研究还不够,特别是在不同亚群的基因型分布和变异方面。本研究的主要目的是系统地估计中国男性的总体 HPV 阳性率,以及高危型 (HR-HPV) 和低危型 (LR-HPV) 阳性率。我们还将评估基因型特异性阳性率(例如 HPV6、HPV11、HPV16、HPV52、HPV58),并根据人群特征(例如男男性行为者 [MSM]、尖锐湿疣患者、门诊就诊者)、地理区域、年龄组和解剖采样部位(例如肛管、尿道、龟头)。我们在 PubMed、Web of Science、中国知网 (CNKI) 和万方数据中进行了从成立到 2025 年 2 月的全面文献检索。将包括提供中国男性 HPV 患病率 基线数据的横断面研究、回顾性分析和 队列研究 研究。数据提取和质量评估将由两名评审员独立进行。所有荟萃分析都将使用 R 软件进行,应用 Freeman-Tukey 双反正弦变换来稳定方差。将计算汇总估计值,并使用 I2 统计量和卡方检验评估异质性,并进行亚组分析和敏感性分析(留一法)以探索异质性的潜在来源。

本研究是一项系统回顾和荟萃分析,旨在综合评估中国男性中人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染的流行病学特征。

Meta-analysis of Epidemiological Studies on Human Papillomavirus Among Men in China

Authors: Zikereya Saimi, Ayipairi Aireti, MiriGuli Musa, Kadeliya Muhetaer, Abuduwupuer Haibier, Guzalinuer Abulizi, Remila Rezhake
Original abstract (English)
This study is a systematic review and meta-analysis aimed at comprehensively evaluating the epidemiological characteristics of Human Papillomavirus (HPV) infection among men in China. Currently, most HPV epidemiological studies in China have focused on women, and research on HPV infection in men remains insufficient, particularly regarding genotype distribution and variations across different subgroups. The primary objective of this study is to systematically estimate the overall HPV positivity rate, as well as high-risk (HR-HPV) and low-risk (LR-HPV) positivity rates among Chinese men. We will also evaluate genotype-specific positivity rates (e.g., HPV6, HPV11, HPV16, HPV52, HPV58) and conduct subgroup analyses stratified by population characteristics (e.g., men who have sex with men [MSM], patients with condyloma acuminata, outpatient clinic attendees), geographic regions, age groups, and anatomical sampling sites (e.g., anal canal, urethra, glans penis). A comprehensive literature search was performed in PubMed, Web of Science, China National Knowledge Infrastructure (CNKI), and Wanfang Data from inception to February 2025. Cross-sectional studies, retrospective analyses, and cohort studies providing baseline HPV prevalence data among Chinese men will be included. Data extraction and quality assessment will be conducted independently by two reviewers. All meta-analyses will be performed using R software, applying the Freeman-Tukey double arcsine transformation to stabilize variances. Pooled estimates will be calculated, and heterogeneity will be assessed using the I2 statistic and chi-squared test, with subgroup analyses and sensitivity analyses (leave-one-out method) conducted to explore potential sources of heterogeneity.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study is a systematic review and meta-analysis aimed at comprehensively evaluating the epidemiological characteristics of Human Papillomavirus (HPV) infection among men in China.
Evidence base and method
A comprehensive literature search was performed in PubMed, Web of Science, China National Knowledge Infrastructure (CNKI), and Wanfang Data from inception to February 2025. Cross-sectional studies, retrospective analyses, and cohort studies providing baseline HPV prevalence data among Chinese men will be included. Data extraction and quality assessment will be conducted independently by two reviewers. All meta-analyses will be performed using R software, applying the Freeman-Tukey double arcsine transformation to stabilize variances.
Consensus and conclusions
The primary objective of this study is to systematically estimate the overall HPV positivity rate, as well as high-risk (HR-HPV) and low-risk (LR-HPV) positivity rates among Chinese men. Genotype-specific positivity rates (e.g., HPV6, HPV11, HPV16, HPV52, HPV58) and subgroup analyses stratified by population characteristics (e.g., men who have sex with men [MSM], patients with condyloma acuminata, outpatient clinic attendees), geographic regions, age groups, and anatomical sampling sites (e.g., anal canal, urethra, glans penis) will be evaluated.
Controversies and gaps
Pooled estimates will be calculated, and heterogeneity will be assessed using the I2 statistic and chi-squared test, with subgroup analyses and sensitivity analyses (leave-one-out method) conducted to explore potential sources of heterogeneity. The study acknowledges that most HPV epidemiological studies in China have focused on women, and research on HPV infection in men remains insufficient, particularly regarding genotype distribution and variations across different subgroups.
Research and practice implications
The study aims to provide a comprehensive understanding of HPV infection among Chinese men, which can inform public health policies and interventions. Future research should focus on genotype distribution and variations across different subgroups.

HPV 相关头颈癌的最新治疗策略。

Recent therapeutic strategies for HPV-Associated head and neck cancers.
作者： Katarzyna Skinderowicz, Wiktoria Łuniewska, Jacek Kuźnicki, Małgorzata Makieła, Karolina Ordon, Piotr Niezgódka, Anna Choromańska, J. Kúznicki
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关头颈癌 (HNC),特别是口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC),代表着日益严重的全球健康负担,近几十年来 发病率 迅速增加。这些肿瘤在生物学和临床上与 HPV 阴性 HNC 不同,更常见于接触烟草和酒精等传统危险因素有限的年轻个体。HPV 驱动的肿瘤发生是由病毒癌蛋白 E6 和 E7 介导的,它们通过 p53 和视网膜母细胞瘤途径的失活破坏基因组稳定性,促进不受控制的增殖、基因组不稳定和 治疗 耐药性。目前 HPV 阳性 HNC 的治疗策略包括手术、放疗和化疗,其中基于顺铂的放化疗仍然是局部晚期疾病的标准治疗方法。然而,HPV 阳性患者的 治疗 反应和总体预后的改善激发了人们对旨在降低毒性而不影响疗效的 治疗 降阶梯策略的兴趣。微创手术方法,例如经口机器人手术和降低强度放射治疗方案显示出有希望的功能结果。免疫疗法代表了复发性和转移性疾病的重大进步,针对 PD-1/PD-L1 轴的免疫检查点抑制剂在选定的患者群体中证明了生存获益。新兴方法包括针对 HPV 癌蛋白的治疗性 疫苗、过继性 T 细胞转移、靶向治疗、溶瘤病毒疗法以及旨在沉默 E6/E7 表达或恢复肿瘤抑制功能的基因治疗。纳米医学进一步提供了药物输送、放射增敏和免疫调节的新平台,增强了 治疗 的特异性和功效。尽管取得了重大进展,但重大挑战仍然存在,包括早期 诊断、治疗 耐药的局限性、生物标志物驱动的患者选择以及公众对 HPV 相关癌症风险的认识不足。先进诊断技术的整合,例如循环肿瘤 HPV DNA 检测和下一代测序,可以改善风险分层和 治疗 监测。对联合疗法、靶向方法和患者定制的 治疗 算法的持续研究对于改善结果仍然至关重要。总之,针对 HPV 相关 HNC 的不断发展的治疗策略反映了向精准肿瘤学的转变,重点是提高生存率、降低毒性以及由分子肿瘤特征驱动的个性化干预。

范围与问题
这些肿瘤在生物学和临床上与 HPV 阴性 HNC 不同,更常见于接触烟草和酒精等传统危险因素有限的年轻个体。
证据基础与方法
先进诊断技术的整合,例如循环肿瘤 HPV DNA 检测和下一代测序,可以改善风险分层和 治疗 监测。
共识与结论
纳米医学进一步提供了药物输送、放射增敏和免疫调节的新平台,增强了 治疗 的特异性和功效。
争议与缺口
总之,针对 HPV 相关 HNC 的不断发展的治疗策略反映了向精准肿瘤学的转变,重点是提高生存率、降低毒性以及由分子肿瘤特征驱动的个性化干预。
科研与实践启示
对联合疗法、靶向方法和患者定制的 治疗 算法的持续研究对于改善结果仍然至关重要。

Recent therapeutic strategies for HPV-Associated head and neck cancers.

Authors: Katarzyna Skinderowicz, Wiktoria Łuniewska, Jacek Kuźnicki, Małgorzata Makieła, Karolina Ordon, Piotr Niezgódka, Anna Choromańska, J. Kúznicki
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated head and neck cancers (HNCs), particularly oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinomas (OPSCC), represent a growing global health burden with rapidly increasing incidence over recent decades. These tumours are biologically and clinically distinct from HPV-negative HNCs, occurring more frequently in younger individuals with limited exposure to traditional risk factors such as tobacco and alcohol. HPV-driven oncogenesis is mediated by viral oncoproteins E6 and E7, which disrupt genomic stability through inactivation of p53 and retinoblastoma pathways, promoting uncontrolled proliferation, genomic instability, and treatment resistance. Current therapeutic strategies for HPV-positive HNCs include surgery, radiotherapy, and chemotherapy, with cisplatin-based chemoradiotherapy remaining the standard of care for locoregionally advanced disease. However, improved treatment responses and overall prognosis in HPV-positive patients have stimulated interest in treatment de-escalation strategies aimed at reducing toxicity without compromising efficacy. Minimally invasive surgical approaches, such as transoral robotic surgery, and reduced-intensity radiotherapy regimens show promising functional outcomes. Immunotherapy represents a major advancement in recurrent and metastatic disease, with immune checkpoint inhibitors targeting the PD-1/PD-L1 axis demonstrating survival benefits in selected patient populations. Emerging approaches include therapeutic vaccines against HPV oncoproteins, adoptive T cell transfer, targeted therapies, oncolytic virotherapy, and gene therapy aimed at silencing E6/E7 expression or restoring tumor suppressor function. Nanomedicine further offers novel platforms for drug delivery, radiosensitization, and immunomodulation, enhancing treatment specificity and efficacy. Despite significant progress, major challenges persist, including limitations in early diagnosis, treatment resistance, biomarker-driven patient selection, and inadequate public awareness of HPV-associated cancer risk. Integration of advanced diagnostics, such as circulating tumor HPV DNA assays and next-generation sequencing, may improve risk stratification and treatment monitoring. Continued research into combination therapies, targeted approaches, and patient-tailored treatment algorithms remains essential to improve outcomes. In summary, evolving therapeutic strategies for HPV-associated HNCs reflect a shift toward precision oncology, focusing on improved survival, reduced toxicity, and personalized interventions driven by molecular tumor characteristics.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
These tumours are biologically and clinically distinct from HPV-negative HNCs, occurring more frequently in younger individuals with limited exposure to traditional risk factors such as tobacco and alcohol.
Evidence base and method
Integration of advanced diagnostics, such as circulating tumor HPV DNA assays and next-generation sequencing, may improve risk stratification and treatment monitoring.
Consensus and conclusions
Nanomedicine further offers novel platforms for drug delivery, radiosensitization, and immunomodulation, enhancing treatment specificity and efficacy.
Controversies and gaps
In summary, evolving therapeutic strategies for HPV-associated HNCs reflect a shift toward precision oncology, focusing on improved survival, reduced toxicity, and personalized interventions driven by molecular tumor characteristics.
Research and practice implications
Continued research into combination therapies, targeted approaches, and patient-tailored treatment algorithms remains essential to improve outcomes.

当前中国大陆女大学生对 HPV 疫苗 安全性和自我报告 HPV 疫苗接种 的关注:一项加权横断面研究

Current concern about HPV vaccine safety and self-reported HPV vaccination among female college students in mainland China: A weighted cross-sectional study
作者： Wen-Qian Yang, Han-Ting Zhang, Kun Tang
摘要中文翻译对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 安全性的担忧是中国女大学生中 疫苗接种 的潜在相关因素。这项加权横断面研究使用了 2025 年全国大学生性与生殖健康调查,这是一项在中国大陆进行的自愿、便利的在线调查。分析样本包括 37,329 名具有 疫苗 意识的女大专和本科生;在选择分析中,对 6,202 名不知道 HPV 疫苗 的合格受访者进行了单独检查。疫苗接种 自我报告接受至少一剂 HPV 疫苗 剂量;剂量编号、系列完成情况、疫苗接种 日期和记录验证不可用。按学校聚类的加权泊松广义估计方程,根据年龄、学校级别、入学年级、地区、每月支出、大学预科居住地、种族和母亲教育程度进行调整。6,708/37,329 名学生报告了 疫苗接种(18.0% 未加权;19.7% 加权)。与不关心的学生相比,非常关心的学生的调整后 患病率 比率为 0.60(95% 置信区间 [CI],0.54-0.67),关心的学生为 0.55(0.50-0.60),中性的学生为 0.70(0.65-0.75),完全不关心的学生为 1.00(0.86-1.16)。参考组的标准化 患病率 为 28.3%,相关组和中性组的标准化 患病率 为 15.5%-19.8%。分类关联偏离了单一对数线性模式 (P < .001),并且在地理、权重、协变量、资格和选择分析中保持稳定。当前对 HPV 疫苗 安全性的担忧与了解 疫苗 的学生群体中先前自我报告的 疫苗接种 较低有关。沟通和追赶策略需要通过验证的启动和完成结果进行前瞻性评估。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究调查了中国大陆女大学生对 HPV 疫苗 安全性的担忧与自我报告的 HPV 疫苗接种 之间的关系。核心问题是 疫苗 安全问题是否与该人群中的 疫苗接种 比率相关。
证据基础与方法
这项加权横断面研究使用了 2025 年全国大学生性与生殖健康调查,这是一项在中国大陆进行的自愿、便利的在线调查。
共识与结论
研究发现,19.7% 的样本报告称接受了至少一剂 HPV 疫苗 剂量。与不关心的学生相比,非常关心或关心 疫苗 安全的学生对先前自我报告的 患病率 的调整后 患病率 比率较低。
争议与缺口
该研究指出,关注点与 疫苗接种 之间的分类关联偏离了单一对数线性模式,表明需要进一步研究的复杂关系。此外,该研究没有验证 疫苗接种 的启动和完成结果。
科研与实践启示
研究结果表明,应对 HPV 疫苗接种 的沟通和追赶策略进行前瞻性评估,以解决大学生的安全问题。

Current concern about HPV vaccine safety and self-reported HPV vaccination among female college students in mainland China: A weighted cross-sectional study

Authors: Wen-Qian Yang, Han-Ting Zhang, Kun Tang
Original abstract (English)
Concern about human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine safety is a potential correlate of vaccination among female college students in China. This weighted cross-sectional study used the 2025 National College Student Survey on Sexual and Reproductive Health, a voluntary, convenience-based online survey conducted across mainland China. The analytic sample comprised 37,329 vaccine-aware female junior-college and undergraduate students; 6,202 eligible respondents who did not know the HPV vaccine were examined separately in selection analyses. Vaccination was self-reported receipt of at least one HPV vaccine dose; dose number, series completion, vaccination date, and record verification were unavailable. Weighted Poisson generalized estimating equations clustered by school adjusted for age, school level, entry grade, region, monthly expenditure, pre-college residence, ethnicity, and maternal education. Vaccination was reported by 6,708/37,329 students (18.0% unweighted; 19.7% weighted). Compared with students who were not concerned, adjusted prevalence ratios were 0.60 (95% confidence interval [CI], 0.54-0.67) for very concerned students, 0.55 (0.50-0.60) for concerned students, 0.70 (0.65-0.75) for neutral students, and 1.00 (0.86-1.16) for those not at all concerned. Standardized prevalence was 28.3% in the reference group and 15.5%-19.8% in the concerned and neutral groups. The categorical association departed from a single log-linear pattern ( P < .001) and remained stable across geographic, weighting, covariate, eligibility, and selection analyses. Current concern about HPV vaccine safety was associated with lower prior self-reported vaccination in this vaccine-aware student population. Communication and catch-up strategies warrant prospective evaluation with verified initiation and completion outcomes.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study examines the relationship between concern about HPV vaccine safety and self-reported HPV vaccination among female college students in mainland China. The central question is whether vaccine safety concerns correlate with vaccination rates in this population.
Evidence base and method
This weighted cross-sectional study used the 2025 National College Student Survey on Sexual and Reproductive Health, a voluntary, convenience-based online survey conducted across mainland China.
Consensus and conclusions
The study found that 19.7% of the sample reported receiving at least one HPV vaccine dose. Students who were very concerned or concerned about vaccine safety had lower adjusted prevalence ratios for prior self-reported vaccination compared to those not concerned.
Controversies and gaps
The study notes that the categorical association between concern and vaccination departed from a single log-linear pattern, suggesting complex relationships that require further investigation. Additionally, the study did not have verified initiation and completion outcomes for vaccination.
Research and practice implications
The findings suggest that communication and catch-up strategies for HPV vaccination should be evaluated prospectively to address safety concerns among college students.

p53 和 pRb 的过度激活是 HPV 驱动的癌症的潜在治疗选择。

Hyperactivation of p53 and pRb is a potential therapeutic option in HPV driven cancers.
作者： Vindya Ranasinghe, Nigel McMillan
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是最常见的性传播感染。HPV 的 E6 和 E7 癌蛋白主要通过分别调节 宿主 p53 和 pRb 肿瘤抑制途径来促进癌性病变的发展。尽管预防性药物 疫苗 可用于治疗 HPV 感染,但对已确诊的 HPV 感染无效。尽管在手术、放疗和全身治疗方面取得了重大进展,但复发性、转移性和 治疗 耐药的 HPV 相关癌症仍然面临着巨大的临床挑战,这凸显了对新治疗策略的迫切需要。由于 E6 和 E7 病毒癌蛋白是 HPV 阳性癌症 发病机制 的关键驱动因素,因此针对这些蛋白代表了开发新疗法的合理方法。基因疗法目前作为癌症消退的潜在策略被广泛研究。RNA 干扰技术和 CRISPR 方法在下调病毒癌蛋白的表达方面非常有效。在大多数癌症类型中,肿瘤抑制蛋白经常发生突变。然而,在 HPV 诱发的癌症中,它们通常保持野生型。因此,增加肿瘤抑制蛋白的生物利用度是一种治疗选择。CRISPR 激活 (CRISPRa) 是一种 CRISPR 系统变体,其中催化失活的 Cas9 (dcas9) 与转录激活因子融合以诱导基因表达。与 CRISPR 不同,CRISPRa 无需基因组修饰即可增加靶基因的表达。尽管可用于增加肿瘤抑制蛋白表达的策略很少,但 CRISPRa 作为一种有效且有前途的策略已获得了主要关注。有潜力将 CRISPRa 开发为未来的治疗干预措施,与其他 治疗 模式相结合来治疗 HPV 诱导的癌症。
查看综述五要素

基因疗法目前作为癌症消退的潜在策略被广泛研究。

尽管在手术、放疗和全身治疗方面取得了重大进展,但复发性、转移性和 治疗 耐药的 HPV 相关癌症仍然面临着巨大的临床挑战,这凸显了对新治疗策略的迫切需要。由于 E6 和 E7 病毒癌蛋白是 HPV 阳性癌症 发病机制 的关键驱动因素,因此针对这些蛋白代表了开发新疗法的合理方法。

Hyperactivation of p53 and pRb is a potential therapeutic option in HPV driven cancers.

Authors: Vindya Ranasinghe, Nigel McMillan
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the most frequently acquired sexually transmitted infection. The E6 and E7 oncoproteins of HPV mainly contribute to the development of cancerous lesions by dysregulating the host p53 and pRb tumour suppressor pathways, respectively. Although prophylactic vaccines are available for HPV infection, they are ineffective for established HPV infections. Despite significant advances in surgery, radiotherapy, and systemic therapies, recurrent, metastatic, and treatment-resistant HPV-associated cancers continue to present substantial clinical challenges, highlighting the urgent need for novel therapeutic strategies.As the E6 and E7 viral oncoproteins are key drivers of HPV-positive cancer pathogenesis, targeting these proteins represents a rational approach for developing new therapies. Gene therapy is currently studied extensively as a potential strategy for cancer regression. RNA interference technology and CRISPR approaches are highly effective in downregulating the expression of viral oncoproteins. In most cancer types, tumour suppressor proteins are frequently mutated; however, in HPV-induced cancers, they typically remain wild type. Hence, increasing the bioavailability of tumour suppressor proteins is a therapeutic option. CRISPR activation (CRISPRa) is a CRISPR system variant in which catalytically inactive Cas9 (dcas9) is fused with a transcriptional activator to induce gene expression. Unlike CRISPR, CRISPRa increases the expression of the targeted gene without genomic modifications. Although there are few strategies available to increase the expression of tumour suppressor proteins, CRISPRa has gained a major focus as an effective and promising strategy. There is potential to develop CRISPRa as a future therapeutic intervention to treat HPV induced cancers combined with other treatment modalities.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Although there are few strategies available to increase the expression of tumour suppressor proteins, CRISPRa has gained a major focus as an effective and promising strategy.
Evidence base and method
Gene therapy is currently studied extensively as a potential strategy for cancer regression.
Consensus and conclusions
Despite significant advances in surgery, radiotherapy, and systemic therapies, recurrent, metastatic, and treatment-resistant HPV-associated cancers continue to present substantial clinical challenges, highlighting the urgent need for novel therapeutic strategies.As the E6 and E7 viral oncoproteins are key drivers of HPV-positive cancer pathogenesis, targeting these proteins represents a rational approach for developing new therapies.
Controversies and gaps
Unlike CRISPR, CRISPRa increases the expression of the targeted gene without genomic modifications.
Research and practice implications
There is potential to develop CRISPRa as a future therapeutic intervention to treat HPV induced cancers combined with other treatment modalities.

考虑个人和提供者因素后,美国青少年 HPV 疫苗接种 启动的州级差异

State-level variation in HPV vaccination initiation among U.S. adolescents after accounting for individual and provider factors
作者： Lihong Ou, Wen-Cong Cui, Sunny Wonsun Kim
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究检验了在考虑了提供者的推荐和个人特征后,HPV 疫苗接种 启动的州级变异是否持续存在。
证据基础与方法
该研究分析了来自 14,622 名 13-17 岁青少年的 2024 年国家免疫调查数据,使用调查加权多变量逻辑回归来调整各种因素。
共识与结论
调整后,居住州仍然与 HPV 疫苗接种 启动联合相关,并且家长报告的医疗保健提供者推荐与启动密切相关。
争议与缺口
年龄较大的青少年感染 HPV 疫苗接种 的几率较高,但女性和男性青少年之间没有观察到显着差异。孕产妇教育和健康保险也与启动相关。
科研与实践启示
这些发现支持了对可能导致州级 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率差异的地点相关背景因素的进一步调查。

State-level variation in HPV vaccination initiation among U.S. adolescents after accounting for individual and provider factors

Authors: Lihong Ou, Wen-Cong Cui, Sunny Wonsun Kim
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination prevents HPV-related cancers, yet coverage among U.S. adolescents has plateaued. This study examined whether state-level variation in HPV vaccination initiation persisted after accounting for provider recommendation and individual characteristics. METHODS: We analyzed 2024 National Immunization Survey-Teen data from 14,622 adolescents aged 13-17 years (mean age = 15.04, SD = 1.40). Survey-weighted multivariable logistic regression examined provider-verified HPV vaccination initiation (≥1 dose), adjusting for parent-reported healthcare provider recommendation, age, sex, race and ethnicity, poverty status, maternal education, health insurance, and state of residence. Adjusted state-level predicted probabilities were estimated using predictive margins. RESULTS: State of residence remained jointly associated with HPV vaccination initiation after adjustment (design-adjusted Wald test: F[50, 14,502] = 3.76, p < 0.001). Parent-reported healthcare provider recommendation was strongly associated with initiation (AOR = 3.12, 95% CI: 2.67-3.65). Older adolescents had higher odds of HPV vaccination initiation (AOR = 1.17, 95% CI: 1.12-1.23), whereas no significant difference was observed between female and male adolescents. Compared with Hispanic adolescents, non-Hispanic White adolescents had lower odds of initiation (AOR = 0.63, 95% CI: 0.51-0.78). Maternal education and health insurance were also jointly associated with initiation. Adjusted predicted probabilities ranged from 64.5% in Mississippi to 89.7% in Hawaii, a 25.2-percentage-point difference. CONCLUSIONS: State-level variation in HPV vaccination initiation persisted after accounting for parent-reported healthcare provider recommendation and measured sociodemographic and healthcare-related characteristics. These findings support further investigation of place-related contextual factors that may contribute to state-level variation in HPV vaccination coverage.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study examined whether state-level variation in HPV vaccination initiation persisted after accounting for provider recommendation and individual characteristics.
Evidence base and method
The study analyzed 2024 National Immunization Survey-Teen data from 14,622 adolescents aged 13-17 years, using survey-weighted multivariable logistic regression to adjust for various factors.
Consensus and conclusions
State of residence remained jointly associated with HPV vaccination initiation after adjustment, and parent-reported healthcare provider recommendation was strongly associated with initiation.
Controversies and gaps
Older adolescents had higher odds of HPV vaccination initiation, but no significant difference was observed between female and male adolescents. Maternal education and health insurance were also jointly associated with initiation.
Research and practice implications
Further investigation of place-related contextual factors that may contribute to state-level variation in HPV vaccination coverage is supported by these findings.

泰国城乡地区 HPV 自我采样 宫颈癌 筛查意愿的决定因素:一项分析横断面研究

Determinants of HPV self-sampling willingness for cervical cancer screening in Urban and Rural Areas of Thailand: An analytical cross-sectional study
作者： Natthawat Wechchitticharoen, Pimchaya Chawalitthichot, Chinaporn Phokham
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 自我采样是提高未筛查人群 人乳头瘤病毒 筛查覆盖率的可行策略。尽管如此,影响泰国各省使用自我 HPV 检测意愿的因素仍然有限。本研究旨在确定泰国城市和农村地区女性使用自我 HPV 检测意愿相关的独立因素。方法:这项分析横断面调查纳入了居住在北标府的 25-60 岁泰国女性。这项研究使用了结构化的自我管理问卷,涵盖人口特征、HPV 知识、态度和使用自我 HPV 检测的意愿。使用多变量回归来估计 患病率 比率 (PR)。结果:总共 527 名女性中有 453 名(86.0%;95% CI 82.8%-88.8%)愿意使用自我 HPV 检测。知识得分(PR 1.038 每增加 10%;95% CI 1.016-1.061)和态度得分(PR 1.092 每增加 1 分;95% CI 1.022-1.165)与较高的意愿显着且独立相关,而低家庭收入与相对于中等收入的较低意愿显着相关(PR 0.904;95% CI 0.830-0.985)。居住区域和筛查史与意愿无关。结论:在城市和农村地区,可通过数字方式访问的受访者中,自我 HPV 意愿均较高,但居住区之间没有统计学上的显着差异;这一发现不应推广到没有数字访问权限的女性。知识和态度是已确定的最强的可改变决定因素,这种假设的意愿应与实际的接受情况区分开来,而实际的接受情况仍有待评估。

Determinants of HPV self-sampling willingness for cervical cancer screening in Urban and Rural Areas of Thailand: An analytical cross-sectional study

Authors: Natthawat Wechchitticharoen, Pimchaya Chawalitthichot, Chinaporn Phokham
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Human papillomavirus (HPV) self-sampling is a feasible strategy to increase cervical cancer screening coverage among under-screened populations. Still, the factors influencing willingness to use self-HPV testing in provincial Thai settings are limited. This study aimed to identify independent factors associated with willingness to use self-HPV testing among women in urban and rural areas of Thailand. METHODS: This analytical cross-sectional survey enrolled Thai women aged 25-60 years residing in Saraburi Province. This study used a structured self-administered questionnaire covering demographic characteristics, HPV knowledge, attitudes, and willingness to use self-HPV testing. Multivariable regression was used to estimate prevalence ratios (PRs). RESULTS: In total, 453 of 527 women (86.0%; 95% CI 82.8%-88.8%) were willing to use self-HPV testing. Knowledge score (PR 1.038 per 10% increase; 95% CI 1.016-1.061) and attitude score (PR 1.092 per 1-point increase; 95% CI 1.022-1.165) were significantly and independently associated with higher willingness, while low household income was significantly associated with lower willingness relative to middle income (PR 0.904; 95% CI 0.830-0.985). Residential area and screening history were not associated with willingness. CONCLUSIONS: Self-HPV willingness was high in both urban and rural settings among digitally accessible respondents, with no statistically significant difference between residential areas; this finding should not be extrapolated to women without digital access. Knowledge and attitudes were the strongest modifiable determinants identified, and this hypothetical willingness should be distinguished from actual uptake, which remains to be evaluated.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study aimed to identify independent factors associated with willingness to use self-HPV testing among women in urban and rural areas of Thailand.
Evidence base and method
This analytical cross-sectional survey enrolled Thai women aged 25-60 years residing in Saraburi Province, using a structured self-administered questionnaire covering demographic characteristics, HPV knowledge, attitudes, and willingness to use self-HPV testing. Multivariable regression was used to estimate prevalence ratios (PRs).
Consensus and conclusions
In total, 453 of 527 women (86.0%; 95% CI 82.8%-88.8%) were willing to use self-HPV testing. Knowledge score (PR 1.038 per 10% increase; 95% CI 1.016-1.061) and attitude score (PR 1.092 per 1-point increase; 95% CI 1.022-1.165) were significantly and independently associated with higher willingness, while low household income was significantly associated with lower willingness relative to middle income (PR 0.904; 95% CI 0.830-0.985).
Controversies and gaps
Residential area and screening history were not associated with willingness. The study did not find a statistically significant difference between residential areas, but this finding should not be extrapolated to women without digital access. Knowledge and attitudes were the strongest modifiable determinants identified, and this hypothetical willingness should be distinguished from actual uptake, which remains to be evaluated.
Research and practice implications
Self-HPV willingness was high in both urban and rural settings among digitally accessible respondents, with no statistically significant difference between residential areas; this finding should not be extrapolated to women without digital access.

爱、性和免疫抑制:移植临床医生的性健康概述

Love, Sex, and Immunosuppression: A Sexual Health Overview for the Transplant Clinician
作者： Adam G. Stewart, Carlos Alejandro Portales Castillo, Gabriel Motoa, César Rodriguez, Camille N. Kotton, C. Portales-Castillo
摘要中文翻译摘要 性健康是实体器官移植受者综合护理的一个重要但经常被忽视的方面。虽然移植可以恢复整体健康和生活质量,但由于生理、心理和药理因素,患者可能会继续出现性功能障碍。此外,移植受者仍然面临性传播感染 (STI) 的风险,包括 HIV、人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)、梅毒等,这对患者和移植结果都有影响。HPV 相关恶性肿瘤,特别是肛门鳞状细胞癌,在该人群中的发生率明显较高,这凸显了针对针对性筛查和 疫苗接种 提出建议的必要性。这篇综述讨论了移植人群(包括肝脏、肾脏、心脏和肺脏受者)的一系列性健康问题,并提供了性功能、性传播感染筛查和降低风险策略的实用指导。我们概述了针对艾滋病毒和细菌性传播感染的暴露前和暴露后预防的当前证据和建议,包括使用基于替诺福韦的暴露前预防和多西环素暴露后预防。我们还旨在强调针对 HPV、乙型肝炎、甲型肝炎和猴痘病毒的免疫接种的作用,强调对免疫功能低下个体的安全性、时机和有效性考虑。尽管人们的认识不断提高,但移植医学中常规性健康评估和性传播感染预防策略的实施仍然不一致。我们呼吁将性健康更多地纳入移植护理途径,并确定关键研究重点以优化结果。临床医生应该感到有权发起公开对话,倡导 疫苗接种 和预防,并根据个体化风险定制护理。这一全面的更新提供了实用的工具和建议,以改善实体器官移植受者的性健康。

科研与实践启示
临床医生应该感到有权发起公开对话,倡导 疫苗接种 和预防,并根据个体化风险定制护理。

Love, Sex, and Immunosuppression: A Sexual Health Overview for the Transplant Clinician

Authors: Adam G. Stewart, Carlos Alejandro Portales Castillo, Gabriel Motoa, César Rodriguez, Camille N. Kotton, C. Portales-Castillo
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Sexual health is a critical, yet often overlooked, aspect of comprehensive care for solid organ transplant recipients. While transplantation can restore overall health and quality of life, patients may continue to experience sexual dysfunction because of physiological, psychological, and pharmacological factors. Furthermore, transplant recipients remain at risk for sexually transmitted infections (STIs), including HIV, human papillomavirus (HPV), syphilis, and others, with implications for both patient and graft outcomes. HPV-associated malignancies, particularly anal squamous cell carcinoma, occur at significantly higher rates in this population, highlighting the need for recommendations on targeted screening and vaccination. This review addresses the spectrum of sexual health issues across transplant populations-including liver, kidney, heart, and lung recipients-with practical guidance on sexual function, STI screening, and risk reduction strategies. We outline current evidence and recommendations for preexposure and postexposure prophylaxis against HIV and bacterial STIs, including the use of tenofovir-based preexposure prophylaxis and doxycycline postexposure prophylaxis. We also aim to highlight the role of immunization against HPV, hepatitis B, hepatitis A, and monkeypox virus, emphasizing safety, timing, and efficacy considerations in immunocompromised individuals. Despite growing awareness, implementation of routine sexual health assessment and STI prevention strategies remains inconsistent in transplant medicine. We call for greater integration of sexual health into transplant care pathways and identify key research priorities to optimize outcomes. Clinicians should feel empowered to initiate open conversations, advocate for vaccination and prophylaxis, and tailor care based on individualized risk. This comprehensive update offers practical tools and recommendations to improve sexual health in solid organ transplant recipients.

Evidence base and method

放射治疗2.0:以更少的努力更有效地治疗头颈部肿瘤的艺术

Radiotherapy 2.0: The art of doing less to treat head and neck tumors more effectively
作者： Joel Castelli Castelli, Florian Estrade, Agathe Pronier, Xavier Liem, Robin Morcet-Delattre, Louise Bouvier, Quentin Oresve, Yoann Pointreau, Xavier Palard-Novello, Xu-Shan Sun
摘要中文翻译头颈部鳞状细胞癌的治疗正在经历重大的概念转变。长期以来,在治疗升级逻辑的指导下——目标是以重大功能性后遗症为代价最大限度地控制肿瘤——该领域正日益朝着精准个性化方向发展。这种范式转变可以被描述为“放射治疗2.0”,部分原因是人乳头瘤病毒阳性口咽肿瘤的出现,其良好的预后,特别是在年轻和活跃的患者中,促使人们重新考虑治愈概率和长期生活质量之间的平衡。因此,我们的目标不仅是治愈,而且是“更好地治愈”。这种方法——减少治疗效果,得到越来越多的临床证据的支持,表明在适当选择的患者中可以安全地降低 治疗 强度。从几何角度来看,重点是解剖学上的保留:无论是保留咽缩肌以减少晚期吞咽困难,还是在局部病例中省略预防性对侧颈部照射,选择性减少照射量的研究越来越多。从数量上讲,当在佐剂环境中针对中等风险组织学特征或依赖动态生物选择时,减少物理剂量可能是可行的。在这方面,早期肿瘤对诱导化疗的反应或通过分子成像(氟米索硝唑正电子发射断层扫描)评估缺氧可能代表有用的选择工具,包括涉及将剂量大幅减少至 30 Gy 的方法。同时,通过适度大分割对 治疗 持续时间进行放射生物学优化是一种潜在的治疗替代方案,可减少 治疗 持续时间,同时保持可接受的毒性。然而,这种剂量递减的探索也遇到了重要的限制,需要仔细选择患者。最近的数据表明,根据经验削弱 治疗 的关键成分 - 无论是用西妥昔单抗替代顺铂,还是在淋巴结包膜破裂的情况下大幅减少剂量 - 可能会增加局部失败的风险。因此,放射治疗 2.0 不应基于经验性的 治疗 减少,而应基于仔细定义的临床、解剖学和(如果有)生物学标准,以确定每位患者的适当剂量和 治疗 体积。

证据基础与方法
提供的证据没有明确说明审查方法、数据库、资格流程或证据基础。
共识与结论
在这方面,早期肿瘤对诱导化疗的反应或通过分子成像(氟米索硝唑正电子发射断层扫描)评估缺氧可能代表有用的选择工具,包括涉及将剂量大幅减少至 30 Gy 的方法。
争议与缺口
然而,这种剂量递减的探索也遇到了重要的限制,需要仔细选择患者。
科研与实践启示
最近的数据表明,根据经验削弱 治疗 的关键成分 - 无论是用西妥昔单抗替代顺铂,还是在淋巴结包膜破裂的情况下大幅减少剂量 - 可能会增加局部失败的风险。

Radiotherapy 2.0: The art of doing less to treat head and neck tumors more effectively

Authors: Joel Castelli Castelli, Florian Estrade, Agathe Pronier, Xavier Liem, Robin Morcet-Delattre, Louise Bouvier, Quentin Oresve, Yoann Pointreau, Xavier Palard-Novello, Xu-Shan Sun
Original abstract (English)
The management of squamous cell carcinomas of the head and neck is undergoing a major conceptual shift. Long guided by a logic of therapeutic escalation - where the goal was to maximize tumor control at the cost of significant functional sequelae - the field is increasingly moving toward precision personalization. This paradigm shift, which can be described as "Radiotherapy 2.0", is partly driven by the emergence of human papillomavirus-positive oropharyngeal tumors, whose favorable prognosis, particularly in younger and more active patients, has prompted renewed consideration of the balance between the probability of cure and long-term quality of life. The goal is therefore not only to cure, but also to "cure better". This approach - doing less to treat better - is supported by a growing body of clinical evidence suggesting that treatment intensity may be safely reduced in appropriately selected patients. From a geometric standpoint, the emphasis is on anatomical sparing: whether it involves preserving the pharyngeal constrictor muscles to reduce late dysphagia, or omitting prophylactic contralateral cervical irradiation in well-localized cases, selective reduction of irradiation volumes is increasingly being investigated. Quantitatively, reducing physical doses may be feasible when targeting intermediate-risk histological profiles in the adjuvant setting or when relying on dynamic biological selection. In this regard, early tumor response to induction chemotherapy or the assessment of hypoxia via molecular imaging (fluoromisonidazole positron-emission tomography) may represent useful selection tools, including for approaches involving substantial dose reduction to 30 Gy. At the same time, radiobiological optimization of treatment duration through moderate hypofractionation represents a potential therapeutic alternative, reducing treatment duration while maintaining acceptable toxicity. However, this quest for dose de-escalation has also encountered important limitations and requires careful patient selection. Recent data suggest that empirically weakening key components of treatment - whether by replacing cisplatin with cetuximab or by substantially reducing the dose in the presence of lymph node capsule rupture - may increase the risk of locoregional failure. Radiotherapy 2.0 should therefore not be based on empirical treatment reduction, but rather on carefully defined clinical, anatomical and, where available, biological criteria to determine the appropriate dose and treatment volume for each patient.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This approach - doing less to treat better - is supported by a growing body of clinical evidence suggesting that treatment intensity may be safely reduced in appropriately selected patients.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
In this regard, early tumor response to induction chemotherapy or the assessment of hypoxia via molecular imaging (fluoromisonidazole positron-emission tomography) may represent useful selection tools, including for approaches involving substantial dose reduction to 30 Gy.
Controversies and gaps
However, this quest for dose de-escalation has also encountered important limitations and requires careful patient selection.
Research and practice implications
Recent data suggest that empirically weakening key components of treatment - whether by replacing cisplatin with cetuximab or by substantially reducing the dose in the presence of lymph node capsule rupture - may increase the risk of locoregional failure.

对安全网环境中 HPV 自我采样情况上游领域的实施前评估:范围界定审查。

Pre-implementation assessment of upstream domains on the uptake of HPV self-sampling in safety-net settings: a scoping review.
作者： Lynsey G Huppe, Cassandra A Okechukwu, Thelma Nanbigne, Grace Wang
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 筛查可用作 宫颈癌 的早期检测器。美国食品和药物管理局新批准的 HPV 自采样检测可供美国的安全网实践使用,以帮助减轻目前患者中出现的筛查差异。由于患者的可接受性和能力已经建立,本次范围审查从安全网提供者和领导层的角度综合了实施 HPV 自我采样的障碍和促进因素,以及当前研究中尚未探讨的上游因素。遵循 PRISMA-ScR 指南,进行了文献检索,探索同行评审的出版物和灰色文献,以确定 HPV 自我采样的当前状况。调查结果是使用实施研究综合框架进行组织的。从纳入的 57 项工作中发现,安全网实践能够利用当前的工作流程和团队成员来实施 HPV 自我采样。然而,外部制定的指导方针和政策严重影响实施过程,可能导致安全网实践的延迟。最近的指南认可和即将推出的保险范围解决了其中一些障碍,使安全网设置的批准后实施研究成为当务之急。

Pre-implementation assessment of upstream domains on the uptake of HPV self-sampling in safety-net settings: a scoping review.

Authors: Lynsey G Huppe, Cassandra A Okechukwu, Thelma Nanbigne, Grace Wang
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) screening can be used as an early detector for cervical cancer. The newly US Food and Drug Administration-approved HPV self-sampling tests can be used by safety net practices in the USA to help mitigate the current screening disparities seen in patients. As patient acceptability and capability have already been established, this scoping review synthesizes the barriers and facilitators of implementing HPV self-sampling from the perspective of safety net providers and leadership, and the upstream factors not yet explored in current research. Following PRISMA-ScR guidelines, a literature search was conducted exploring peer-reviewed publications and grey literature to establish the current landscape of HPV self-sampling. Findings were organized using the Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research. From the 57 works included, it was found that safety net practices are capable of implementing HPV self-sampling by utilizing current workflows and team members. However, outer setting guidelines and policies heavily influence the implementation process, likely causing a delay in uptake by safety net practices. Recent guideline endorsements and forthcoming insurance coverage address several of these barriers, making post-approval implementation research in safety net settings an urgent priority.
View review five-element analysis

Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps

Research and practice implications

宫颈癌 中的自然杀伤细胞功能障碍:HPV 驱动的免疫逃避通过肿瘤免疫微环境屏障

Natural killer cell dysfunction in cervical cancer: HPV-driven immune evasion through the tumor immune microenvironment barrier
作者： Bo-Wen Xu, Wang-Shu Li, Dan-Dan Wang, Xia Li, Aziz ur Rehman Aziz, 于晓辉, Bo Liu, Xiao-Hui Yu
摘要中文翻译由高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 持续感染驱动的 宫颈癌 仍然是全球癌症相关 死亡率 的主要原因。自然杀伤 (NK) 细胞是抗肿瘤免疫的关键介质,其在 宫颈癌 中的功能障碍已被报道。然而,目前的理解仍然不平衡,大多数研究侧重于孤立的机制,而不是提供关于 HPV 介导的肿瘤免疫微环境 (TIME) 如何损害 宫颈癌 中 NK 细胞功能的综合观点。在这篇综述中,我们将 HPV 驱动的免疫调节与 TIME 重塑相结合,以研究结构、细胞、分子和代谢约束如何聚合以限制 宫颈癌 中的 NK 细胞浸润和功能,区分 宫颈癌 特异性、HPV 相关和跨肿瘤证据。此外,我们讨论了针对这些障碍的新兴治疗策略,并强调它们作为现有免疫疗法的补充方法的潜力,同时指出,宫颈癌 中的大多数策略仍处于临床前或假设生成阶段。该框架提供了一个统一的视角来指导 宫颈癌 中更有效的基于 NK 细胞的免疫疗法的开发。

Natural killer cell dysfunction in cervical cancer: HPV-driven immune evasion through the tumor immune microenvironment barrier

Authors: Bo-Wen Xu, Wang-Shu Li, Dan-Dan Wang, Xia Li, Aziz ur Rehman Aziz, 于晓辉, Bo Liu, Xiao-Hui Yu
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer, driven by persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV), remains a major cause of cancer-related mortality worldwide. Natural killer (NK) cells are critical mediators of antitumor immunity, and their dysfunction in cervical cancer has been reported. However, current understanding remains uneven, with most studies focusing on isolated mechanisms rather than providing an integrated view of how the HPV-mediated tumor immune microenvironment (TIME) impairs NK cell functions in cervical cancer. In this review, we integrate HPV-driven immune modulation with TIME remodeling to examine how structural, cellular, molecular, and metabolic constraints may converge to restrict NK cell infiltration and function in cervical cancer, distinguishing cervical cancer-specific, HPV-associated, and cross-tumor evidence. Furthermore, we discuss emerging therapeutic strategies targeting these barriers and emphasize their potential as complementary approaches to existing immunotherapies, while noting that most remain preclinical or hypothesis-generating in cervical cancer. This framework provides a unified perspective to guide the development of more effective NK cell–based immunotherapies in cervical cancer.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Cervical cancer, driven by persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV), remains a major cause of cancer-related mortality worldwide.
Evidence base and method
However, current understanding remains uneven, with most studies focusing on isolated mechanisms rather than providing an integrated view of how the HPV-mediated tumor immune microenvironment (TIME) impairs NK cell functions in cervical cancer.
Consensus and conclusions
In this review, we integrate HPV-driven immune modulation with TIME remodeling to examine how structural, cellular, molecular, and metabolic constraints may converge to restrict NK cell infiltration and function in cervical cancer, distinguishing cervical cancer-specific, HPV-associated, and cross-tumor evidence.
Controversies and gaps
Furthermore, we discuss emerging therapeutic strategies targeting these barriers and emphasize their potential as complementary approaches to existing immunotherapies, while noting that most remain preclinical or hypothesis-generating in cervical cancer.
Research and practice implications
This framework provides a unified perspective to guide the development of more effective NK cell–based immunotherapies in cervical cancer.

了解南亚年轻女性的宫颈筛查参与度:女性和医疗保健专业人员的观点。

Understanding Cervical Screening Engagement Among Young South Asian Women: Perspectives of Women and Healthcare Professionals.
作者： Katie Merry, Zainab Laheri, Sarah Johns, Yasemin Hirst, Rebecca Jefferson
摘要中文翻译背景:尽管 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 的使用率有所增加,但南亚年轻女性的宫颈筛查参与率有所下降,特别是在英格兰的贫困地区。造成这种模式的原因仍然知之甚少。本研究探讨了贫困地区 25-29 岁南亚女性宫颈筛查的障碍和促进因素。方法:与 Darwen 一起对布莱克本 25-29 岁的南亚女性 (n = 15) 和参与宫颈筛查的医疗保健专业人员 (n = 6) 进行半结构化访谈。使用反思性主题分析对数据进行分析。结果:筛查的障碍是多因素的,包括对宫颈筛查和 HPV 的了解有限、关于谦虚和性的文化规范、对社区判断和尴尬的恐惧,以及预约机会和竞争责任等结构性挑战。家庭网络既是障碍又是促进者。医疗保健专业人员描述了调整语言以减少恐惧,但强调了与人员配置和资金相关的服务压力,这限制了积极参与。尽管大多数女性报告接受了 HPV 疫苗接种,但对 HPV 和宫颈筛查的持续重要性的了解仍然有限,这凸显了加强宫颈筛查教育的机会。结论:南亚年轻女性的宫颈筛查参与度受到文化、情感和结构等交叉影响的影响,这种影响在贫困环境中可能会加剧。HPV 疫苗接种 应伴随着关于宫颈筛查的持续重要性的明确沟通,特别是在知识和意识仍然较低的情况下。需要采取文化敏感的沟通、灵活的服务提供和社区伙伴关系方法来解决筛查方面持续存在的不平等问题。患者或公众贡献:该研究是根据先前的社区参与和针对服务不足人群的公众参与工作而进行的。访谈指南是与南亚社区的公共顾问合作制定的,以确保它们具有相关性、包容性并反映社区的优先事项。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究探讨了贫困地区 25-29 岁南亚年轻女性宫颈筛查的障碍和促进因素,重点关注文化、情感和结构影响。
证据基础与方法
Darwen 与 Blackburn 的 15 名南亚女性和 6 名医疗保健专业人员进行了半结构化访谈,并使用反思性主题分析对数据进行了分析。
共识与结论
筛查的障碍包括对宫颈筛查和 HPV 的了解有限、文化规范、对社区判断的恐惧以及结构性挑战。家庭网络既是障碍又是促进者。大多数女性报告接受了 HPV 疫苗接种,但对 HPV 和宫颈筛查的持续重要性了解有限。
争议与缺口
该研究强调了筛查障碍的多因素性质,这种障碍在贫困环境中可能会加剧。然而,这些方法没有明确报告异质性或相互矛盾的发现。
科研与实践启示
HPV 疫苗接种 应伴随着关于宫颈筛查的持续重要性的明确沟通,特别是在知识和意识仍然较低的情况下。需要采取文化响应型沟通、灵活的服务提供和社区伙伴关系方法来解决筛查采用方面持续存在的不平等问题。

Understanding Cervical Screening Engagement Among Young South Asian Women: Perspectives of Women and Healthcare Professionals.

Authors: Katie Merry, Zainab Laheri, Sarah Johns, Yasemin Hirst, Rebecca Jefferson
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Despite increased uptake of the human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine, cervical screening participation among younger South Asian women has declined, particularly in deprived areas in England. The reasons for this pattern remain poorly understood. This study explored barriers and facilitators to cervical screening among South Asian women aged 25-29 in a deprived locality. METHODS: Semi-structured interviews were conducted with South Asian women aged 25-29 (n = 15) and healthcare professionals involved in cervical screening delivery (n = 6) in Blackburn with Darwen. Data were analysed using reflexive thematic analysis. RESULTS: Barriers to screening were multifactorial and included limited understanding of cervical screening and HPV, cultural norms surrounding modesty and sexuality, fear of community judgement and embarrassment, and structural challenges such as appointment access and competing responsibilities. Family networks acted as both barriers and facilitators. Healthcare professionals described adapting language to reduce fear, but highlighted service pressures related to staffing and funding, which constrained proactive engagement. Although most women reported receiving the HPV vaccination, understanding of HPV and the continued importance of cervical screening remained limited, highlighting opportunities to reinforce cervical screening education. CONCLUSION: Cervical screening engagement among young South Asian women is shaped by intersecting cultural, emotional, and structural influences, which may be intensified in deprived settings. HPV vaccination should be accompanied by clear communication about the continued importance of cervical screening, particularly where knowledge and awareness remain low. Culturally responsive communication, flexible service provision and community partnership approaches are needed to address persistent inequalities in screening uptake. PATIENT OR PUBLIC CONTRIBUTION: The study was informed by prior community engagement and public involvement work with underserved populations. Interview guides were developed in collaboration with a public advisor from the South Asian community to ensure they were relevant, inclusive, and reflective of community priorities.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study explored barriers and facilitators to cervical screening among young South Asian women aged 25-29 in a deprived locality, focusing on cultural, emotional, and structural influences.
Evidence base and method
Semi-structured interviews were conducted with 15 South Asian women and 6 healthcare professionals in Blackburn with Darwen, and data were analysed using reflexive thematic analysis.
Consensus and conclusions
Barriers to screening included limited understanding of cervical screening and HPV, cultural norms, fear of community judgement, and structural challenges. Family networks acted as both barriers and facilitators. Most women reported receiving the HPV vaccination but had limited understanding of HPV and the continued importance of cervical screening.
Controversies and gaps
The study highlighted the multifactorial nature of barriers to screening, which may be intensified in deprived settings. However, the methods did not explicitly report heterogeneity or conflicting findings.
Research and practice implications
HPV vaccination should be accompanied by clear communication about the continued importance of cervical screening, particularly where knowledge and awareness remain low. Culturally responsive communication, flexible service provision, and community partnership approaches are needed to address persistent inequalities in screening uptake.

智力障碍女孩和男孩 HPV 疫苗 的摄取:系统评价。

Uptake of the HPV vaccine in girls and boys with an intellectual disability: a systematic review.
作者： Peter Knapp, Mabel Redgrave, Ruksana Begum‐Meades, Umesh Chauhan, Ruksana Begum-Meades
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 宫颈癌 是第四种最常见的女性癌症,可通过 (人乳头瘤病毒) HPV 疫苗接种 进行预防,在大多数世界卫生组织国家,该病毒可用于青春期女孩和男孩。由于预防、筛查、早期 诊断 和 治疗 方面的缺陷,智力障碍人士的癌症结果明显比一般人群差,并且有人担心该人群中 HPV 疫苗接种 的感染率可能较低。目的是对智力障碍人群中 HPV 疫苗接种 的摄取及其对 疫苗接种 的影响进行系统回顾。方法 检索多个数据库(Medline;Embase;CINAHL;PsycINFO;Cochrane Central;Overton)至 2026 年 5 月。定量、定性和混合方法研究符合条件,如果:(i) 报告智力障碍人群中 HPV 疫苗接种 的摄取率,或 (ii) 报告影响智力障碍人群中 HPV 疫苗接种 的因素。两名评审员独立筛选研究并做出纳入决定。数据提取和质量评估(JBI 工具)由一名研究人员进行,并由另一名研究人员进行验证。酌情对 疫苗接种 启动率和完成率的数据进行叙述性综合和荟萃分析(风险比,RR);对 疫苗接种 的影响被映射到社会生态模型。结果 纳入来自八个高收入国家的 25 项研究(18 项定量;7 项定性)。荟萃分析显示,与无智力障碍的女孩和男孩相比,有智力障碍的女孩和男孩接受 疫苗(分别为 RR 0.74 和 0.56)并完成 疫苗 课程(分别为 RR 0.63 和 0.55)的可能性较小。报告的障碍主要是个人的,但也发生在人际、机构或社会层面。定量和定性研究总体上是高质量的。结论 与无智力障碍的女孩和男孩相比,患有智力障碍的女孩和男孩接受 HPV 疫苗 或一旦开始就完成 疫苗 课程的可能性较小,从而增加了癌症风险。已确定的障碍和促进因素表明医疗保健和教育部门有机会进行干预,以减少这种不平等。未来的研究需要来自更广泛的国家,包括来自智力障碍者本身的数据。注册 该协议于 2025 年 9 月在 OSF 注册:https://osf.io/7h8cz/overview

荟萃分析显示,与无智力障碍的女孩和男孩相比,有智力障碍的女孩和男孩接受 疫苗(分别为 RR 0.74 和 0.56)并完成 疫苗 课程(分别为 RR 0.63 和 0.55)的可能性较小。

Uptake of the HPV vaccine in girls and boys with an intellectual disability: a systematic review.

Authors: Peter Knapp, Mabel Redgrave, Ruksana Begum‐Meades, Umesh Chauhan, Ruksana Begum-Meades
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Cervical cancer is the fourth most common female cancer and is preventable by (human papillomavirus) HPV vaccination, given to adolescent girls and boys in most World Health Organisation countries. People with an intellectual disability have significantly worse cancer outcomes than the general population, attributable to deficits in prevention, screening, early diagnosis and treatment, and there is concern that rates of HPV vaccination may be lower in this population. The aim was to undertake a systematic review of research into HPV vaccination uptake in people with intellectual disability, and influences on vaccination. Methods Multiple databases (Medline; Embase; CINAHL; PsycINFO; Cochrane Central; Overton) were searched to May 2026. Quantitative, qualitative and mixed-methods studies were eligible if they: (i) reported rates of HPV vaccination uptake in people with an intellectual disability, or (ii) reported factors influencing HPV vaccination in people with an intellectual disability. Two reviewers independently screened studies and took inclusion decisions. Data extraction and quality appraisal (JBI tools) were undertaken by one researcher and verified by another. Data on vaccination initiation and completion rates were synthesised narratively and meta-analysed (risk ratio, RR) as appropriate; influences on vaccination were mapped to the Socio-Ecological Model. Results 25 studies (18 quantitative; 7 qualitative) from eight high-income countries were included. Meta-analyses showed that girls and boys with intellectual disability were less likely to receive the vaccine (RR 0.74 and 0.56, respectively) and complete a vaccine course (RR 0.63 and 0.55, respectively), compared to those without intellectual disability. Reported barriers were primarily individual but also occurred at interpersonal, institutional or societal levels. The quantitative and qualitative studies were generally of high quality. Conclusions Girls and boys with an intellectual disability are less likely than those without intellectual disability to receive HPV vaccines, or complete a vaccine course once started, increasing cancer risk. Identified barriers and facilitators indicate opportunities in the healthcare and education sectors to intervene to reduce this inequity. Future research is needed from a wider range of countries, including data from people with intellectual disability themselves. Registration The protocol was registered with the OSF in September 2025: https://osf.io/7h8cz/overview
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Registration The protocol was registered with the OSF in September 2025: https://osf.io/7h8cz/overview.
Evidence base and method
Methods Multiple databases (Medline; Embase; CINAHL; PsycINFO; Cochrane Central; Overton) were searched to May 2026.
Consensus and conclusions
Meta-analyses showed that girls and boys with intellectual disability were less likely to receive the vaccine (RR 0.74 and 0.56, respectively) and complete a vaccine course (RR 0.63 and 0.55, respectively), compared to those without intellectual disability.
Controversies and gaps
Identified barriers and facilitators indicate opportunities in the healthcare and education sectors to intervene to reduce this inequity.
Research and practice implications
Future research is needed from a wider range of countries, including data from people with intellectual disability themselves.

人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染:症状和预防 - Verywell Health

Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Infection: Symptoms and Prevention - Verywell Health
新闻简报人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是一种常见的性传播感染,影响美国 15-59 岁人群中约 40%。虽然许多感染不会引起症状,但 HPV 可导致生殖器疣、宫颈癌 和其他健康问题。建议女性进行常规巴氏涂片检查,并建议 9-26 岁的个人进行 HPV 疫苗 以预防这些并发症。
查看新闻五要素
时间
大多数 HPV 感染不会引起健康问题,并会在两年内自行消失。
地点与对象
在某些情况下,癌症是一种风险。
事件
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是一种常见的性传播感染 (STI),可能不会出现症状,但如果持续存在,可能会导致严重的健康问题。
规模、影响与风险
在美国,约 40% 的 15-59 岁人群感染 HPV。在某些情况下,它可能导致尖锐湿疣、癌症和其他健康问题。男性没有 HPV 常规筛查,但女性可以进行常规巴氏涂片检查以检查宫颈变化,包括早期 宫颈癌。建议 9 至 26 岁的人群接种 HPV 疫苗。
应对、状态与不确定性
建议进行常规筛查和 疫苗接种 以预防并发症。文章没有提供当前测试或隔离措施的具体细节。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Infection: Symptoms and Prevention - Verywell Health

News Brief
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a common STI affecting about 40% of people aged 15-59 in the United States. While many infections do not cause symptoms, HPV can lead to genital warts, cervical cancer, and other health issues. Routine Pap testing is recommended for women, and the HPV vaccine is advised for individuals aged 9-26 to prevent these complications.
Original source text (English)
Part 2 of 4 Understanding Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Infection Symptoms By Ann Pietrangelo Updated on November 25, 2026 Medically reviewed by Renee Nilan, MD Add Us On Table of Contents View All Table of Contents HPV Infection Symptoms Common Symptoms HPV and Cervical Cancer Other Complications Prevention Next in HPV Guide How Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Is Diagnosed IN THIS GUIDE HPV Overview Symptoms CURRENT ARTICLE Diagnosis Treatment Key Takeaways HPV is a common sexually transmitted infection (STI) affecting about 40% of people aged 15 to 59 in the United States. Most HPV infections cause no health issues and clear up on their own within two years. In some cases, cancer is a risk. There is an effective HPV vaccine recommended for people between the ages of 9 and 26. Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a common STI that may not show symptoms but can cause significant health problems if persistent. Learn about the symptoms and how to protect yourself. Mypurgatoryyears / Getty Images Understanding HPV Infection Symptoms HPV often goes unnoticed because it usually doesn’t cause symptoms. The immune system typically clears the virus without issues. HPV includes over 200 viruses, and symptoms, if they appear, can include warts that might itch or cause discomfort. 1 HPV can infect cells in the skin and moist linings of the body, including areas in and around the: 2 Vulva , vagina, cervix Anus Inner foreskin and urethra of the penis Inner lining of the nose, mouth, and throat Windpipe and bronchi Inner eyelids HPV spreads through sexual contact . There’s currently no routine screening for HPV in men since there’s no approved test. 3 Women can get routine Pap testing to check for cervical changes, including early cervical cancer . HPV testing can take place along with your Pap test. There’s also an effective HPV vaccine generally recommended for people between the ages of 9 and 26. 4 Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a sexually transmitted infection (STI). In the United States, HPV affects about 40% of people aged 15-59. 5 It’s so common that just about all sexually active people get it at some point. 6 In as many as nine cases out of 10, HPV doesn’t cause health issues. And it usually clears up on its own within two years. But in cases where it doesn’t, it can lead to genital warts, cancer, and other health problems. 7 Common Symptoms of HPV Infection The most common thing is to have no symptoms at all. Certain low-risk HPV infections can cause a variety of warts . 8 Viral warts are benign , but they can be a nuisance. Warts often resolve on their own within one to five years. 9 Genital Warts This photo contains content that some people may find graphic or disturbing. Reproduced with permission from © DermNet and © Te Whatu Ora dermnetnz.org 2023. Genital warts can be small or large and raised or flat. They can be dome-shaped or resemble small cauliflowers. Genital warts can appear on the: 10 Vulva Vagina Cervix Penis Scrotum Anus Genital warts sometimes cause itching. Depending on their location, they can cause pain when you move your bowels or when you have sex. 10 Common Warts This photo contains content that some people may find graphic or disturbing. Dmitry Epov / Getty Images Common warts (verruca vulgaris) grow on the fingers, around the nails, and on the backs of the hands. The bumps are rough and may have black dots that look like seeds. They can range from the size of a pinhead to the size of a pea. 11 Plantar Warts This photo contains content that some people may find graphic or disturbing. Alena Ivochkina / Getty Images Plantar warts appear on the soles of the feet. They’re hard and can grow rather large. They sometimes have black dots and appear in clusters (mosaic warts). Since feet carry the weight of your whole body, plantar warts push inward instead of growing outward like other types of warts, which can cause tenderness and pain. 12 Flat Warts This photo contains content that some people may find graphic or disturbing. Reproduced with permission from © DermNet dermnetnz.org 2023. Flat warts are smooth, slightly raised, and small, usually just millimeters wide. These warts can grow anywhere but tend to appear on the face, especially the forehead and cheeks. Men may get these in the beard area, and women may get them on their legs. Flat warts typically appear in multiples. You can get 20 to 100 at a time. 11 HPV Infections and Cervical Cancer Some low-risk strains of HPV may lead to abnormal changes in the cells on the surface of the cervix (cervical dysplasia) that are not precancerous. 13 However, research suggests that HPV causes about 99% of all cases of cervical cancer . 14 Cervical cancer happens when the immune system can’t clear high-risk strains of HPV. Over time, these cells can turn precancerous , then cancerous. Routine Pap screening can allow healthcare providers to detect and remove precancerous cells before they become cancerous. And HPV vaccines can prevent the types of HPV most likely to cause cervical cancer. 1 Other Possible HPV Complications HPV types 16 and 18 cause most cancers related to HPV, including cancer of the cervix, vagina, vulva, penis, and anus. Some strains can also lead to cancer of the throat, tongue, and tonsils ( oropharyngeal cancer ). 13 Research suggests that HPV causes about 75% of vaginal cancers , 69% of vulvar cancers , 70% of oropharyngeal cancers, and 91% of anal cancers . 15 HPV is also associated with some benign proliferative (rapid-growth) and malignant (cancerous) lesions of the head and neck, the middle part of the throat (oropharynx), tonsils, and tongue. 16 A Word From Verywell Given the safety and efficacy of the HPV vaccine, it is important to consider vaccinating your children to protect them from developing certain cancers later in life. — RENEE NILAN, MD, MEDICAL EXPERT BOARD How to Prevent an HPV Infection HPV can be transmitted through skin-to-skin sexual contact as well as vaginal, oral, or anal sex. 9 Since there are so many types of HPV, you can get infected multiple times or get more than one strain at a time. HPV vaccines are highly effective in protecting against seven types of HPV that are most likely to cause cancer and two low-risk types that cause most genital warts. 1 Since vaccines became available, infections with types that cause most HPV genital warts and cancers dropped 88% among teen girls and 81% among adult women. 17 The best time to get vaccinated is around age 11 to 12, but catch-up vaccination is recommended up to age 26. 17 However, if you’re older than 26 and haven’t been vaccinated, you can speak with your healthcare provider about the potential benefits of getting vaccinated now. HPV vaccination won’t treat an existing infection, but it can still protect you against other HPV types. It’s less effective than the vaccine, but you can lower your risk of getting HPV by using a condom or dental dam during vaginal, oral, and anal sex. Event
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a common sexually transmitted infection (STI) that may not show symptoms but can cause significant health problems if persistent.
Scale, impact and risk
HPV affects about 40% of people aged 15-59 in the United States. In some cases, it can lead to genital warts, cancer, and other health problems. There is no routine screening for HPV in men, but women can get routine Pap testing to check for cervical changes, including early cervical cancer. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

三分之一被推荐的伊朗女性 人乳头瘤病毒 检测呈阳性 - Bioengineer.org

One in Three Referred Iranian Women Tested Positive for Human Papillomavirus - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报对伊朗南部 8,209 名女性进行的一项回顾性研究发现,大约三分之一的女性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 检测呈阳性,其中近 29% 的 队列研究 检测出高危毒株。该研究采用了严格的统计方法,并评估了专门转诊分子实验室的医疗记录。然而,研究人群完全由医生推荐的女性组成,而不是一般社区的代表性群体。
查看新闻五要素
时间
An additional 6.9 percent of the evaluated individuals harbored concurrent high-risk and low-risk infections.
地点与对象
伊朗,伊朗南部,18岁及以上女性。
事件
对 8,209 名女性进行的回顾性横断面分析发现,大约三分之一的女性 人乳头瘤病毒 检测呈阳性,其中近 29% 的 队列研究 检测出高风险病毒株。
规模、影响与风险
34.2% 的女性 人乳头瘤病毒 检测呈阳性,总体样本中 28.8% 检测出高风险菌株。5.4% 的参与者发现了低风险菌株,6.9% 的参与者同时存在高风险和低风险感染。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究采用了严格的统计方法,并评估了专门转诊分子实验室的医疗记录。然而,研究人群完全由医生推荐的女性组成,而不是一般社区的代表性群体。

One in Three Referred Iranian Women Tested Positive for Human Papillomavirus - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A retrospective study of 8,209 women in southern Iran found that approximately one in three tested positive for human papillomavirus (HPV), with high-risk strains detected in nearly 29% of the cohort. The study utilized rigorous statistical methods and evaluated medical records from a specialized referral molecular laboratory. However, the study population consisted entirely of women referred by medical practitioners rather than a representative cross-section of the general community.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Saturday, November 28, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health One in Three Referred Iranian Women Tested Positive for Human Papillomavirus by November 25, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 4 mins read 0 One in Three Referred Iranian Women Tested Positive for Human Papillomavirus Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram A retrospective cross-sectional analysis of 8,209 women referred for molecular testing in southern Iran found that approximately one in three tested positive for human papillomavirus, with high-risk viral strains detected in nearly twenty-nine percent of the cohort. Overall human papillomavirus positivity reached 34.2 percent, accompanied by a 95 percent confidence interval spanning from 33.2 percent to 35.3 percent. Infections classified as high-risk only accounted for 21.8 percent of the patient population, while low-risk strains alone were identified in 5.4 percent of participants. An additional 6.9 percent of the evaluated individuals harbored concurrent high-risk and low-risk infections. When combining the single high-risk and mixed categories, any high-risk human papillomavirus genotype was detected in 28.8 percent of the overall sample, with a 95 percent confidence interval ranging between 27.8 percent and 29.8 percent. The investigation evaluated medical records extracted from patients’ files at a specialized referral molecular laboratory located in Shiraz. The analyzed cohort comprised women aged 18 years and older who had been referred by physicians between the Iranian calendar years 1399 and 1403, a period corresponding approximately to March 2020 through March 2025. Specific clinical indications for each patient’s referral were not documented in the laboratory database. Deoxyribonucleic acid was extracted from collected clinical samples, and human papillomavirus genotyping was performed using commercial testing kits that varied across the multi-year study period. Because laboratory protocols and diagnostic assays changed over time, the specific panel of reported genotypes differed between individual calendar years, reflecting evolving diagnostic platforms. Discover more infections Infections infection To evaluate viral patterns systematically, researchers classified identified genotypes according to the International Agency for Research on Cancer framework, designating viral types belonging to Groups 1, 2A, and 2B as high-risk human papillomavirus, while all remaining genotypes were categorized as low-risk. Each tested participant was assigned to exactly one of four mutually exclusive diagnostic categories: human papillomavirus-negative, high-risk only, low-risk only, or mixed high-risk and low-risk infection. Multiple infection was formally defined as the presence of two or more distinct genotypes within a single patient sample. The analytical protocol utilized Pearson chi-square tests and linear-by-linear association to assess statistical relationships with patient age, establishing a rigorous framework for evaluating age-specific viral dynamics across the referral cohort. Discover more Support Causes Book a Checkup Read Med Journals Find Cancer Care Among individual viral types detected across the patient cohort, the low-risk genotype HPV6 emerged as the most frequent, identified in 6.0 percent of the evaluated women. The prominent oncogenic type HPV16 followed closely as the second most common strain, detected in 5.7 percent of the tested population. Beyond these two leading genotypes, several high-risk types outside the classic HPV16 and HPV18 classifications exhibited substantial prevalence across the cohort. Specifically, the non-16 and non-18 high-risk genotypes HPV53, HPV51, and HPV39 were identified in 3.4 percent to 4.2 percent of the studied women, underscoring the notable circulation of diverse oncogenic strains within this specialized clinical referral setting. Multiple infections were highly prevalent among viral carriers, occurring in 42.5 percent of human papillomavirus-positive individuals, representing 1,193 of the 2,809 infected women. Genotype positivity demonstrated a statistically significant downward trajectory with advancing age, declining from 50.5 percent among women aged 25 years or younger to 28.3 percent among those aged 46 years or older, yielding a Pearson chi-square statistic of 125.4 with three degrees of freedom and a significance level of P < 0.001. Multiple genotype infections were likewise most frequent among the youngest demographic, detected in 54.0 percent of positive women aged 25 or younger. Carrying multiple genotypes was strongly associated with harboring at least one high-risk strain, exhibiting an odds ratio of 10.0 with a 95 percent confidence interval from 7.1 to 14.0, a pattern the researchers noted is partly expected because individuals carrying multiple strains have greater cumulative opportunity for a high-risk type to be detected. Discover more Study Biology Check Symptoms Explore History Researchers evaluated co-detection patterns across 418 possible pairwise combinations of high-risk and low-risk genotypes, finding that 227 unique pairs were co-detected at least once within the dataset. Using descriptive Fisher’s exact tests applied to two-by-two contingency tables alongside age-stratified exact testing and Holm adjustment, 21 specific pairs occurred more frequently than expected by chance, with 10 of those pairs involving HPV6. However, none of these combinations remained statistically significant when the analysis was restricted exclusively to human papillomavirus-positive women. The authors emphasized that these co-detection metrics remain descriptive and do not demonstrate biological interaction between viral types. Annual positivity rates fluctuated across calendar years, ranging from 29.2 percent to 55.7 percent during years with at least 400 tested women, although restricted statistical estimates focusing on the final Iranian calendar years 1402 and 1403 yielded results comparable to the primary full-cohort findings. The authors emphasized critical epidemiological boundaries when interpreting the retrospective findings, noting that the study population consisted entirely of women referred by medical practitioners rather than a representative cross-section of the general community. Furthermore, the dataset lacked accompanying cytological examinations or histological tissue assessments, measuring viral infection rather than clinical disease or cervical neoplasia. Consequently, the observed genotype frequencies and positivity rates cannot be generalized to the broader Iranian population or utilized to guide individual clinical risk stratification. Inhibiting CDK5 and HPV18 E6 Interaction Suppresses Cervical Cancer Cell Growth - Bioengineer.org
