DutchNews.nl - DutchNews.nl brings daily news from The Netherlands in English 28 November 2026 Newsletter Donate Advertise News Life in the Netherlands Jobs Partner content Podcast Advertise About us Search Home Economy Politics Art and culture Sport Europe Society Environment Health Housing Education News Home Economy Politics Art and culture Sport Europe Society Environment Health Housing Education Life in the Netherlands Latest Opinion Books Travel 10 Questions Learning Dutch Inburgering with DN Food & Drink Ask us anything Jobs Partner content Podcast Advertise About us Donate Team Advertise Contact us Writing for Dutch News Privacy Newsletter Search New HPV vaccine could save nearly 13,000 cancer diagnoses a year November 24, 2026 Photo: Depositphotos.com See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results See more DutchNews articles in your Google search results Add as a favourite source on Google Add DutchNews as a favourite source on Google Listen to this article 2 min Nearly 13,000 people could avoid a cancer diagnosis later in life if the Netherlands raises its HPV vaccination rate, new medical research has found. Children aged 10 are given two doses of the vaccine, which protects against a virus that causes cervical cancer in women. A new version introduced in September covers a wider range of cancers that affect both sexes, such as vaginal and penile cancers. Around 80% of people in the Netherlands contract the human papillomavirus, mainly though sexual activity. Most people who are infected have no symptoms, but persistent infection is responsible for around 99.7% of all cases of cervical cancer, one of the most common types in women. Every year 1,100 women and 400 men are diagnosed with a form cancer linked to HPV, most of whom are under the age of 45. Researchers at Erasmus Medical Centre in Rotterdam and the Dutch cancer centre IKNL said 12,800 diagnoses could be saved if the new vaccine is given to all children who reach the age of 10 in future. The Netherlands currently has a vaccination rate of 61% for the HPV vaccine, well below the World Health Organisation’s advisory rate of 90% for girls. Around 60% of boys have had the jab as well since the public health agency RIVM added it to their vaccine schedule in 2022. Gynaecologist and oncologist Luc van Lonkhuijsen, of Amsterdam’s university medical centre, said many parents were anxious about side effects or dismissed it as unimportant. “We have been vaccinating for 15 years and the number of side-effects we see is really very limited,” he told NOS. Original source text (English)
Votre navigateur n’est pas à jour et ne permet pas l’affichage du site. Veuillez le mettre à jour. Close EN FR EN FR EPI-PHARE Who are we? Our missions Governance Our academic partners Our work program Our publications News Thematic Files COVID-19 Pregnancy and Children EPI-PHARE Who are we? Our missions Governance Our academic partners Our work program Our publications News Thematic Files COVID-19 Pregnancy and Children EN FR AccueilRisk of Guillain-Barré syndrome following each dose of HPV vaccination ‌November 23rd, 2026 Risk of Guillain-Barré syndrome following each dose of HPV vaccination EPI-PHARE publishes an article in Vaccine Risk of Guillain-Barré syndrome following each dose of HPV vaccination: an 18-year nationwide self-controlled case series study Background Although the safety of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines has been largely established, the risk of Guillain-Barré syndrome (GBS) remains uncertain. This study aimed to assess the dose-specific risk of GBS following HPV vaccination, considering the whole population targeted by the national vaccination program in France. Methods Using data from the French National Health Data System (SNDS), a self-controlled case series (SCCS) study was conducted, including all individuals aged 10–29 years hospitalized with a diagnosis of GBS from 2007 to 2024. The relative incidence (RI) of GBS during the 42-day risk periods following each HPV vaccine dose compared to control periods was estimated, adjusting for other vaccinations, concomitant respiratory and gastrointestinal infections, age, and seasonality. The number of excess cases attributable to HPV vaccination was derived. Findings During the study period, 14.4 million (M) HPV vaccine doses (6.7 M first, 5.1 M second, 2.5 M third doses) were administered in a target population of 32.3 M individuals. Among 2604 GBS cases, 656 had received at least one dose of HPV vaccine, and 25 cases occurred within 42 days following HPV vaccination (9 after the first dose, 9 after the second, 7 after the third). No increase in GBS incidence was observed after the first vaccine dose (RI 1.29 [0.68–2.45]). In contrast, the incidence of GBS was increased after the second (RI 1.91 [1.01–3.60]) and third (RI 2.25 [1.06–4.79]) doses, a result consistent across sex, age subgroups and vaccine products. Accounting for the rarity of GBS, these relative increases resulted in 0.9 and 2.5 excess cases of GBS per million vaccinated individuals under a two-dose and three-dose schedule, respectively. Conclusion Although revealing a potentially heterogeneous risk profile across vaccine doses, this large study found no evidence of a substantial increase in the absolute risk of GBS following HPV vaccination, further confirming the safety of HPV vaccination. Article Dufour, L. et al. 医院研究中 JAM3 和 PAX1 基因甲基化追踪宫颈病变严重程度 - Bioengineer.org

JAM3 and PAX1 Gene Methylation Tracks Cervical Lesion Severity in Hospital Study - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报新疆医科大学附属肿瘤医院妇科肿瘤中心进行的一项医院研究发现,宿主基因JAM3和PAX1的DNA甲基化与高危人乳头瘤病毒(hrHPV)感染女性宫颈病变组织学严重程度的增加显着相关。该研究发表于 2026 年 11 月 26 日,评估了 205 名 25 岁或以上的 治疗 阴性女性,分为五个组织学组:正常组织、宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 1-3 级和侵袭性 宫颈癌。宫颈癌 的甲基化阳性率从正常病例的 20% 上升至 65.12%,其中 HPV16 是最常见的毒株。该研究强调了 JAM3/PAX1 甲基化作为改善 宫颈癌 检测风险分层的客观生物标志物的潜力,因为与组合甲基化检测相比,独立 HPV16/18 基因分型显示的诊断准确性有限。研究结果表明需要进一步研究将这些分子标记物整合到临床实践中,以获得更好的患者治疗结果。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于2024年9月至2025年12月期间进行,新闻于2026年11月26日发布。
地点与对象
该研究在新疆医科大学附属肿瘤医院妇科肿瘤中心进行,涉及 205 名 治疗 阴性、年龄 25 岁或以上且基线 高危型HPV 确诊为阳性的女性。
事件
一项以医院为基础的观察性研究发现,宿主 基因 JAM3 和 PAX1 的 DNA 甲基化与 高危型HPV 感染女性宫颈病变组织学严重程度的增加显着相关。Bioengineer.org 于 2026 年 11 月 26 日报道了该研究。
规模、影响与风险
该研究分析了 205 名女性,分为 5 个组织学组:5 例正常病例、51 例 CIN1、36 例 CIN2、70 例 CIN3 和 43 例侵袭性 宫颈癌 病例。甲基化阳性率随着病变严重程度的增加而增加,宫颈癌 病例中甲基化阳性率达到 65.12%。HPV16 是最常见的毒株,占 59.05%,其次是 HPV52 (14.29%)、HPV58 (13.33%) 和 HPV18 (6.67%)。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究是作为观察分析进行的,具有严格的纳入/排除标准。它使用多变量建模和统计检验(Cochran-Armitage 趋势检验、序数逻辑回归)来评估关联性。这些发现基于组织病理学诊断和甲基化测试,没有提及正在进行的公共卫生反应或结果的不确定性。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

JAM3 and PAX1 Gene Methylation Tracks Cervical Lesion Severity in Hospital Study - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A hospital-based study conducted at the Gynecologic Oncology Center of the Affiliated Cancer Hospital of Xinjiang Medical University found that DNA methylation of host genes JAM3 and PAX1 is significantly associated with increasing histological severity of cervical lesions among women with high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) infections. The research, published on November 26, 2026, evaluated 205 treatment-negative women aged 25 or older, classified into five histological groups: normal tissue, cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN) grades 1-3, and invasive cervical cancer. Methylation positivity rates rose from 20% in normal cases to 65.12% in cervical cancer, with HPV16 being the most prevalent strain. The study highlights the potential of JAM3/PAX1 methylation as an objective biomarker for improving risk stratification in cervical cancer detection, as standalone HPV16/18 genotyping showed limited diagnostic accuracy compared to combined methylation testing. The findings suggest a need for further research to integrate these molecular markers into clinical practice for better patient outcomes.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Saturday, November 28, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Cancer JAM3 and PAX1 Gene Methylation Tracks Cervical Lesion Severity in Hospital Study by November 26, 2026 in Cancer, Health Reading Time: 5 mins read 0 JAM3 and PAX1 Gene Methylation Tracks Cervical Lesion Severity in Hospital Study Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram A hospital-based investigation conducted at the Gynecologic Oncology Center of the Affiliated Cancer Hospital of Xinjiang Medical University has found that DNA methylation of host genes JAM3 and PAX1 is significantly associated with increasing histological severity of cervical lesions among women testing positive for high-risk human papillomavirus. Published in Frontiers in Oncology, the research examined cervical exfoliated cell samples to evaluate how epigenetic alterations correlate with disease stages ranging from normal tissue to invasive cervical cancer, while also assessing whether combining methylation testing with HPV16 and HPV18 genotyping could refine the detection of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or higher. Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus, or hrHPV, is established as the causative factor behind more than ninety percent of cervical cancer cases worldwide. Although primary screening programs relying on hrHPV testing provide substantial sensitivity for identifying viral presence, hrHPV positivity by itself fails to differentiate benign or transient infections from high-grade precancerous lesions, frequently precipitating unnecessary colposcopy referrals and repeat clinical follow-up visits. Liquid-based cytology is widely employed as a secondary triage method, but its reliance on morphological evaluation introduces challenges related to subjective interpretation and interobserver variability, driving the search for objective, reproducible molecular markers capable of improving risk stratification under international cervical cancer elimination initiatives. Discover more Hospital Check Symptoms Book a Checkup Host-cell DNA methylation has surfaced as a prominent molecular mechanism reflecting cellular transformation and progression toward malignancy. To rigorously investigate their clinical relevance, the research team designed a hospital-based observational study assessing treatment-negative women aged 25 years or older with confirmed baseline hrHPV positivity who presented with available JAM3 and PAX1 methylation measurements alongside definitive histopathological diagnoses. Discover more Order DNA Kits Study Biology Find Skin Care Find Cancer Care From an initial hospital cohort of 325 women evaluated between September 2024 and December 2025, investigators applied rigorous inclusion and exclusion criteria to isolate an untreated target population. Patients were excluded if they lacked definitive histopathological verification, had undergone a prior hysterectomy, or had received surgical or chemoradiotherapy interventions for cervical cancer before methylation testing. After removing 19 individuals without definitive histology, 4 with prior hysterectomies, and 97 who underwent testing only following cancer therapy, the primary analytical cohort comprised 205 treatment-negative women. Based on reference histopathology, the 205 participants were classified into five distinct groups: 5 normal cases, 51 cases of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 1, 36 cases of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2, 70 cases of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3, and 43 cases of invasive cervical cancer. Laboratory procedures combined commercial multiplex molecular diagnostics and standardized cytology systems. HPV testing identified 14 high-risk HPV types using PCR amplification and capillary electrophoresis fragment analysis, while liquid-based cytology was executed using the BD SurePath platform and evaluated according to the 2014 Bethesda System criteria by experienced cytologists working in a blinded manner. DNA methylation of PAX1 and JAM3 was quantified from exfoliated cervical cells using a commercial multiplex real-time PCR assay with fluorescent probes, utilizing GAPDH as an internal reference gene. Methylation positivity was defined by predefined cycle threshold differences, specifically a delta Ct of 6.6 or less for PAX1, a delta Ct of 10.0 or less for JAM3, or positivity in either gene to constitute an overall positive JAM3/PAX1 result. Across the entire cohort of 205 women, the overall positivity rate for combined JAM3/PAX1 methylation was 46.83 percent, representing 96 positive cases. Stratification across histological categories revealed a pronounced, statistically significant upward gradient: methylation positivity was recorded at 20.00 percent in the normal histopathology group, rising to 23.53 percent in CIN1, 33.33 percent in CIN2, 61.43 percent in CIN3, and reaching 65.12 percent among women diagnosed with cervical cancer. The Cochran-Armitage trend test confirmed this progressive escalation across advancing lesion severity with a chi-square value of 24.51 and a significance value of P less than 0.001. Age-stratified analyses among the 205 participants evaluated lesion severity and biomarker positivity across four distinct age bands: younger than 40 years, 40 to 49 years, 50 to 59 years, and 60 years or older. While overall methylation positivity rates showed statistically significant differences across these age brackets—registering at 32.73 percent, 50.85 percent, 58.06 percent, and 41.38 percent, respectively—the Cochran-Armitage test indicated no significant linear trend with advancing age. Histopathological grade distributions also varied significantly by age, reflecting distinct disease burdens within the hospital-based clinical sample. Discover more cancer Cancer See an OB-GYN Find Hospitals hospital To evaluate independent risk associations and diagnostic accuracy, the researchers conducted detailed multivariable modeling within a complete-data subset of 105 participants who possessed full records for HPV genotyping, cytology, methylation, and histopathology. Within this subset, 14 women had lesions classified as CIN1 or normal, 20 had CIN2, 53 had CIN3, and 18 had cervical cancer. Genotype analysis demonstrated that HPV16 was the most prevalent strain at 59.05 percent, followed by HPV52 at 14.29 percent, HPV58 at 13.33 percent, and HPV18 at 6.67 percent. HPV16/18 positivity rose across severity stages from 28.57 percent in the sub-CIN2 group to 77.78 percent in cervical cancer, displaying a significant linear trend. In the ordinal logistic regression model adjusted for concurrent diagnostic tests, JAM3/PAX1 methylation positivity was independently associated with higher cervical lesion grades, yielding an adjusted odds ratio of 5.02 with a 95 percent confidence interval of 2.15 to 11.77. HPV16/18 positivity was likewise independently associated with increased lesion severity, demonstrating an adjusted odds ratio of 3.88 and a 95 percent confidence interval of 1.75 to 8.60. Conversely, positive liquid-based cytology findings of atypical squamous cells of undetermined significance or higher showed no statistically significant independent association with histological severity in this adjusted model, displaying an odds ratio of 0.95 with a 95 percent confidence interval spanning 0.42 to 2.15. Diagnostic accuracy evaluations for detecting CIN2 or worse highlighted marked operational differences between standalone and combined testing modalities. Standalone HPV16/18 genotyping achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.692, with a sensitivity of 67.03 percent and a specificity of 71.43 percent. Standalone JAM3/PAX1 methylation produced an area under the curve of 0.676, with 63.74 percent sensitivity and 71.43 percent specificity. Combining both tests through a parallel testing strategy—where positivity for either marker defined a positive screen—boosted sensitivity to 86.81 percent, but reduced specificity to 50.00 percent, generating a test-positive rate of 81.90 percent. In contrast, sequential testing requiring positivity on both assays maximized specificity at 92.86 percent and achieved a positive predictive value of 97.56 percent, though sensitivity dropped to 43.96 percent. The authors emphasized several methodological constraints inherent to the study design. Because the investigation was performed within a single tertiary cancer hospital, the enrolled cohort was clinically enriched with high-grade lesions and cancer cases, limiting direct extrapolation to unselected primary screening populations. Furthermore, the complete diagnostic subset contained 91 cases of CIN2+ and only 14 cases below CIN2, an imbalance that heavily influences specificity and predictive value metrics, particularly the elevated positive predictive value seen in sequential testing. Finally, because the investigation utilized a cross-sectional assessment of existing lesions rather than longitudinal follow-up, the observed associations do not demonstrate an ability to predict prospective disease progression, underscoring the need for large multicenter prospective trials to clarify the clinical triage utility of JAM3 and PAX1 methylation. Original title (Chinese)
HPV疫苗系列科普(十一)| HPV疫苗≠只防宫颈癌!这些疾病也能抵挡
News Brief
The supplied evidence does not clearly report the event time in verified English. The Estimated Lifetime Probability of Acquiring Human Papilonmavirus in the United Dtetes[J]. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the central event in verified English. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the scale, impact, or risk in verified English. The supplied source does not clearly report the response status or unresolved information.
Original source text (Chinese)
新闻 体育 汽车 房产 旅游 教育 时尚 科技 财经 娱乐 HPV疫苗系列科普(十一)| HPV疫苗≠只防宫颈癌!这些疾病也能抵挡 2026-11-24 16:36 来源: 中部城市生活指南 发布于:北京市 提起HPV疫苗,很多人的第一印象就是“宫颈癌疫苗”。不少人认为,它的作用仅仅是保护宫颈。事实上,HPV病毒不只是会感染宫颈,还可以感染外阴、阴道、肛门、口腔、咽喉、阴茎等多处黏膜皮肤,持续感染后可能诱发多种病变和癌症。HPV疫苗的防护范围远比大家想象的更广,是一种面向男女都有健康收益的疫苗。 HPV是什么? 人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是一类主要感染人体皮肤和黏膜上皮的球形DNA病毒,大约有200多种亚型。大多数HPV感染是无症状的,可自行消除,但持续感染HPV可能导致疾病发生。研究显示,在至少有一名异性伴侣的人群中,女性一生中感染HPV的概率为84.6%,男性为91.3%[1]。 HPV的致病性 HPV依据其致癌潜力可分为高危型和低危型两大类。 在高危型HPV中, 16型和18型是致癌能力最强的两个亚型,大约造成全球70%的宫颈癌,同时还可能诱发阴道癌、肛门癌、阴茎癌等多种恶性肿瘤。 低危型HPV通常引起良性病变,常见的型别有6型和11型,占生殖器疣病因的90%[2]。 HPV疫苗分为哪几种? 可以预防哪些疾病? 二价HPV疫苗(16、18型): 可预防约70%宫颈癌,也可预防由16、18型 HPV病毒导致的外阴癌、阴道癌、肛门癌、口咽癌、阴茎癌以及对应的癌前病变。 四价HPV疫苗(6、11、16、18型): 可预防约70%宫颈癌+90%生殖器疣。拥有二价全部防护能力,额外预防尖锐湿疣、复发性呼吸道乳头状瘤病。 九价HPV疫苗(6、11、16、18、31、33、45、52、58型): 可预防约90%宫颈癌+90%生殖器疣。覆盖范围最广,在四价HPV疫苗的基础上增加5种高危亚型,能预防更多HPV相关恶性肿瘤。 避开常见误区 错误 HPV只通过性接触传播。 正确 性接触传播是最常见的传播途径;也可通过密切接触,如皮肤直接接触被病毒污染的物品或共用毛巾、浴巾、浴缸等感染;还存在母婴传播,如分娩经产道时传染给婴儿。 展开全文 错误 接种HPV疫苗会影响怀孕。 正确 HPV疫苗是重组蛋白疫苗,不含活病毒,不会损伤卵巢和输卵管,不会降低怀孕概率。 错误 已经有性生活/已经查出HPV阳性就不能接种HPV疫苗。 正确 可以接种,对还没有感染的亚型依然有保护作用。 错误 接种完疫苗就不用再做宫颈癌筛查。 正确 疫苗不能覆盖全部致癌HPV亚型:无论接种几价疫苗,女性到适龄之后都要定期做宫颈癌TCT(液基薄层细胞学检测)/HPV筛查。 错误 只有女性需要接种HPV疫苗。 正确 肛门癌、口咽癌、阴茎癌、尖锐湿疣等疾病男性同样会中招,男性接种HPV疫苗既能保护自己,也能减少病毒传给伴侣的概率。 HPV疫苗不只是“宫颈癌专属疫苗”,它可以预防HPV病毒家族带来的多种疾病。比起盲目观望等待,尽早接种才是最重要的原则。结合自身需求选择合适的疫苗,再配合定期筛查,才能建立起完整可靠的健康保护屏障。 参考文献: [1] Chesson H W, Dunne E F, Hariri S, et al. The Estimated Lifetime Probability of Acquiring Human Papilonmavirus in the United Dtetes[J]. Sexually Transmitted Diseases, 2014, 41(11): 660-664. [2] 中华预防医学会,中华预防医学会疫苗与免疫分会. 子宫颈癌等人乳头瘤病毒相关疾病免疫预防专家共识(2025年版)[J]. 中国预防医学杂志, 2025, 26(7): 775-812.返回搜狐,查看更多 阅读 () 平台声明 该文观点仅代表作者本人,搜狐号系信息发布平台,搜狐仅提供信息存储空间服务。

11月25日,北京佑安医院HPV(人乳头瘤病毒)感染防控与精准诊疗中心启用。该中心整合多学科资源,构建集筛查、诊断、治疗、随访、疫苗接种于一体的一站式诊疗平台,助力区域HPV综合防治能力升级。佑安医院感染免疫科主任张彤介绍,此前,该院HPV相关病变的诊疗资源分散在妇产科、感染中心、皮肤科、外科、病理科等多个科室 ...
